### [1] [Multi-Scale Latent Temporal Compression Attention and Video-Text Cross Attention Fusion for Video Action Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29176)
*Yicheng Qiu,Keiji Yanai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出多尺度潜在时序压缩注意力（MSLTCA）视频时序注意力模块与视频-文本交叉注意力融合（VTCAF）模块，以缓解帧冗余、全局/局部特征失衡及视觉-文本融合不足问题，在多个视频识别基准上取得优于现有方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频动作检测需建模连续视频流中的时序依赖。现有深度神经网络方法虽能完成帧级视频识别，但难以建模动态时序模式，存在三类问题：(1) 帧信息冗余；(2) 全局与局部特征难以平衡；(3) 视觉与文本特征融合不充分。

Method: 提出基于多尺度潜在时序压缩注意力的视频时序注意力模块：将连续时序序列聚合为多时间尺度上的特征表示，同时高效降低冗余；并提出视频-文本交叉注意力融合模块，在潜在特征层与标准特征层两个层级使用交叉注意力，实现视频与文本特征的细粒度融合，增强语义对齐与特征交互。

Result: 在多个视频识别基准上的实验结果表明，所提方法性能优于现有方法，验证了其有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 多尺度时序压缩注意力与双层级交叉注意力融合相结合，可有效改善时序建模与跨模态对齐，为视频动作检测提供有效且具泛化性的方案。

Abstract: In video action detection, modeling the temporal dependencies in continuous video streams is crucial. Previous methods based on deep neural networks can perform frame-level video recognition tasks. However, these methods struggle to model dynamic temporal patterns, facing challenges such as redundant frame information, difficulty balancing global and local features, and insufficient fusion of visual and textual features. To address these issues, we propose a video temporal attention module based on the Multi-Scale Latent Temporal Compression Attention, which aggregates continuous temporal sequences into feature representations across diverse temporal scales while efficiently reducing redundancy. We also introduce a Video-Text Cross Attention Fusion module that leverages cross-attention at both latent and standard feature levels, achieving fine-grained fusion between video and textual features to enhance semantic alignment and feature interaction. Experimental results across multiple video recognition benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed methods achieve superior performance compared to existing methods, validating the effectiveness and generalization capability.

</details>


### [2] [OACap: Caption-Guided Active Observation for Detailed Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29327)
*Hao Shi,Hong Yan,Xiaoyang Zhang,Qing Cui,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以字幕为中心的智能体 OACap（Observation-Adaptive Video Captioner），将缺失或无支撑信息转化为定向观察请求并用时空工具取证、迭代校验修订字幕；同时构建 FSCap-Bench 及 CSFS、CSRS 两项指标诊断字幕的事实性与语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式的详细视频字幕生成不同于查询明确感知目标的问答任务，需要自行决定获取哪些证据以及如何将其整合为连贯的全局描述；而现有评测缺乏对字幕所保留信息的细粒度诊断手段。

Method: OACap：以字幕为中心的智能体，将无支撑或缺失的信息转化为针对性观察请求，调用时空工具获取证据，并迭代验证与修订字幕。评测方面提出 FSCap-Bench：Core-Support Factuality Score（CSFS）结合断言精确率与重要性加权的关键事实覆盖率；Cross-Modal Semantic Round-Trip Score（CSRS）通过 video-caption-video-caption 往返流程匹配源字幕与恢复字幕中的原子事实来衡量语义保持。

Result: 在固定纯文本 reader 条件下，OACap 生成的字幕在多个通用视频理解基准上取得一致性能提升；CSFS 在 93.0–98.3% 的有效字幕对上对四类受控扰动类型表现出符合预期的响应。

Conclusion: 两项指标共同将下游文本记忆效用与事实性和语义保持性联系起来，为开放式视频字幕提供更可诊断的评测框架。

Abstract: Unlike question answering, where a query specifies a perceptual target, open-ended detailed captioning must determine what evidence to acquire and how to integrate it into a coherent global description. We propose Observation-Adaptive Video Captioner (OACap), a caption-centered agent that turns unsupported or missing information into targeted observation requests, uses spatiotemporal tools to acquire evidence, and iteratively verifies and revises the caption. OACap captions consistently improve performance across general video-understanding benchmarks using a fixed text-only reader. To better diagnose the information they preserve, we introduce Factual-Semantic Captioning Bench (FSCap-Bench). Its Core-Support Factuality Score (CSFS) combines claim precision with importance-aware fact coverage and responds as expected to four controlled perturbation types on 93.0-98.3% of valid caption pairs. Its Cross-Modal Semantic Round-Trip Score (CSRS) measures semantic preservation through a video-caption-video-caption round trip by matching atomic facts from source and recovered captions. Together, they connect downstream textual-memory utility with factual and semantic preservation.

</details>


### [3] [Different Defects, Different Effects: A Controlled Audit of Face Recognition Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28766)
*Ana Dias,João Ribeiro Pinto,Hugo Proença,João C. Neves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统审计MS1MV3中残留的训练数据缺陷，提出五类缺陷识别策略，并在IResNet-50+ArcFace受控协议下评估修复效果，发现数据清洗的收益高度依赖缺陷类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MS-Celeb-1M经MS1MV2/MS1MV3等精炼版本清洗，大规模人脸数据集仍存在标注错误、冗余样本与数据不一致，而训练数据质量这一因素相对缺乏系统研究。

Method: 提出互补的数据审计策略，在MS1MV3上识别五类缺陷：精确与近似重复图像、身份切分（split identity）、裁剪错误、多人脸歧义、无效内容；随后在基于IResNet-50与ArcFace的受控训练协议下，对比修复前后的识别性能与收敛所需优化步数。

Result: 针对性修复可保持甚至在部分情形下提升识别性能，同时以更少优化步数达到原始训练集的性能水平；去重带来最显著的整体收益，切分身份合并与无效内容移除亦提升训练效率。

Conclusion: 数据清洗的效果强烈依赖于所处理的缺陷类型，训练数据质量在大规模人脸识别中仍是重要且相对欠探索的因素；训练清单与代码将在录用后公开。

Abstract: The development of face recognition has been strongly supported by the availability of large-scale training datasets. Among them, MS-Celeb-1M has played an important role by providing millions of facial images from nearly 100K identities. However, the size and heterogeneity of such datasets increase the likelihood of annotation errors, redundant samples, and other forms of data inconsistency. This motivated the introduction of refined versions, such as MS1MV2 and MS1MV3, aimed at improving the quality of the training data. Despite these cleaning efforts, we show that several types of training-data defects remain in MS1MV3. By proposing complementary data-auditing strategies, we identify five defect categories: exact and near-duplicate images, split identities, crop errors, multiple-face ambiguity, and invalid content. We then assess the effect of correcting these defects under a controlled face-recognition training protocol based on IResNet-50 and ArcFace. The results show that targeted interventions can preserve, and in some cases improve, recognition performance while requiring fewer optimization steps to reach the performance of the original training set. Duplicate removal provides the clearest overall benefit, while split-identity merging and invalid-content removal also improve training efficiency. Overall, the results indicate that the effect of dataset cleaning depends strongly on the type of defect being addressed and provide further evidence that training-data quality remains an important and comparatively underexplored factor in large-scale face recognition. The training manifests and code used in this work will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [4] [Complexity-Aware Surgical Video Reasoning Segmentation with Digital Twin and Context Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28776)
*Yiqing Shen,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SurgReasonSeg，一种复杂度感知的手术视频推理分割方法：基于手术基础模型构建数字孪生表示，用RL训练两个LoRA适配器并按查询复杂度路由，辅以自适应时间窗口与手术上下文记忆检索；同时发布EndoVis-ReasonSeg基准，J&F较最强基线提升0.178/0.237/0.297，平均18.8 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手术分割依赖闭集类别或显式文本查询，无法处理需要对工作流程、器械功能与时间关系进行推理的隐式查询。

Method: 1) 由手术基础模型构建数字孪生(DT)表示；2) 用强化学习训练同一LLM的两个LoRA适配器，分别针对不同查询复杂度；3) 用RL训练的轻量路由器在延迟与精度间权衡选择适配器；4) 依据查询复杂度与工作流阶段设定自适应滑动窗口的时间上下文；5) 按工作流相似度与流程相关性从手术上下文记忆检索历史病例；6) 构建EndoVis-ReasonSeg基准（1,460训练/961测试，涵盖语义、空间、时间三类）。

Result: 在EndoVis-ReasonSeg上，J&F分别较最强基线ReSurgSAM2在语义、空间、时间查询上提升0.178、0.237、0.297；在Ref-EndoVis17与Ref-EndoVis18指代分割基准上亦取得最佳结果；采用骨干匹配的归因协议区分上游分割器与推理模块的贡献；平均18.8 FPS，支持实时推理分割。

Conclusion: 将推理分割引入手术视频DT表示，并以复杂度感知的路由、时间建模与上下文检索实现精度—延迟平衡，可在实时条件下完成复杂隐式查询的手术分割。

Abstract: Conventional surgical segmentation relies on closed-set categories or explicit text queries, which cannot resolve implicit queries that require reasoning about workflows, instrument functions, and temporal relationships. To address these limitations, we propose SurgReasonSeg, a complexity-aware method that brings reasoning segmentation over digital twin (DT) representations to surgical video. Specifically, SurgReasonSeg constructs DT representations from surgical foundation models and trains two low-rank adapters (LoRA) of one large language model with reinforcement learning (RL), each specialized for a different query complexity. A lightweight router, also trained with RL, selects between the two adapters to balance latency against accuracy. SurgReasonSeg also sets the temporal context with an adaptive sliding window conditioned on query complexity and workflow phase, and retrieves relevant historical cases from a surgical context memory by workflow similarity and procedural relevance. We further introduce EndoVis-ReasonSeg, a surgical reasoning segmentation benchmark with 1,460 training and 961 test samples across semantic, spatial, and temporal categories. On EndoVis-ReasonSeg, SurgReasonSeg improves J&F over the strongest baseline, ReSurgSAM2, by 0.178, 0.237, and 0.297 on semantic, spatial, and temporal queries, and it also achieves the best results on the Ref-EndoVis17 and Ref-EndoVis18 referring segmentation benchmarks. A backbone-matched attribution protocol separates the contribution of the upstream segmenter from that of the reasoning modules. Our method runs at 18.8 FPS averaged over the query distribution, enabling real-time reasoning segmentation in surgical videos.

</details>


### [5] [JUICE: Unified Multimodal Modeling in Pretrained Representation Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28801)
*Jiahan Li,Huangyuan Su,Shengbang Tong,Shusheng Yang,Ruihan Guo,Suozhi Huang,Hexu Zhao,Pratyusha Sharma,Sherry Yang,Jiatao Gu,Rob Fergus,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JUICE：在冻结的图文连续编码空间中，用单一流匹配Transformer从零联合训练生成文本与图像，实现无固定顺序、无模态主导的统一多模态建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型存在不对称性：用预训练语言模型生成文本、外接扩散头生成图像，且多模态输出按固定顺序产生，导致前一输出无法根据后一输出调整。作者希望两种模态由同一过程联合训练生成。

Method: 在预训练编码器（语义空间）的连续表征中直接建模联合分布；采用单一流匹配Transformer，图像与文本各设独立噪声水平；通过指定哪些模态为干净、哪些为生成，切换图像生成、图像描述、VQA、联合生成等任务；控制变量预训练逐项验证设计选择。

Result: 同等引导下，连续状态生成文本不损害图像生成（GenEval 48.41，对比纯T2I模型47.86），但描述在精确措辞上有所损失；联合与条件去噪联合训练优于单独训练；语义编码空间优于VAE潜变量与原始像素；2.1B参数、224×224、无语言模型初始化下，GenEval达0.88，VQAv2达73.1；耦合采样可在写步骤的同时绘制地图路线，多解迷宫联合成功率由独立采样的54.6%升至93.7%；视觉空间规划在其训练规模地图上求解率达99.8%。

Conclusion: 证实可在连续编码空间中由单一流匹配过程从零联合生成图文，无需模态先后顺序，联合分布采样显著提升需要跨模态一致性的任务表现。

Abstract: Most unified multimodal models treat images and text asymmetrically: a pretrained language model generates text, an attached diffusion head generates images, and outputs that contain both are produced in a fixed order, so the first output cannot adapt to the second. We ask whether both modalities can instead be generated by one process that is trained on them jointly from scratch. Pretrained encoder spaces hold both modalities in one continuous form and carry knowledge learned at scale, and recent work finds them effective as generative targets for vision and language. We therefore model the joint distribution directly in these spaces. We introduce JUICE (Joint Unified multimodal modeling In Continuous Encoder spaces), a single flow matching Transformer over frozen image and text representations, in which each modality has its own noise level. Which modalities are clean and which are generated then decides the task: image generation, captioning, visual question answering, or joint generation. Controlled pretraining runs test each design choice. At equal guidance, generating text as continuous states leaves image generation intact (48.41 GenEval, against 47.86 for a model trained only for text-to-image generation), while captions lose some exact wording. Training on joint and conditional denoising together beats either alone, and semantic encoder spaces beat VAE latents and raw pixels as targets. At 2.1B parameters, with no language model initialization and at 224x224, JUICE reaches up to 0.88 on GenEval and 73.1 on VQAv2. By sampling both modalities from the joint distribution (coupled sampling), it can also draw a route on a map while writing out the matching moves. On mazes with several valid solutions, joint success rises from 54.6% with independent sampling to 93.7%, and no modality has to go first. On visual spatial planning, JUICE solves 99.8% of maps at its training sizes.

</details>


### [6] [SAIL: A Learnable Generalisation of Spatial Autocorrelation over Graph-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28806)
*Piotr Keller,Fayyaz Minhas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAIL框架，将经典空间自相关统计量推广为可微分、可学习的算子，通过联合学习特征投影与节点间邻域亲和度，实现高维图数据的空间自相关建模、预测与发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典空间自相关统计量（Moran's I、Geary's C）仅作用于单一标量变量且邻域权重固定，难以适用于高维、类图结构数据，因此需要一个可学习、可微的推广框架。

Method: SAIL联合学习两组参数：定义候选标量场的特征投影，以及节点间邻域亲和度，从而将空间自相关泛化为可微算子；输出可解释的局部与全局空间一致性描述子，支持有监督预测与无监督的自相关模式/热点发现。

Result: 理论上证明在适当参数化下SAIL可精确还原经典空间统计量，且对由潜在空间自相关驱动的任务可逼近贝叶斯最优预测器；合成数据与大规模生物医学图实验验证了统计正确性与空间一致性发现能力，覆盖视觉、地理空间分析与生物医学应用。

Conclusion: SAIL为高维图数据的空间自相关提供了统一、可解释且可端到端学习的方法，兼具理论保证与跨领域实证效果，代码已开源。

Abstract: In vision and scientific domains involving spatial data, a key question is whether node feature values vary independently across space or form coherent spatial structures. Classical spatial autocorrelation statistics such as Moran's $I$ and Geary's $C$ quantify this phenomenon but operate on a single scalar variable with fixed neighbourhood weights, limiting their use for high-dimensional graph-like data. We introduce SAIL (Spatial Autocorrelation Index Learner), a differentiable framework that generalises spatial autocorrelation into a learnable operator by jointly learning feature projections defining candidate scalar fields and inter-node neighbourhood affinities. SAIL produces interpretable local and global spatial coherence descriptors, enabling supervised prediction and unsupervised discovery of spatial autocorrelation modes and hotspots. We show that SAIL exactly recovers classical spatial statistics under appropriate parametrisations and, for tasks driven by latent spatial autocorrelation, can approximate the Bayes-optimal predictor with an inductive bias matched to such tasks. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale biomedical graphs demonstrate statistical correctness and discovery of spatial coherence with applications across vision, geospatial analysis, and biomedicine. The code repository is available at: https://github.com/pkeller00/SAIL.

</details>


### [7] [Register Anything Model for Generalizable and Robust Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28821)
*Zheng Qin,Junhua Xi,Yan Huang,Jiahong Lai,Anwen Huang,Qiong Li,Guangda Zhang,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一模型RAM，通过几何感知归一化与层次化几何Transformer，实现跨传感器、跨场景的多域可泛化3D点云配准，在12个基准上达SOTA并优于单域训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有配准方法通常针对单一域定制网络结构与超参数，迁移到新域时性能显著退化；而跨室内、室外、物体等域统一建模的核心障碍是严重的几何差异（场景尺度、传感器噪声、点密度、结构模式），使统一的基于点的模型难以处理。

Method: 1) 在多域混合数据上训练单一模型；2) 提出几何感知归一化策略，联合体素化与归一化，依据点云几何属性自适应地将点云映射到标准化几何空间，在统一分辨率的同时保留显著局部结构；3) 基于该归一化构建层次化由粗到细配准范式，并采用高效几何Transformer，仅建模局部几何一致性而非全局所有点，以缓解体素化离散噪声并提升计算效率。

Result: 在12个基准上，内点率（inlier ratio）与配准召回率（registration recall）均取得SOTA；且优于在各单域分别训练的对比方法，展现更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 统一多域训练配合几何感知归一化与局部几何建模，可有效克服跨域几何差异，实现强泛化的6-DoF点云配准，为通用配准模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present Register Anything Model (RAM), a unified model for generalizable 3D point cloud registration that robustly estimates 6-DoF transformations across diverse domains spanning sensors and environments. Existing registration methods are typically tailored to a single domain, with carefully tuned architectures and hyperparameters, and thus degrade markedly when applied to novel domains. We bridge this gap by training a single model on diverse data from indoor, outdoor, and object domains. A central challenge is the severe geometric discrepancy across domains, i.e., differences in scene scale, sensor noise, point density, and structural patterns, which hinders processing with a unified point-based model. To address this, we introduce a geometry-aware normalization strategy that jointly voxelizes and normalizes point clouds into a standardized geometric space, adaptively based on their geometric properties to preserve salient local structures while standardizing resolution. Built on this normalization, RAM follows a hierarchical coarse-to-fine paradigm, and employs an efficient geometric transformer that models local geometric consistency instead of globally over all points. This design mitigates discretization noise introduced by voxelization, and improves computational efficiency. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in terms of inlier ratio and registration recall. Notably, our method outperforms the counterparts trained on each separate domain, showing superior generalization capability.

</details>


### [8] [WorldStream: Streaming Interactive 3D Worlds with Hierarchical Planning and Persistent State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28827)
*Xiaoxiao Ma,Weiyang Jin,Yanwen Ma,Haoran Li,Zhixiang Wei,Songchun Zhang,Yaowei Li,Junhao Zhuang,Zeyue Xue,Yuming Li,Zheming Fu,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldStream 是一个开放式 3D 世界模型，可无限扩展可玩地形、响应开放式语言指令，并能还原带编辑的已访问区域；其核心是分层规划（Macro/Micro）、确定性局部代码编译、流式分离与单一世界记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 世界模型难以同时做到地图无限外延、语言可交互操作以及对已访问区域的编辑持久化。论文希望在同一系统中兼顾世界扩展、跨瓦片一致性与交互性，并在有限预算下运行。

Method: 提出类型化决策模型上的分层规划器：Macro 在一组瓦片上确定聚落、生态、交通与水文结构，Micro 在实测地形与已接受的邻接承诺约束下将契约细化为瓦片语义。由确定性局部代码把已接受计划编译为地形、道路、聚落、植被与实体。采用流式机制分离各阶段作用范围：规划沿预测走廊前瞻运行，仅对即将需要的区域编译，用户身后几何被释放。单一世界记录保存计划、种子、对象身份与可变状态、边界承诺及按属性记录的编辑，随用户推进与编辑更新，返回瓦片据此重新编译。该记录同时支撑交互：作用于既有实体（含随行为更新的有状态 NPC）、编辑当前区域，或为未规划区域附带意图。

Result: 得到的世界可在有界预算下持续扩展，在独立规划的各瓦片间保持连接一致，并支持交互操作。

Conclusion: 通过分层规划、确定性编译、流式调度与统一世界记录的组合，WorldStream 实现了可扩展、连通且可交互的开放式 3D 世界生成。

Abstract: We present WorldStream, an open-ended 3D world model with three simultaneous capabilities: it extends playable terrain beyond any pre-specified map, acts on open-ended language in the running world, and restores visited places with their edits. A hierarchical planner over a typed decision model emits structured decision state: Macro fixes settlement, ecology, transport, and hydrological structure across a group of tiles; Micro specializes that contract into tile semantics under measured terrain and accepted neighbor commitments. Deterministic local code compiles accepted plans into terrain, roads, settlements, vegetation, and entities. Streaming separates their reach: plans run ahead along the predicted corridor, compilation only where movement will soon need it, geometry behind the user released. One world record retains plans, seeds, object identities and mutable state, boundary commitments, and edits by attribute, revised as the user advances and edits; returning tiles recompile from it. The same record grounds interaction: the model acts on existing entities, including stateful NPCs that update as they act, edits the present region, or attaches intent to unplanned regions. Together they yield a world expanding under bounded budgets, connected across independently planned tiles, and interactive.

</details>


### [9] [HorizonComposer: Spatiotemporally Consistent Driving Video Editing with Enriched Traffic Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28829)
*Mauricio Soroco,Yuqiu Liu,Zaid Tasneem,Francesco Pittaluga,Abhishek Aich,Wuyang Chen,Manmohan Chandraker,Ziyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向驾驶场景指令引导视频编辑的交通语义保护方法，从数据构建、训练奖励和时序过渡三层面抑制结构幻觉与"时序冲击"，在时空一致性与下游任务上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用指令引导视频编辑器在驾驶场景的全局条件编辑中频繁破坏严格交通语义，产生结构幻觉与"时序冲击"，不利于构建可信的自动驾驶压力测试仿真。

Method: 三层设计：(1) 构建混合数据集，将时间对齐的真实视频与合成编辑结合，平衡基础场景结构与明确外观信号；(2) 训练时引入离线强化学习中的优势条件化（advantage conditioning），从混合数据提取像素级稠密奖励作为局部信号，引导生成偏向结构可靠的像素；(3) 引入"思考帧"（thinking frames）过渡机制，衔接静态图像条件与动态视频上下文以缓解时序冲击。

Result: 避免多主体动态被破坏，实现最优时空一致性；用户偏好上内容保持相对提升39%、时序一致性提升12%；下游道路线分割提升5%，多目标检测提升7%。

Conclusion: 通过在数据、训练信号与时间过渡三个层面蒸馏并保护交通语义，可有效提升指令引导驾驶视频编辑的结构可靠性与时空一致性，并带来下游感知任务的实质收益。

Abstract: Instruction-guided video generative models offer a scalable solution for stress-testing autonomous driving systems by simulating diverse environmental conditions. However, driving scenes operate within a dense Operational Design Domain (ODD) governed by strict traffic semantics. When applied to global driving-condition edits, general-purpose instruction-guided video editors frequently corrupt these semantics, leading to structural hallucinations and ``temporal shock.'' This work distills and protects traffic semantics during video editing at three levels. First, we construct a hybrid dataset by combining temporally aligned real-world videos with synthetic edits, balancing foundational scene structure with unambiguous appearance signals. Second, during training, we adapt advantage conditioning from offline reinforcement learning to align the model using dense rewards. We extract dense, pixel-level rewards from the hybrid data to serve as a localized signal and actively steer generation toward structurally reliable pixels. Finally, our model mitigates temporal shock by introducing thinking frames, a transition mechanism that seamlessly reconciles static image conditioning with dynamic video context. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method prevents the corruption of multi-actor dynamics, establishing state-of-the-art spatiotemporal consistency, yielding consistent user preference (39% relative improvement in content preservation and 12% in temporal consistency), and achieving a significant 5% gain in downstream road line segmentation and 7% gain in downstream multi-object detection.

</details>


### [10] [Conditional Imaging for Privacy-Preserving Visual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28835)
*Hengyu Lian,Debabrata Mandal,Anzhou Wen,Yujie Wang,Xi Peng,Praneeth Chakravarthula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"条件成像"(conditional imaging)：每采集一帧就改变相机光学状态，使单次测量本身隐去场景，而掌握对应物理状态的一方才能重建，从而在采集端实现可恢复的视觉隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉传感存在根本困境：今日应保密的信息明日可能需恢复。相机日益普及使该问题更突出。现有方案要么仅在成像后保护内容，要么在采集时抑制信息以致无法日后恢复，难以兼顾隐私与可恢复性。

Method: 首先以信息论分析证明静态光学系统中隐私与可恢复性不可兼得：为重建而保留在测量中的信息同样可为该测量的观察者所用。据此提出条件成像——逐帧改变光学状态，使重建需依赖相机物理状态；分析光学参数如何决定可恢复信息量，并据此设计相机原型；在人脸数据集上采用逐级增强的恢复攻击（直接可视化、计算反演、学习式重建、身份识别）进行评估。

Result: 实验表明：持有系统状态的授权重建可保持身份信息；而无系统状态的观察者在上述各类攻击下均无法获取视觉信息，隐私得到有效保护。

Conclusion: 将相机物理状态纳入重建所需信息，条件成像实现了在采集时刻即保护视觉信息，同时保留未来授权恢复的可能，突破了静态光学系统的固有限制。

Abstract: Conventional visual sensing faces a fundamental dilemma: information that should remain private today may need to be recovered tomorrow. This challenge is becoming increasingly important as cameras become pervasive in our daily surroundings. Existing safeguards either protect visual content only after an image is formed or suppress information during capture in ways that prevent its later recovery. Here, we show through an information-theoretic analysis that privacy and recoverability cannot be achieved simultaneously in static optical systems. Information preserved in a measurement for later reconstruction also remains available to an observer of that measurement, limiting the privacy such systems can provide. To overcome this limitation, we introduce conditional imaging, an imaging approach that changes the optical state for every captured frame. The measurement alone conceals the scene, while knowledge of the corresponding physical system state enables its reconstruction. We analyze how the optical parameters determine the information that can be recovered and use this analysis to guide the design of a camera prototype. We evaluate the system on a captured dataset of human faces using increasingly powerful attempts to recover the scene, from direct visualization and computational inversion to learned reconstruction and identity recognition. Our experiments show that conditional imaging preserves identity in authorized reconstructions while withholding visual information from observers without access to the system state. By making the physical state of the camera part of the information needed for reconstruction, conditional imaging enables visual information to be protected at the point of capture while remaining recoverable for future authorized use.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond Correct Answers: How Compositional Cues Shape Visual Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28843)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Zheyuan Zhang,Kaiwen Shi,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统剖析结构化提示中"标签"与"辅助提示"在视觉绑定任务中的具体作用：通过隔离字段标签效应、对比保留/交换角色分配的提示、以及测试更清晰措辞与关系直接选择，揭示整体准确率所掩盖的模型差异与交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQA需要把属性和关系正确绑定到对象（visual binding），结构化提示用带标签字段使绑定显式化；但"完整提示带来提升"并不能说明哪个标签起作用、标签之间如何交互，因此需要拆解分析。

Method: 三组受控实验：(1) 固定图像与claim，在10个模型、6个任务上隔离字段标签效应；(2) 在两组图像集上，将保留或交换问题角色分配的辅助提示与无提示基线对比，并考察字段顺序对危害的影响；(3) 测试更清晰的提示措辞，以及直接选择图中所示关系。

Result: 存在关系标签时，加入对象角色标签对Qwen3在Spatial上有帮助、却损害Qwen2；Qwen3使用保留型提示的优势主要源于交换型提示引发的错误，保留型提示接近基线，字段顺序会影响危害程度；更清晰措辞改善文本匹配但未稳定改善视觉判断，而关系选择在Qwen3与GPT-4.1-mini上部分减少了特定提示的错误。

Conclusion: 整体准确率会掩盖标签层面的差异与交互；标签收益具有模型与任务依赖性，交换角色分配的提示会显著致错，措辞清晰化不等于视觉判断提升，直接关系选择是较有前景的干预方向。

Abstract: Visual question answering requires models to assign attributes and relations to the correct objects, a problem known as visual binding. Structured prompts make these assignments explicit through labeled fields. Yet an improvement from the full prompt does not reveal which labels help or how they interact. First, we isolate field-label effects across ten models and six tasks by holding the image and claim fixed. These comparisons reveal differences that overall accuracy can hide. With a relation label present, adding object-role labels helps Qwen3 but hurts Qwen2 on Spatial. Second, we compare auxiliary hints that preserve or swap the question's role assignments against a no-hint baseline. Across two image sets, Qwen3's advantage with preserving hints mainly reflects errors caused by swapped hints. Preserving hints remain near baseline. Field order further affects the harm. Third, we test clearer hint wording and direct selection of the relation shown in the image. Clearer hints improve text matching but do not consistently improve visual judgments. In contrast, relation selection partly reduces errors for some hints in Qwen3 and GPT-4.1-mini.

</details>


### [12] [Depth-guided Geometric Adaptation for Few-shot Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28856)
*Zhiqi Miao,Estefanía Talavera,Weichuan Zhang,Jiapan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAFIC 提出几何引导的小样本图像分类框架，将单目深度引入表征学习、情景自适应与稠密支持-查询匹配，缓解视觉歧义，在四个基准上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本图像分类（FSIC）常受视觉歧义困扰：在数据稀缺条件下，背景杂乱与 RGB 外观相似性削弱了判别性特征的匹配。

Method: 1）通过 RGB-D 自监督预训练学习几何感知的 token 表征；2）深度引导几何自适应模块（DGA）利用深度几何精炼 patch 表征，注入几何增强的类上下文并抑制背景干扰；3）空间双向 patch 匹配（SBiM）对精炼特征进行双向稠密匹配，降低对虚假对应与背景杂乱的敏感性。

Result: 在四个基准上的大量实验表明，DAFIC 相较现有方法取得强性能；消融与鲁棒性分析验证了几何条件化情景自适应与双向匹配耦合的有效性。

Conclusion: 将单目深度几何与双向稠密匹配结合，可有效提升小样本视觉识别的判别性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Few-shot image classification (FSIC) frequently suffers from visual ambiguity, where background clutter and RGB appearance similarities hinder discriminative feature matching in data-scarce settings. To mitigate this, we propose DAFIC, a geometry-guided framework that incorporates monocular depth into representation learning, episodic adaptation, and dense support-query matching. DAFIC first learns geometry-aware token representations through RGB-D self-supervised pre-training. Subsequently, the Depth-guided Geometric Adaptation (DGA) module uses depth-derived geometry to refine patch representations with geometry-enriched class context while suppressing background interference. Spatial Bidirectional Patch Matching (SBiM) then performs bidirectional dense matching on the refined features, reducing sensitivity to spurious correspondences and background clutter. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks show that DAFIC achieves strong performance against existing methods, with ablation and robustness analyses supporting the effectiveness of coupling geometry-conditioned episodic adaptation with bidirectional matching for few-shot visual recognition.

</details>


### [13] [DualHeads: Reward-tilted few-step diffusion made simple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28858)
*Yuanzhi Zhu,Eleftherios Tsonis,Simo Alami Chehboune,Xi Wang,Stéphane Lathuilière,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualHeads 仅用两个二分类头（奖励头与参考头）在冻结扩散骨干上，对少步蒸馏生成器做奖励微调，无需可微奖励或分数场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏扩散模型可在极少步内生成高质量图像，但将其对齐到任务需求与人类偏好很困难。现有多步扩散对齐方法依赖可微奖励、分数场或随机轨迹，而少步蒸馏生成器往往不具备这些条件，因此需要仅凭外部奖励评估与参考样本即可完成适配的方案。

Method: 在冻结扩散骨干上添加两个轻量分类头：奖励头学习同一提示下哪张样本得分更高（成对偏好），参考头区分参考图像与生成图像；生成器最大化两点头 logits 的加权和。两头顶最优时，该目标等价于对学习奖励的 KL 正则化最大化，且头权重之比决定正则强度。

Result: 在规则型与模型型奖励下，少步 DualHeads 生成器的任务奖励接近 40 步强化学习方法，同时保持图像质量，且无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: DualHeads 提供了一种简单优雅的双头形式化，将偏好对齐化为二分类驱动的 KL 正则奖励最大化，推进了少步奖励倾斜生成（reward-tilted generation）。

Abstract: Distilled diffusion models generate high-quality images in a few sampling steps, but aligning them with task requirements and human preferences is difficult. Methods developed for multi-step diffusion models rely on differentiable rewards, or on score fields and stochastic trajectories that distilled generators may lack. We ask whether a few-step generator can be adapted using only external reward evaluations and reference samples, and show that two binary classifiers suffice. Our method, DualHeads, implements them as lightweight heads on a frozen diffusion backbone. The reward head learns which samples of the same prompt score higher, the reference head separates reference images from generated ones, and the generator maximizes a weighted sum of their logits. When the heads are optimal, this objective is KL-regularized maximization of the learned reward, and the ratio of head weights sets the regularization strength. Empirically, with both rule-based and model-based rewards, a few-step DualHeads generator reaches task rewards close to those of 40-step reinforcement learning methods while preserving image quality, at no extra inference cost. We hope that this simple and elegant dual-head formulation will help advance few-step reward-tilted generation.

</details>


### [14] [Policy-Mask: Budget-Aware Adaptation of Diffusion Features to Discriminative Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28889)
*Ivan Listopadov,Maksim Nakhodnov,Aibek Alanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Policy-Mask，一个受预算约束的策略，在下游目标下联合选择扩散模型中间特征的 timestep、block 与通道子集；通过按子集基数归一化通道得分稳定联合训练，在分割、分类、语义对应上均优于固定抽取基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从冻结扩散模型抽取判别性中间特征并非易事：有用信号分布在去噪轨迹（timestep）、网络深度（block）与通道三个维度上。现有方法要么手工固定这些选择，要么只优化少数抽取超参（多为 timestep），并对所有输入和预算复用同一规则，缺乏自适应性与预算感知能力。

Method: 1) 将 timestep、block、channel 三个选择统一建模为预算条件化策略（Policy-Mask），直接以下游任务目标训练，在给定通道预算下联合选择；2) 针对未归一化时通道得分贡献随所选通道数增长、压过 timestep 与 block 更新尺度的问题，按子集基数（cardinality）归一化通道得分，使三个策略头量级可比、联合训练稳定；3) 将方法扩展至扩散 Transformer（PixArt-Σ、SD3.5 MMDiT），以图像 token 隐维度替代 U-Net 通道维度。

Result: 在同等预算下优于固定抽取与特征过滤基线：ADE20K 分割 38.9 mIoU；CIFAR-100 在 128 维下 75.4% top-1；SPair-71k 语义对应 76.3 PCK@0.1。消融显示 block 选择对下游精度有实质影响，最高通道预算得分出现在保留 256 个通道处，可作为紧凑扩散表征的实用工作点。

Conclusion: 联合、预算感知地选择 timestep/block/channel 优于手工或部分优化的抽取规则，归一化通道得分是稳定联合训练的关键；256 通道构成实用的紧凑表征配置，且方法可迁移到 DiT 类骨干。

Abstract: Selecting intermediate features from a frozen diffusion model for discriminative tasks is non-trivial because the useful signal is distributed across the denoising trajectory, network depth, and channels. In practice, this requires choosing a timestep, a block, and a channel subset. Existing methods either fix these choices by hand or optimize only a few extraction hyperparameters, most often the timestep, then reuse the same rule across inputs and budgets. We introduce Policy-Mask, a budget-conditioned policy trained on the downstream objective that jointly selects all three under a prescribed channel budget. Without normalization, the channel-score contribution grows with the number of selected channels and dominates the timestep and block updates. We normalize it by subset cardinality, keeping the three policy heads on comparable scales and stabilizing joint training. At matched budgets, Policy-Mask improves over fixed extraction and feature-filter baselines across segmentation, classification, and semantic correspondence, reaching 38.9 mIoU on ADE20K, 75.4% top-1 on CIFAR-100 at 128 dimensions, and 76.3 PCK@0.1 on SPair-71k. Component-wise ablations show that block selection materially affects downstream accuracy, and the highest recorded channel-budget score occurs at 256 retained channels, identifying a practical operating point for compact diffusion representations. We further adapt Policy-Mask to diffusion transformers, including PixArt-$Σ$ and SD3.5 MMDiT, by replacing U-Net channels with image-token hidden dimensions.

</details>


### [15] [Data-Driven Glyph Positioning for Handwritten Music Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28899)
*Elona Shatri,Jiří Mayer,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文验证 Smashcima 合成框架中硬编码的符号定位规则是否有实证依据，并用实证均值与图神经网络（GNN）学习上下文相关定位，显著降低定位误差并提升下游 OMR 检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写乐谱标注数据稀缺，合成数据是 OMR 的常用对策；但 Smashcima 依赖从 MUSCIMA++ 采样字形并用硬编码规则排布，这些规则缺乏实证支撑，其参数可能与真实手写存在系统性偏差。

Method: 从 MUSCIMA++ 与 OmniOMR 约 28.5 万条标注关系中提取符号间分布，检验 Smashcima 关键参数偏差；用实证均值替换硬编码常数；再基于 Music Notation Graph 结构训练 GNN 预测上下文相关偏移；并进行跨数据集分析与下游 YOLOv8 检测评估。

Result: 关键参数与真实手写存在系统性偏离，替换为实证均值使定位 RMSE 最多下降 60%；GNN 在符干与符梁上再降 50%–85% RMSE。跨数据集间距差异由音符密度解释（3.3），而非书写风格；时值比例间距在手写中不成立（R²=0.001）。在 200 个真实手写谱表上微调后，YOLOv8 较基线提升 +30% mAP50、较实证均值提升 +11%。

Conclusion: Smashcima 的硬编码定位规则缺乏实证依据，实证均值与 GNN 学习式上下文定位可显著改善合成数据质量，且定位精度对下游 OMR 性能有实质影响。

Abstract: Synthesised data is a common strategy to address the lack of annotated handwritten music scores in Optical Music Recognition (OMR). Smashcima is a recent synthesis framework that assembles pages of handwritten scores by sampling glyph images from MUSCIMA++ and positioning them with hard-coded rules. In this work we ask whether these rules are grounded empirically and whether a learned model for context-dependent positioning can further improve synthesis quality. We extract inter-glyph distributions from around 285 thousand annotated relationships across MUSCIMA++ and OmniOMR, finding that several key parameters deviate systematically from real handwriting. Replacing these constants with empirical means reduces positioning RMSE by up to 60%. We then train a Graph Neural Network (GNN) on the Music Notation Graph structure to predict context-dependent offsets, achieving an additional 50 to 85% RMSE reduction on stems and beams by modelling how neighbouring glyphs constrain placement. Cross-dataset analysis shows that spacing differences between the two corpora are explained by note density (3.3), not style, and that duration-proportional spacing does not hold in handwriting (R2 = 0.001). In downstream evaluation, a YOLOv8 detector pretrained on GNN-positioned synthetic data achieves +30% mAP50 over the baseline and +11% over empirical means when fine-tuned on 200 real handwritten staves, showing that positioning accuracy matters for downstream OMR.

</details>


### [16] [MINER: Modeling Inter- and Intra-Person Variance for Reidentification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28941)
*Oron Nir,Eliyahu Strugo,Ariel Shamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MINER：通过对比困难样本并自适应平衡数据集相关的类内/类间方差，抑制服装偏置的模型无关框架，在CC-ReID四个数据集达SOTA，并在TVRID RGB-Depth ReID挑战赛三个赛道名列前茅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换衣行人重识别（CC-ReID）中服饰变化导致外观剧烈变化，是核心难点。现有工作多依赖生物特征信号提取的启发式方法（如分割掩码）来增强类间差异，却忽视了类内方差的控制。

Method: 提出MINER，一个简单且模型无关的框架：采用困难样本来做对比学习以抑制服装偏置，并自适应地平衡依赖数据集的类内方差与类间方差。

Result: 在多个CC-ReID基准上检索性能提升，在四个数据集上取得SOTA；在公开TVRID RGB-Depth ReID挑战赛三个赛道中均位列领先。

Conclusion: 显式建模并控制类内方差、结合困难样本对比，是缓解服装偏置的有效途径；且方法具有良好的模型无关性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Clothes-Changing Person Re-Identification (CC-ReID) remains challenging due to drastic appearance shifts across outfits. Most prior works devise biometric signal-extraction heuristics to enhance inter-person variance, e.g., using segmentation masks, while leaving intra-person variance uncontrolled. We present MINER, a simple model-agnostic framework that suppresses garment bias by contrasting hard examples and adaptively balancing dataset-dependent intra- and inter-person variance. Extensive experiments show that MINER improves retrieval across CC-ReID benchmarks, achieving state-of-the-art performance on four datasets and ranking among the leaders in all three tracks of the open TVRID RGB-Depth ReID challenge.

</details>


### [17] [Pair-Free Coarse-to-Real Video Generation via Real-to-Coarse Image Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28954)
*Gonzalo Gómez-Nogales,Daohan Lu,Peiye Zhuang,Tinghui Zhou,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免配对训练框架C2R#：利用易得的"真实→粗糙"图像对训练DINO-R2C特征域转换模型，逐帧将真实视频转为伪粗糙视频，从而无需(coarse, realistic)视频对即可训练粗到真实视频生成，推理时丢弃DINO-R2C，直接由真实粗糙视频驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗到真实(C2R)视频生成需要对齐的(粗糙, 真实)视频对，这类数据昂贵且现有系统只能局限于狭窄的合成域，限制了规模化训练。

Method: 利用"配对视频难获取、但真实→粗糙图像对易构造"的不对称性：先用现有编辑与3D重建工具构造图像对，训练DINO-R2C在DINO特征空间将真实图像转为粗糙图像；再逐帧作用于普通真实视频生成伪粗糙视频；最后训练C2R#模型学习从伪粗糙对应帧重建真实视频，从而摆脱视频对依赖。

Result: 训练无需任何(粗糙, 真实)视频对，可随任意真实视频语料规模化扩展；稀疏（逐帧）机制仍生成连贯视频；开箱即用，处理单张图像约0.5秒、约8张图像级联到视频，并可直接应用于用户视频与AI生成视频。

Conclusion: 通过图像域的伪配对构造替代视频配对，实现了免配对的C2R视频生成训练，突破了数据瓶颈与合成域限制，并具备对普通真实视频与生成视频的良好泛化性。

Abstract: Coarse-to-real (C2R) video generation converts coarse animation, simulation, or game-rendered videos into photorealistic videos while preserving scene layout, camera motion, and dynamics. Training such models requires aligned (coarse, realistic) video pairs, which are expensive to create and confine prior systems to narrow synthetic domains. We exploit a simple asymmetry: paired videos are hard to obtain, but paired real-to-coarse images are easy to construct with existing editing and 3D reconstruction tools. Using these image pairs, we train the DINO-R2C model to transfer realistic images to coarse images in DINO feature space, and apply it frame-wise to ordinary real videos to synthesize pseudo-coarse videos. The C2R# model then learns to generate each real video from its pseudo-coarse counterpart, free from needing (coarse, realistic) video pairs. At inference, the DINO-R2C model is discarded and actual coarse videos drive the C2R# model directly. Thus, our pair-free training scales with any real-video corpora rather than with paired synthetic videos. We will release the model and project webpage at https://gonzalognogales.github.io/coarse2real-sharp/.

</details>


### [18] [RoPT: Robust 6DoF Object Pose Tracking in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28962)
*Yongqing Liang,Yuzhong Chen,Sihang Li,Junsuk Kim,Chase Kidder,Hui-ping Wang,Yunzhi Xu,Raihan Haque,Yubiao Zhang,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoPT是一个将视觉感知、时序运动学与全局重定位紧耦合为闭环的6-DoF物体位姿跟踪框架，显著提升快速运动下的跟踪存活率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目6-DoF物体位姿跟踪对机器人交互至关重要，但传统单目流程在物体快速运动时容易发生灾难性发散；现有跟踪器多为开环结构，缺乏失败感知与自恢复能力。

Method: 三项核心机制：1）视觉感知模块通过跟踪物体掩码处理部分及严重遮挡；2）运动学因子图对帧间更新进行正则化、前向位姿预测，并驱动并行多假设验证以跳出局部极小；3）主动重定位模块将流程变为闭环，使之具备失败感知与确定性自恢复能力。

Result: 在多个挑战性基准与真实机器人演示中验证，快速运动条件下跟踪存活率显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将视觉、运动学与重定位紧耦合为闭环是提升6-DoF位姿跟踪鲁棒性（尤其快速运动场景）的有效途径。

Abstract: Accurate 6-DoF object pose tracking is essential for robotic interaction, yet conventional monocular pipelines frequently suffer from catastrophic divergence under rapid object motion. We present RoPT, a robust tracking framework engineered to overcome this limitation by unifying visual perception, temporal kinematics, and global re-localization into a tightly coupled closed loop. Unlike existing open-loop trackers, RoPT delivers three critical capabilities that fundamentally alter system resilience. First, the visual perception component tracks object masks to robustly handle partial and severe occlusions. Second, a kinematic factor graph regularizes frame-to-frame updates, forecasts forward pose predictions, and drives a parallel multi-hypothesis verification scheme to bypass local minima. Finally, an active re-localization module transforms the pipeline into a closed-loop system, making it failure-aware and capable of deterministic self-recovery. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework across challenging benchmarks alongside real-world robotic demonstrations, achieving significantly higher tracking survivability under rapid motion.

</details>


### [19] [TEMPL: A Resource-Efficient and Transparent Model-Free Framework for Pseudo-Label Generation in Material CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28974)
*Ashiqur Rahman,Venkata Devesh Reddy Seethi,Austin Yunker,Rajkumar Kettimuthu,Hamed Alhoori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种多阶段、无模型（model-free）的伪标签框架，直接从灰度CT图像经阈值化、形态学膨胀与自定义BFS区域生长生成多边形标注，无需预训练权重、标注数据或GPU，在材料CT分割中显著优于零样本SAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料CT分析中大规模标注数据稀缺，限制了深度学习分割；基于DL的伪标签方法计算开销大且可解释性差，尤其在资源受限场景难以使用。

Method: 多阶段无模型框架：阈值化 + 形态学膨胀 + 自定义广度优先搜索（BFS）区域生长算法，直接从灰度CT生成多边形伪标签；与零样本SAM、全监督3D U-Net及经典基线对比；并进行阈值敏感性分析。

Result: 枝晶数据集上IoU达63.19%（零样本SAM为10.74%），0.5 IoU下实例召回率为SAM的4倍（33.3% vs 8.3%）；监督3D U-Net作为需专家标签的性能上限参照。硅砂样本上，用其标签训练的YOLO与用SAM标签训练者定性相当；1,053颗颗粒中位等效直径163.8 μm，接近筛分范围106–150 μm，SAM为219.5 μm（合并相邻颗粒并产生虚假检测）。敏感性分析显示关键阈值具有稳定工作区间，失效点与相位对比度相关且可预测。单图CPU耗时15s，SAM需GPU 28s。

Conclusion: 该透明框架可作为标注自举（annotation bootstrapping）与微观结构定量工具，适用于资源受限环境下的材料CT分析。

Abstract: The scarcity of large annotated datasets limits deep learning (DL) segmentation in material CT analysis. DL-based pseudo-labeling can help, but it is often computationally expensive and hard to interpret. We introduce a multi-stage, model-free framework that generates pseudo-labels directly from grayscale CT scans. It combines thresholding, morphological dilation, and a custom breadth-first search region-growing algorithm to produce polygonal annotations without pre-trained weights, labeled data, or a GPU. We compare it against the zero-shot Segment Anything Model (SAM), a fully supervised 3D U-Net, and classical baselines. On a dendrite dataset with ground truth, our method reaches 63.19% IoU versus 10.74% for zero-shot SAM, with four times the instance recall at 0.5 IoU (33.3% versus 8.3%). The supervised 3D U-Net serves as a reference ceiling that requires expert labels. On a silica sand specimen, YOLO models trained on our labels performed comparably to models trained on SAM labels in a qualitative comparison. Across 1,053 extracted grains, the median equivalent diameter was 163.8 micrometers, near the specimen's known 106 to 150 micrometers sieve range, compared with 219.5 micrometers for SAM, whose labels merge adjacent particles and add spurious detections. A sensitivity analysis shows a stable operating range for the key threshold and a sharp, predictable failure point linked to phase contrast. Our method needs 15s per image on a standard CPU versus 28s for SAM on a GPU. These results support our transparent approach as a tool for annotation bootstrapping and microstructural quantification in resource-limited settings.

</details>


### [20] [MedBOE: Optimization-Based Estimation of Complete Patient Body Models from Whole-Body Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28985)
*Hoijoon Jung,Yuhao Wei,Mingxiao Tu,Younhyun Jung,Michael Fulham,Jinman Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的MedBOE框架，利用SMPL参数化人体模板，从缺失部分身体区域的全身体影像中优化估计完整患者身体模型，误差控制在2%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身医学影像受扫描仪几何（如孔径）、患者体型与体位限制，常缺失侧臂/躯干、手、前臂、肘等区域，导致患者身体模型不完整，进而限制体况指标（如体体积、表面积）的计算，影响放射性示踪剂给药方案、心血管代谢风险评估及计算解剖建模等下游分析。

Method: MedBOE（Medical Body model Optimization-based Estimator）为自动化、免训练框架：将SMPL参数化人体网格模板引入医学影像，通过属性引导优化将模板对齐到不完整的患者身体模型，并在几何与体积约束下变形以估计缺失身体区域。

Result: 在AutoPET（91例全身PET-CT）与HIT（40例MRI全身分割）两个公开数据集上，MedBOE在体积与几何性能上总体最优；由其导出的体况指标误差在2%以内。

Conclusion: MedBOE提供了一种自动化、免训练的方法，可从存在缺失身体区域的全身影像中估计完整患者身体模型，满足下游医学分析对低误差体况指标的需求。

Abstract: True whole-body medical imaging captures a patient from head to toe and provides comprehensive anatomical and/or functional information. This coverage enables reconstruction of the external body surface, yielding patient body models. However, scanner geometry (e.g., bore diameter), patient characteristics, and positioning can leave body regions missing (e.g., lateral arms/torso, hands, forearms, elbows), producing incomplete patient body models. Such incompleteness limits downstream medical analyses, including derivation of body-habitus metrics (BHMs), such as body volume and surface area, for injected radiotracer activity regimens, cardiometabolic risk assessment, and computational anatomical modeling for medical imaging simulation and radiation dosimetry. We present the Medical Body model Optimization-based Estimator (MedBOE), an automated, training-free framework for estimating complete patient body models from whole-body images with missing body regions. Our innovation is to leverage the Skinned Multi-Person Linear (SMPL) template, a parametric body mesh representing a broad range of body shapes and poses, in medical imaging. MedBOE aligns the SMPL template to the incomplete patient body models via attribute-guided optimization and estimates missing body regions through geometrically and volumetrically constrained deformation. We evaluated MedBOE using two public datasets: AutoPET (91 whole-body PET-CT images) and Human Implicit Tissues (HIT; 40 whole-body segmentations from MRI images). MedBOE achieved the best overall volumetric and geometric performance among evaluated methods. BHMs derived from MedBOE were within 2% error, suggesting low percentage errors for downstream analyses. MedBOE offers an automated, training-free method for estimating complete patient body models from whole-body images with missing body regions.

</details>


### [21] [T-DIG: Text-Conditioned Diversity for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29003)
*Simran Khanuja,Rohit Gandikota,Gaurav Parmar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T-DIG：训练一个LLM策略生成K条轴条件提示/编辑指令，驱动冻结的图像生成器，实现用户可控的多图多样性，并发布首个文本条件多样性数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2I平台每次返回多张图供用户探索，但模型偏向单图质量，牺牲了多图多样性；且"多样性"是语境相关的——不同用户/时刻希望沿背景、风格、服装等不同轴变化，生成图应沿用户关心的维度差异化。

Method: 迭代式框架：用户输入提示→查看默认结果→用自然语言请求变化→继续细化。核心是一个LLM策略，为冻结的生成器/编辑器产出K条轴条件提示或编辑指令；用GRPO训练，奖励包含文本条件多样性奖励与对齐惩罚项；因生成器冻结，可适用于开源权重、蒸馏和专有模型。同时发布首个文本条件提示多样性数据集（训练/验证集由人工撰写轴构建，测试集来自真实用户交互）。

Result: 在多种指标与人工评估中，T-DIG大幅提升文本条件多样性，同时保持基提示对齐；验证了无需访问模型权重即可为各类图像生成模型（开源与闭源）加入用户导向的多样性探索能力。

Conclusion: 用户导向的多样性探索可作为即插即用模块加诸广泛的T2I模型，实现沿用户指定轴的、可交互细化的多图多样性生成。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) platforms typically return several images per prompt so users can explore different visual interpretations and choose one that matches their intent. However, T2I models are usually optimized for single-image quality which comes at a cost of multi-image diversity, limiting this exploration. Diversity is also contextual: one user wants variations in background, while another wants them in artistic style, and the same user may later want variations in clothing. The generated images should differ along whatever axis the user cares about. In this paper, we introduce T-DIG, a text-conditioned diversity framework for image generation and editing. T-DIG is an iterative framework comprising multiple modules that lets users type prompts, inspect default generations, request variations using natural language, and continue refining outputs until they're satisfied. At its core, T-DIG uses an LLM policy that produces K axis-conditioned prompts or edit instructions for a frozen generator or editor. We train this policy with GRPO using a text-conditioned diversity reward and alignment penalties. Because the image generator remains frozen, T-DIG applies to open-weight, distilled, and proprietary models. We also release the first dataset for text-conditioned prompt diversity in image generation, with the training and validation splits built from human-written axes and a test set drawn from real user interactions with T-DIG. T-DIG far exceeds baselines in improving text-conditioned diversity, while preserving base-prompt alignment across a variety of metrics and human judgements. We show that user-directed diversity exploration can be added to a wide range of image generation models, open and closed, without the need to access their weights. Website: https://image-study.org/t-dig/

</details>


### [22] [Enhancing Cross-Grade Generalization in Banana Variety Classification via Hybrid CNN-Transformers and Knowledge Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29008)
*Khush Kataruka,Mohammad Muzammila,Zahid Akhtar,Raghavendra Ramachandra,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对跨质量等级（unseen grade）的香蕉品种分类任务，在Leave-One-Grade-Out协议下系统比较CNN、ViT与双流混合架构，并提出基于KL散度自蒸馏的Swin-ResNet双流混合框架，平均准确率达81.25%，优于单范式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化水果分类在精准农业中用于产量估计、质量控制和供应链优化，但在质量差异、成熟度、光照、纹理退化、形态属性、域偏移及采后处理等条件下仍具挑战。作者聚焦真实场景中的跨等级香蕉品种分类：模型在特定质量等级上训练后需泛化到未见等级，存在显著域偏移。同时，单一范式框架（纯CNN或纯Transformer）存在固有缺陷，需要混合架构加以弥补。

Method: 构建含31,678张香蕉图像、3个品种与3个质量等级的大规模数据集；采用严格的Leave-One-Grade-Out评估协议进行跨等级泛化测试；对CNN、ViT及双流混合架构开展对比分析；提出双流Swin-ResNet混合框架，并引入基于KL散度的自蒸馏，对齐CNN流与Transformer流的概率特征表示。

Result: 所提混合框架配合KL散度自蒸馏取得最佳整体性能，平均准确率81.25%，显著超越CNN与Transformer基线；定性分析表明该框架提升了判别性特征的可迁移性、改善了注意力定位，并稳定了优化过程。

Conclusion: 双流Swin-ResNet结合KL散度自蒸馏能有效缓解跨质量等级的域偏移问题，验证了混合范式相较单一范式在真实农业视觉分类场景中的优势，为跨域水果分类提供了可复用的架构与评估范式。

Abstract: In recent years, automated fruit classification systems have become a more critical component in precision agriculture for efficient yield estimation, quality control, and supply chain optimization. However, they still face significant challenges under varying quality, fruit maturity, illumination, texture degradation, morphological attributes, domain shift, and post-harvest handling conditions. In this study, we address the challenging issue of cross-grade banana variety classification. This is a real-world setting where models that have been trained on specific quality grades must generalize to unseen grades to handle significant domain shift. Under a rigorous Leave-One-Grade-Out evaluation protocol, we present a thorough comparative analysis of CNNs, ViTs, and dual-stream hybrid architectures using a large-scale dataset of 31,678 banana images with three varieties and three quality grades. Specifically, to address the drawbacks of single-paradigm frameworks, we present a dual-stream Swin ResNet hybrid framework enhanced with KL-divergence-based self-distillation to align probabilistic feature representations across the CNN and Transformer streams. Comprehensive experimental analyzes show that the presented hybrid framework with KL-divergence based self-distillation achieves better overall performance, i.e., it attains a mean accuracy of 81.25% and significantly surpasses CNN and Transformer baseline models. Qualitative analysis further confirms that the hybrid framework in this study improves discriminative feature transferability, improves attention localization, and stabilizes optimization.

</details>


### [23] [Single-Image Food Mass Estimators Do Not Transfer Their Gram Scale Across Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29025)
*Yongsheng Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在Nutrition5k上微调的DINOv2-base域内MAE为24.8 g（不到Qwen3-VL-32B的一半），但在三个外部数据集上均未超过零样本视觉语言模型；其克度量随拍摄视野偏移、且食物间存在不同缩放因子，新食物的排序能力不优于像素计数，且随机划分因同一餐盘多次拍摄而高估精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张RGB照片估计食物质量是图像化膳食评估的核心任务，但已训练模型能否迁移到新相机与未见食物尚不清楚；已有评测多在同域随机划分下进行，可能因重复拍摄导致乐观偏差。

Method: 以2,253份称重Nutrition5k餐盘微调DINOv2-base，从单张RGB照片回归质量；与零样本Qwen3-VL-32B在Nutrition5k及三个外部集（ASA24 studio portions、ECUSTFD全食物、Food Portion Benchmark餐盘）对比；开展食物/托盘再训练的消融、DINOv2-large骨干对照、单张同食物标注照片的少样本校正、90%预测区间覆盖率检验，并分析随机测试划分与训练的重复图像重叠。

Result: 域内MAE 24.8 g，优于Qwen一倍以上；外部数据集未超越Qwen。未见食物的份量排序与食物像素计数相当。克尺度随视野占比变化，并因食物类别存在不同乘性偏差。用被测数据集的其他食物或托盘训练可将外部误差降低38%–65%；DINOv2-large未降低外部误差。一张同食物标注照片使ASA24误差降至28.3 g，优于该照片单独估计。90%预测区间在外部照片上覆盖率至多50%。

Conclusion: 单图质量估计器缺乏跨相机与跨食物的可迁移性，其绝对克尺度不可靠；在Nutrition5k上微调并无外部优势，模型容量扩展亦无帮助，而少量目标场景标注与预测区间校准至关重要。实际部署须在使用场景采集标注照片，并避免因同一餐盘重复拍摄导致的随机划分精度高估。

Abstract: Estimating food mass from a photograph is central to image-based dietary assessment, but whether trained models transfer to new cameras and foods is unclear. We fine-tuned DINOv2-base on 2,253 weighed Nutrition5k dishes to predict mass from one RGB photograph and compared it with a zero-shot vision-language model, Qwen3-VL-32B, on Nutrition5k and three external datasets: ASA24 studio portions, ECUSTFD whole foods and Food Portion Benchmark meal trays. In domain, the fine-tuned model's mean absolute error was 24.8 g, less than half of Qwen's. On external datasets it did not beat Qwen. For a food it had never seen, it ranked portions by size, but no better than a count of food pixels. Its gram scale was wrong in two ways: it changed with how much of the scene the photograph showed, and it was off by a different factor for each food. Training on other foods or trays from the tested dataset cut the external error by 38 to 65%. DINOv2-large, a larger backbone trained on the same dishes, did not reduce the external error. One labeled photograph of the same food cut the model's ASA24 error to 28.3 g, better than that photograph achieved alone. Prediction intervals that held the true mass for 90% of Nutrition5k dishes held it for at most half of external photographs. Nutrition5k often photographed the same plate several times, so a random test split shared near-identical photographs with training and overstated accuracy. Single-image mass estimators therefore need labeled photographs from the setting where they are used.

</details>


### [24] [HumanCentric-Scenes-AI: A Human-Curated Multimodal Dataset of AI-Generated Human-Centric Scenes Across Surveillance, Occupational Portraits, AI-Edited Images, and Short-Form Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29045)
*Nima Kamali,Sana Nasiri,Negar Kamali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文发布 HumanCentric-Scenes-AI 数据集，包含 296 个由人工多轮筛选的 AI 生成刺激（85 张监控图像、128 张配对的职业肖像、27 张 AI 编辑的真实照片、56 段短视频），并提供提示词与逐项元数据，用于人类感知研究、检测器校准与生成模型偏差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成媒体研究缺乏贴近真实人机遭遇场景、经人工筛选且具备元数据的刺激集；作者希望提供一个可控、可复现的多场景基准，支撑人类感知实验、检测器校准与生成模型偏差分析。

Method: 人工多轮筛选与迭代，构建四个子集：Surveillance（85 张 CCTV 风格图）、Occupations（128 张职业肖像，64 种职业×男女配对的平衡设计）、AI-edited（27 张真实照片中局部区域被生成模型替换/修改）、Video（56 段短视频）。每项刺激公开文件、生成提示词（若记录）与逐项元数据；分析包括未指定性别的 134 次生成中的性别分布与美国劳工统计局职业性别比例对比、编辑区域大小与零样本检测难度的关联、监控子集图像质量分布、视频子集主体类别覆盖。

Result: 报告了生成图像中呈现性别的分布并与美国劳工统计局职业性别占比进行对照；量化了编辑区域面积与零样本检测难度的关系；给出 Surveillance 子集的图像质量分布及 Video 子集的主体类别覆盖情况。

Conclusion: HumanCentric-Scenes-AI 首先面向人类感知研究，同时可用于 AI 检测分类器的校准以及生成模型的偏差分析。

Abstract: We introduce HumanCentric-Scenes-AI, a human-curated dataset of 296 AI-generated stimuli that depict people across four contexts in which people encounter AI-generated media. The Surveillance subset contains 85 AI-generated CCTV-style images. The Occupations subset contains 128 AI-generated occupational portraits in a balanced paired design covering 64 occupations with one female and one male version of each. The AI-edited subset contains 27 real photographs in which a region was altered or replaced by a generative model. The Video subset contains 56 AI-generated short videos. We curated the images and videos manually over multiple rounds to make them as photorealistic as possible. For each stimulus we release the file, the generation prompt where recorded, and per-stimulus metadata. We report depicted-gender distributions from an initial set of 134 generations without a specified gender and compare them with U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics occupational gender shares. We report edit-region size and its association with zero-shot detection difficulty for the AI-edited subset. We report image-quality distributions for the Surveillance subset and subject-category coverage for the Video subset. HumanCentric-Scenes-AI is intended first for human-perception research, and also for calibration of AI-detection classifiers and bias analysis of generative models.

</details>


### [25] [Weakly Supervised Fish Segmentation through Point-Supervised Tracking and Motion-Informed Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29047)
*Vedanth Kallakuri,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅需点标注（每条鱼一次点击）的弱监督鱼类分割流水线，结合点跟踪、语言引导分割与后处理滤波，在DeepFish上提升前景mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼类种群分析（长度、宽度、质量测量及野生种群计数）对水产养殖和海洋保护有重要价值，深度学习分割模型可规模化获取此类信息，但逐像素标注极其昂贵（每条鱼约需两分钟），因此希望以低成本标注实现准确分割。

Method: 四阶段弱监督分割流水线：(1) 点监督跟踪器为每帧预测点标注；(2) 将点转换为运动信息增强的语言表达（motion-informed language expressions）；(3) 用这些表达驱动语言条件分割模型LMPM++，利用时序一致性生成候选分割掩码；(4) 以光流和背景减除作为后处理滤波器，抑制错误分割带来的噪声。

Result: 在DeepFish数据集上，前景平均交并比（mIoU）相比基线模型有所提升。

Conclusion: 点级弱监督配合跟踪、语言条件分割与运动线索后处理，可在显著降低标注成本的同时获得优于基线的鱼类分割效果，为规模化鱼类表型与种群监测提供可行方案。

Abstract: Understanding the properties of fish populations is increasingly important. Analyzing fish length, width, and mass is integral to maintaining healthy stock in the aquaculture industry, while counting wild fish populations supports marine conservation and coastal reef restoration efforts. Deep learning-based segmentation models offer a promising means of obtaining such information at scale. Most of these models, however, require costly and time-consuming per-pixel segmentation labels for training, which can take up to two minutes per fish to annotate manually. To address this inefficiency, we propose a weakly supervised segmentation pipeline that requires only point-labeled data (one click per fish) to produce accurate segmentation masks. Our pipeline consists of four stages. First, a point-supervised tracker predicts point annotations for each frame. These points are then converted into motion-informed language expressions, which guide a language-conditioned segmentation model (LMPM++) to capture temporal consistency and produce candidate segmentation masks. Finally, optical flow and background subtraction are applied as post-processing filters to suppress noise from incorrect segmentations. Experiments on the DeepFish dataset show improvement in foreground mean intersection over union (mIoU) as compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [26] [OccJEPA: Occlusion as Masking for Amodal Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29061)
*Genpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将遮挡建模为掩码问题，把预训练的V-JEPA 2.1预测器用于填补物体被遮挡区域的特征，并用轻量解码头生成amodal掩码，提出确定性的OccJEPA模型，比扩散方法快33.5倍且在多个基准上更准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有amodal视频分割最优方法依赖微调的视频扩散模型，需多步去噪与额外深度网络，且每次采样结果不同（随机性）。作者注意到JEPA类自监督视频世界模型的预训练任务——从可见时空区域预测被掩码区域的表征——与amodal分割高度相关，因此可将遮挡视为掩码。

Method: 将遮挡视作掩码：丢弃物体可见掩码未覆盖的token，由预训练的V-JEPA 2.1预测器填补这些token的特征，再用轻量解码头将观测特征与预测特征解码为amodal掩码，得到确定性模型OccJEPA。

Result: 模型确定性且比扩散类SOTA快33.5倍；在SAIL-VOS、TAO-Amodal（含留出测试集+4.00 AP50）以及用SAM2跟踪可见掩码的设置上更准确，并在Kubric上更优。控制实验表明增益来自预测器预训练权重而非模型容量，覆盖87M至1.85B参数的世界模型。

Conclusion: 遮挡即掩码的范式可复用JEPA世界模型的预训练表征预测能力，实现更快、确定性且更准确的amodal视频分割；性能提升源于预训练权重而非规模。代码已开源。

Abstract: Amodal video segmentation asks for the complete mask of an object in every frame, including the parts hidden behind other objects. The best current methods generate these masks with fine-tuned video diffusion models, which need many denoising steps and an extra depth network, and give a different answer for every sample. Self-supervised video world models of the JEPA family are trained on a closely related task: predicting the representation of masked space-time regions from the visible ones. We therefore treat occlusion as masking. The tokens that the object's visible mask does not cover are dropped, the pre-trained V-JEPA 2.1 predictor fills them in, and a light head decodes the observed and predicted features into amodal masks. The resulting model, OccJEPA (Occlusion-as-masking JEPA), is deterministic and 33.5x faster than the diffusion-based state of the art, and more accurate on SAIL-VOS, on TAO-Amodal, including a held-out test split (+4.00 AP50) and visible masks tracked by SAM2, and on Kubric. Controlled experiments trace the gain to the predictor's pre-trained weights rather than to its capacity, for world models from 87M to 1.85B parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/zgp110/occjepa.

</details>


### [27] [ReSeg: Retraining-free segmentation of abdominal organs via generalizable semantic representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29090)
*Derong Yu,Guoyan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReSeg两阶段免重训练框架，利用合成数据预训练实现跨模态零样本腹部器官分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法依赖大规模真实图像，存在泛化能力不足与隐私风险。基于域随机化图像合成的可泛化训练范式可缓解该问题，但现有范式在腹部器官上分割性能有限，或需针对特定数据集微调。

Method: 两阶段无真实图像训练框架：第一阶段由专用数据引擎生成语义与视觉多样的合成图像，预训练器官无关的通用语义表征网络GSRNet，学习对解剖结构、成像对比度、分辨率与空间上下文变化鲁棒的语义特征；第二阶段用GSRNet权重初始化OESNet（器官特定编解码分割网络）的编码器，并由另一数据引擎在严格保留腹部器官固有空间布局的前提下生成多样化视觉图像进行训练。

Result: 在涵盖CT、MRI、PET三种模态的七个腹部器官分割数据集上，ReSeg持续优于现有SOTA方法，且无需任何真实训练图像。

Conclusion: 域随机化合成数据结合两阶段表征预训练与器官特定分割训练，可实现免重训练、域无关的零样本腹部器官分割，为隐私敏感场景提供可行方案。

Abstract: Abdominal organ segmentation is essential in modern medical applications. While existing deep learning approaches demonstrate promising performance, their heavy reliance on large-scale image data raises concerns over generalization capability and privacy risks. This motivates the development of generalizable segmentation training paradigms based on domain-randomized image synthesis, facilitating generalization across unseen real image domains. However, existing paradigms either suffer from limited segmentation performance when applied to abdominal organs, or require dataset-specific finetuning. To address these limitations, we propose ReSeg, a novel two-stage retraining-free framework for domain-agnostic zero-shot abdominal organ segmentation. At the first stage, our organ-agnostic synthetic data-driven pretraining approach exploits semantically and visually diverse synthetic images generated by a dedicated data engine to optimize a Generalizable Semantic Representation Network (GSRNet). The network is thereby capable of learning semantic features robust against variations in anatomical structure, imaging contrast, resolution and spatial context, which provide universal representation capabilities that serve as a foundation for segmentation learning. Subsequently, in the second stage, we continue to train an Organ-specific Encoder-decoder Segmentation Network (OESNet), whose encoder is initialized with the pretrained weights. This training process is facilitated by another dedicated data engine, which generates diversified visual images while strictly retaining the inherent spatial layout of abdominal organs. Comprehensive evaluation on seven abdominal organ segmentation datasets across three different modalities including CT, MRI, and PET demonstrates that ReSeg consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods while eliminating the need for real training images.

</details>


### [28] [UNIVERS: Learning UNIversal Voxel-wise Embedding via Randomized Synthesis for generalizable one-shot landmark localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29098)
*Derong Yu,Qingsong Zhao,Yanbing Yue,Yikai Li,Zhi Zhang,Guoyan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UNIVERS，一个端到端可训练框架，通过随机合成多样伪数据与跨域判别式体素级对比学习，学习通用体素嵌入，实现任意FOV多模态体扫中的单样本地标定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法依赖同一图像增强视图的对比学习，跨模态泛化能力受限；基于配对多模态图像的跨模态嵌入学习又面临训练数据有限和多阶段迭代训练的负担。

Method: 利用器官形状模板合成伪数据，对语义布局、视觉外观、FOV与空间位姿施加随机化，并结合跨域判别式体素级对比学习，迫使网络忽略域特定偏差，学习对成像对比度、分辨率和空间上下文变化鲁棒的特征；整体端到端训练。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的肾上腺数据集上验证，UNIVERS持续优于SOTA单样本地标定位方法，在跨模态场景中优势尤为突出。

Conclusion: 域随机化合成结合体素级对比学习可有效实现跨模态鲁棒的通用嵌入，支持任意FOV多模态体扫的单样本地标定位，并已开源。

Abstract: One-shot landmark localization in volume scans with varying fields of view (FOVs) is challenging. While existing self-supervised learning methods have shown promising performance, they rely on contrastive learning built upon augmented views of the same image, which inherently limits their generalization in cross-modality scenarios. This motivates research on cross-modality embedding learning using paired multi-modality images. However, existing methods suffer from not only limited training data, but also the requirement of multi-stage iterative training. To address these limitations, we propose UNIVERS, an end-to-end trainable framework that learns UNIversal Voxel-wise Embedding via Randomized Synthesis. Our framework leverages diverse pseudo-data synthesized from organ shape templates, with randomization applied to semantic layout, visual appearance, FOV, and spatial pose. Coupled with cross-domain discriminative voxel-wise contrastive learning, this domain randomization strategy forces the network to disregard domain-specific biases and learn features that are robust against variations in imaging contrast, resolution, and spatial context. Consequently, the trained model enables one-shot landmark localization in multi-modality volume scans with arbitrary FOVs. In this study, the efficacy of the proposed method is demonstrated by applying it to the task of localizing the adrenal glands, which are paired, small, and irregular endocrine organs. In particular, comprehensive experiments are conducted on three challenging adrenal gland datasets. The experimental results demonstrate that UNIVERS consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art (SOTA) one-shot landmark localization approaches, with particularly prominent advantages in cross-modality scenarios. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Kendra9999/UNIVERS.

</details>


### [29] [Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Uncertain Forward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29102)
*Wentao Shangguan,Vivek K Goyal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知似然(UAL)，将前向模型参数不确定性积分进似然，得到结构化测量协方差，可直接嵌入现有扩散后验采样器的数据更新中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散后验采样器把前向模型参数（如模糊核、探测器响应）固定为估计值，导致参数不确定性未进入似然而被吸收进重建结果，产生偏差。

Method: 对不确定参数做积分得到边缘似然，用高斯近似构造UAL：为每个候选信号传播参数不确定性为结构化测量协方差，在参数变化可解释的残差方向上削弱二次惩罚。现有采样器保留预训练扩散先验与采样日程即可使用。在高斯冗余参数模型下给出线性化似然误差界及精确边缘后验与UAL后验的平均散度界。

Result: 在六类逆问题（高斯去模糊、运动去模糊、CT、MRI、超声、荧光显微）上重建与集成预测均改善：主高斯去模糊设置几何平均MSE降9%–20%；非盲采样器使用群体均值核时运动去模糊降51%–92%；DAPS与PnP-DM+UAL的集成均值PSNR比BlindDPS和Fast Diffusion EM高6.2–6.4 dB。消融中优于同迹各向同性膨胀与最佳固定标量数据加权（PnP-DM高3.3 dB，DAPS高4.2 dB）。

Conclusion: 显式建模参数不确定性可显著提升扩散后验采样器的重建精度与集成预测质量，且具即插即用性与理论误差保证。

Abstract: Diffusion posterior samplers reconstruct signals by combining a pretrained diffusion prior with a measurement likelihood specified by a forward model and a noise model. Calibration and reference data can characterize uncertainty in the forward model's parameters, such as blur kernels or detector responses. Fixing these parameters at estimated values leaves their uncertainty out of the likelihood, and parameter errors are thus absorbed into the reconstruction. We introduce an uncertainty-aware likelihood (UAL), a Gaussian approximation to the marginal likelihood obtained by integrating over the uncertain parameters. For each candidate signal, UAL propagates parameter uncertainty into a structured measurement covariance, reducing the quadratic penalty along residual directions that parameter variation can explain. Existing samplers can use UAL in their data updates while retaining their pretrained diffusion priors and sampling schedules. Under a fixed Gaussian nuisance model, we bound likelihood error from linearization and average divergence between the exact marginal and UAL posteriors. We compare samplers with and without UAL across six inverse problems spanning Gaussian and motion deblurring, CT, MRI, ultrasound, and fluorescence microscopy, and find improvements in reconstruction and ensemble prediction. UAL reduces geometric mean MSE by 9% to 20% across samplers in the main Gaussian-deblurring setting and by 51% to 92% in motion deblurring when non-blind samplers use the population-mean kernel. In motion deblurring, DAPS and PnP-DM with UAL also give 6.2 to 6.4 dB higher ensemble-mean PSNR than published BlindDPS and Fast Diffusion EM. On the ablation set, UAL outperforms trace-matched isotropic inflation for PnP-DM in both deblurring tasks; in motion deblurring, it also exceeds the best-tested fixed scalar data weighting by 3.3 dB for PnP-DM and 4.2 dB for DAPS in average PSNR.

</details>


### [30] [From Canon to Nikon: Camera Domain Shift and Potential Equity Implications in Retinal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29104)
*Lisa Y. W. Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究揭示相机平台是视网膜AI性能变异中被忽视的来源，跨相机差异无法通过提高输入分辨率或更换分类器稳定消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病视网膜病变是可预防性失明的主要病因，但全球范围内定期筛查的可及性有限；AI虽可扩展筛查能力，其性能却对图像质量与采集条件敏感，而部署环境中的异构成像设备可能带来被忽视的系统性偏差。

Method: 基于巴西8,524例患者队列，比较Nikon NF5050与Canon CR-2两种相机的图像质量不足率及成本差异；采用冻结的预训练视网膜基础模型，在12项视网膜疾病分类任务、4种输入分辨率和5种下游分类器上进行系统性评估，检验相机相关性能差异是否持续存在。

Result: Nikon NF5050采集图像出现质量不达标的概率是Canon CR-2的两倍以上，而翻新Canon系统的成本亦为Nikon系统的两倍以上，呈现潜在的成本—质量权衡；相机相关性能差异在不同任务间差异显著，且提高输入分辨率或更换分类器均无法一致性地缓解跨相机变异。

Conclusion: 相机平台是视网膜AI性能变异的重要且常被忽视的来源，对异构成像系统部署下的安全性与可靠性构成影响；作者公开全部源代码、评估脚本及逐任务结果以支持可复现基准测试。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy is a leading cause of preventable blindness, yet access to regular screening remains limited worldwide. AI offers a scalable approach, but its performance can be sensitive to image quality and acquisition conditions. In a Brazilian cohort of 8,524 patients, retinal images acquired with the Nikon NF5050 were more than twice as likely to have inadequate image quality compared with those acquired with the Canon CR-2, despite refurbished Canon systems costing more than twice as much as Nikon systems. This potential cost-quality trade-off highlights an important consideration for the reliability of AI systems deployed across heterogeneous imaging systems. Using a frozen pretrained retinal foundation model, we systematically evaluate whether camera-associated performance differences persist across 12 retinal disease classification tasks, four input resolutions, and five downstream classifiers. We find that camera-associated performance differences vary substantially across tasks, and that neither higher input resolution nor classifier choice consistently mitigates cross-camera variation. These findings identify camera platform as a potentially overlooked source of performance variability, with implications for the safety and reliability of retinal AI when deployed across heterogeneous imaging systems. We release all source code, evaluation scripts, and per-task results on GitHub to support reproducible benchmarking.

</details>


### [31] [DriftNeRF: Cross-Ray Density Drifting for Sparse-View Neural Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29106)
*Juntang Wang,Yihan Wang,Sheng'en Li,Hao Wu,Dongmian Zou,Shixin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出现有NeRF渲染损失无跨射线密度一致性项，导致稀疏视角下密度矛盾；提出DriftNeRF正则器，直接测量并抑制跨射线密度不一致，在稀疏视角下提升PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRF在密集视角下能合成逼真新视角，但视角稀疏时性能急剧下降。作者将这一缺陷归因于优化目标本身：渲染损失是独立射线上的求和，不含比较不同射线对同一空间区域所赋密度的项，导致相邻3D点可收敛到相互矛盾的密度，而每个训练像素仍被正确重建。

Method: 作者在训练中直接测量跨射线密度不一致（cross-ray density disagreement），并验证其在vanilla NeRF中持续偏高、在频率正则下仅偶然下降、且在稀疏设置下与留出质量同向变化。随后提出DriftNeRF：一个即插即用正则器，在密度样本上实例化drifting-field模板——用掩码指数核对其他射线上邻近点的密度进行聚合，形成stop-gradient共识目标；所得drifting场惩罚无支撑的不一致，同时保留已达成一致的边界。该正则器仅需采样位置、射线ID与预测密度，无需预训练模型、外部几何或额外图像，并可跨场参数化迁移。

Result: 在NeRF-Synthetic上将残余密度不一致降低一个数量级；在NeRF-Synthetic、DTU、LLFF上，3-view PSNR较最强MLP-NeRF基线提升0.42–0.65 dB，并在匹配协议下与稀疏视角高斯管线保持竞争力，且无需其深度、光流或共视预处理。

Conclusion: 稀疏视角NeRF退化的一个可测根源在于渲染损失缺乏跨射线密度一致性约束；DriftNeRF以轻量、通用、无需外部先验的正则化方式显式闭合该缺口，显著改善稀疏视角重建质量。

Abstract: Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) synthesize photorealistic novel views from dense image collections, but degrade sharply when few views are available. We attribute a specific, measurable defect to the objective itself: the rendering loss is a sum over independent rays and contains no term comparing densities that different rays assign to the same region of space, so nearby 3D points can settle on contradictory densities while every training pixel is reproduced correctly. We make this quantity explicit by measuring cross-ray density disagreement directly during training, and show that it stays high in vanilla NeRF, falls only incidentally under frequency-based regularization, and moves together with held-out quality in the sparse regime. We then close the gap with DriftNeRF, a drop-in regularizer that instantiates the drifting-field template on density samples: a masked exponential kernel aggregates densities from nearby points on other rays into a stop-gradient consensus target, and the resulting drifting field penalizes unsupported disagreement while leaving agreed-upon boundaries intact. The regularizer reads only sampled positions, ray identifiers, and predicted densities, so it requires no pretrained model, external geometry, or additional images, and transfers across field parameterizations. On NeRF-Synthetic it reduces residual disagreement by an order of magnitude, and across NeRF-Synthetic, DTU, and LLFF it improves 3-view PSNR over the strongest MLP-NeRF baseline by 0.42-0.65 dB and remains competitive with sparse-view Gaussian pipelines under a matched protocol without their depth, flow, or covisibility preprocessing.

</details>


### [32] [PhyMo: Learning Physical Dynamics with Accurate and Continuous Motion from Multi-View Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29107)
*Shangjia Liu,Jinxi Li,Siyuan Zhou,Bo Yang,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyMo 提出运动感知轨迹表示与双向运动学连续性约束，从多视角视频中恢复可信的几何、外观与物理运动状态，避免外观捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠渲染监督时，模型会通过改变颜色或不透明度来补偿不准确的形变，从而"拟合出"合理图像而非真实运动；在快速或局部复杂运动场景下尤为严重，导致重建不可靠、未来外推不连续。

Method: 提出 PhyMo 框架：1) Motion-Aware Trajectory Representation，强约束模型从形变中恢复准确运动而非依赖外观捷径；2) Continuous Motion Evolution 模块，配合双向运动学连续性约束，使位移、速度、加速度在时间上一致演化。

Result: 在四个动态场景基准上取得 state-of-the-art 性能，并实现几何忠实的插值与连续的运动外推。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离运动与外观、并施加运动学连续性约束，可有效缓解外观欺骗问题，提升动态重建与未来预测的可靠性。

Abstract: Recovering dynamic 3D scene geometry, appearance, and the physical motion states that govern scene evolution from multi-view videos is important yet challenging. A key difficulty is that appearance can be deceptive. Under rendering supervision alone, a model may fit the observed images by changing color or opacity to compensate for inaccurate deformation, rather than recovering the true evolving geometry and motion. This issue is especially severe in scenes with fast or locally complex motion, where inaccurate trajectories can still produce plausible images over the observed frames, but lead to unreliable reconstruction and discontinuous future prediction. In this paper, we propose PhyMo, a framework for learning evolving dynamics from multi-view videos. We introduce a Motion-Aware Trajectory Representation that drives the model to recover accurate motion from deformation, rather than relying on appearance shortcuts to explain observed motion. On top of this, we propose a Continuous Motion Evolution module with bidirectional kinematic continuity constraint to promote temporally consistent evolution of displacement, velocity, and acceleration. Together, these designs yield geometrically faithful interpolation and continuous motion extrapolation. Experiments on four dynamic scene benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [33] [WaFIR: Wavelet-Fourier Mixture-of-Experts with Subband-Adaptive Routing for Blind All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29108)
*Trong Nhan Nguyen,Vu Hoang Minh Doan,Thanh Dat Le,Truong Tien Vo,Thi Thu Ha Vu,Tan Hung Vo,Jaeyeop Choi,Junghwan Oh,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WaFIR 解码器模块，将特征分解为 Haar 子带，用傅里叶描述子进行子带级路由，驱动提示条件化的频谱调制专家，在盲一体化图像复原中取得稳定提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲一体化图像复原需在无任务标签条件下用共享模型处理异构甚至混合退化；现有提示/图像级频率条件化难以做频率特异处理，故需在子带层面进行路由以兼顾频率特异性与骨干学到的任务上下文

Method: WaFIR 解码器：Haar 小波分解特征为子带→对每个子带用紧凑傅里叶描述子概括→据描述子路由至提示条件化的频谱调制专家；在去噪、去雨、去雾统一训练与评测协议下评测，并设匹配容量与路由消融控制

Result: 三任务平均 PSNR 较 PromptIR 基线提升 0.48 dB、较 Up-Restorer 提升 0.38 dB；匹配容量与路由控制表明增益非仅来自额外参数；未见噪声强度下基于描述子的路由比离散标签条件化更鲁棒，复合退化下持平；路由分析显示跨退化类型存在稳定的子带依赖专家使用

Conclusion: 子带频率结构可作为提示条件化的可解释补充，用于盲图像复原

Abstract: Blind all-in-one image restoration must adapt a shared model to heterogeneous and sometimes mixed degradations without a task label at test time. We introduce WaFIR, a decoder module that decomposes features into Haar subbands, summarizes each subband with a compact Fourier descriptor, and routes it to prompt-conditioned spectral modulation experts. Unlike image-level frequency conditioning, WaFIR makes routing decisions at the subband level, enabling frequency-specific processing while retaining the task context learned by the backbone. Under a unified training and evaluation protocol for denoising, deraining, and dehazing, WaFIR improves average PSNR over the three tasks by 0.48 dB over the PromptIR baseline and by 0.38 dB over the recent Up-Restorer. Matched-capacity and routing controls show that the gain is not explained by additional parameters alone. On unseen noise intensities, descriptor-based routing is more robust than a discrete label-conditioned alternative and remains on par under compound degradations, while routing analyses show stable subband-dependent expert use across degradation types. These results support subband frequency structure as an interpretable complement to prompt-based conditioning for blind image restoration. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/WaFIR/README.md

</details>


### [34] [Two for One: Privileged Learning for Resource-Constrained Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29126)
*Lisa Y. W. Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种特权学习框架：训练时教师模型利用双眼CFP图像，学生模型仅以单眼图像为输入并迁移教师的双侧知识；推理时只需单眼，即可在AMD和DR检测上稳定超越常规单眼训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双眼CFP可提供互补信息用于眼病评估，但现有方法（双输入融合模型或双输入教师蒸馏出的轻量学生）在测试时仍依赖双眼图像；而实际中双侧采集未必可行，需要在推理时仅用单眼、又能利用训练期双侧信息的方案。

Method: 特权学习（privileged learning）框架：教师模型在训练阶段接收双侧CFP图像，学习受双眼信息引导的表征；学生模型仅以单眼图像为输入，通过知识蒸馏方式迁移教师的双侧知识；推理阶段学生仅使用单眼图像，无需对侧图像。

Result: 与传统单眼训练相比，AUC一致性提升；左眼图像增益最大：AMD检测AUC 0.931→0.966，DR检测AUC 0.789→0.836，且推理仅需单眼图像。

Conclusion: 通过在训练期利用双侧信息、推理期仅需单眼图像，该框架可在资源受限的临床与筛查环境中支持更可靠的模型部署。

Abstract: Machine learning systems are often developed under data-rich conditions but deployed when only incomplete information is available. A natural example arises in colour fundus photography (CFP), where both eyes are commonly imaged because they can provide complementary information for ocular disease assessment. Prior approaches that exploit bilateral information either fuse both eyes in a single model or use knowledge distillation to compress a dual-eye teacher into a lightweight student that still requires both images at test time. However, bilateral acquisition may not always be feasible. We thus propose a privileged learning framework in which a teacher model has access to bilateral CFP images during training and learns representations informed by information from both eyes, while the student is trained to transfer this knowledge from the bilateral teacher using only a single-eye image as input. At inference, the student operates exclusively on this single-eye input and does not require the contralateral image. Experimental results show consistent improvements in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve over conventional monocular training, with the largest gains observed for left-eye images for the detection of age-related macular degeneration (0.931 vs. 0.966) and diabetic retinopathy (0.789 vs. 0.836).%, while requiring only a single-eye image at inference. By enabling models to benefit from bilateral information during training while requiring only a single-eye image at inference, our approach may support more reliable deployment in resource-constrained clinical and screening environments.

</details>


### [35] [DGCM-SSFA: Dual-Granularity Cross-Modulation and Sparse Strip Frequency Attention for Efficient 3T-to-7T MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29141)
*Zhichun Fan,Zhiqiang Chen,Lanling Liu,Lingjun Liu,Zhonghua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出紧凑多域注意力框架DGCM-SSFA，仅11M参数即可将常规3T MRI合成为高保真7T MRI，性能接近大模型基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3T到7T MRI合成方法依赖超大架构（>45M参数）或计算昂贵的集成策略，难以部署于资源受限的临床环境，亟需在参数量与合成质量间取得更优权衡。
