Method: 在U-Net Transformer主干中集成两个互补模块：多个层级嵌入双粒度交叉调制特征调制器（DGCM），通过Sobel引导的空间注意力与深度可分离的频率感知通道注意力配合跨分支门控增强局部边缘与纹理细节；瓶颈处设置稀疏条带频率注意力（SSFA），以条带池化注意力和近线性复杂度捕获全局空间上下文，同时用FFT进行频域谱调制。二者与主干原生通道注意力协同，形成三重粒度特征精炼流程。

Result: 在UNC与BNU的3T→7T数据集、T1w与T2w模态上验证：BNU T2w达到SSIM 0.8431（最佳基准0.8624），BNU T1w为0.8100，结构保真度强；参数量显著少于ResViT（123.4M）和FS-RWKV（45.4M）等Transformer方法。

Conclusion: DGCM-SSFA以约四分之一至十分之一的参数量实现接近SOTA的7T MRI合成质量，证明多域注意力设计可在紧凑规模下兼顾细节保真与全局建模，具备临床部署潜力。

Abstract: Synthesizing high-fidelity 7T MRI from routinely acquired 3T scans offers a practical pathway to enhanced neuroimaging without the prohibitive cost of ultra-high-field scanners. While existing deep learning approaches have achieved promising results, state-of-the-art methods typically rely on either large-scale architectures exceeding 45 million parameters or computationally intensive ensemble strategies, limiting their practicality in resource-constrained clinical environments. In this work, we propose a compact multi-domain attention framework, termed DGCM-SSFA, that achieves competitive synthesis quality with only 11M parameters. The proposed architecture integrates two complementary modules into a U-Net Transformer backbone. First, a Dual-Granularity Cross-Modulated Feature Modulator (DGCM) is embedded at multiple hierarchical levels to enhance local edge and texture details through Sobel-guided spatial attention and depthwise frequency-aware channel attention with cross-branch gating. Second, a Sparse Strip Frequency Attention (SSFA) module at the bottleneck captures global spatial context via strip-pooled attention with near-linear complexity, while simultaneously performing FFT-based spectral modulation in the frequency domain. These two modules operate synergistically with the backbone's native channel attention, forming a triple-granularity feature refinement pipeline. Extensive experiments on the UNC and BNU 3T-to-7T MRI datasets across T1w and T2w modalities demonstrate that DGCM-SSFA achieves strong structural fidelity, attaining an SSIM of 0.8431 on BNU T2w---approaching the best benchmark value of 0.8624---and 0.8100 on BNU T1w, while using substantially fewer parameters than Transformer-based alternatives such as ResViT (123.4M) and FS-RWKV (45.4M).

</details>


### [36] [From Augmentation to Synthetic Evidence: Evaluating Generated Chest Radiographs at Dataset Intake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29152)
*Shaikh Akib Shahriyar,Zinedine Sehili,Adam Arthur,Christopher Schwartz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文区分"通过自动化质量检查"、"来源分类器判定为生成"与"可信记录支持采集声明"三个问题，实验表明选择策略能提升质量通过率，却无证据表明能同时通过两项像素级检查；即使未处理，仍有152张合成图像同时通过两项检查，且JPEG压缩使AUROC从0.987降至0.841。作者主张对胸片数据入库必须单独记录并核验来源与预期用途。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成的胸片若作为声明性数据增强是有用的，但同一张图像若被当作真实检查记录提交则成为无支撑证据。现有实践往往把"质量合格"或"来源检测"混同于"证据可信"，作者希望厘清这些检查各自能承担什么、不能承担什么，从而为医学数据集的准入提供明确边界。

Method: 将问题拆为三项独立判据：自动化质量检查、来源分类器标记、可信记录支撑采集声明。实验由Qwen从1,000张Cheff-identified或SynthCheX候选池中最多选出20张图像；接收方用MedGemma做质量评估，并用基于冻结DINOv3特征的分类器预测来源。比较均匀选择、质量选择与质量约束代理选择三种策略；在未处理测试图像上评估分类器的误报与漏报，并考察JPEG压缩对SynthCheX-对-NIH AUROC的影响。

Result: 质量选择将Cheff-identified图像的质检通过数由12/20提升至19/20，SynthCheX由8/20提升至17/20。但没有证据表明选择能提升双检查同时通过率：所选Cheff-identified图像无一同时通过两项检查；均匀、质量、质量约束代理三种选择下SynthCheX同时通过数分别为2、1、2。未处理测试图上，分类器在5,000张SynthCheX中标记4,677张，但同时把2,297张NIH真实图像中的110张、及另一数据集957张真实图像中的568张误判为假。JPEG压缩使SynthCheX-对-NIH AUROC从0.987降至0.841。即便未经处理，仍有152张SynthCheX图像同时通过两项像素检查。

Conclusion: 像素级检查可用于过滤提交，但无法验证采集声明的真实性：质量与来源检测各自可被满足，却不等价于证据可信，且对压缩等后处理脆弱、对真实图像存在显著误报。因此强烈主张在医学数据集准入胸片时，将来源与预期用途作为独立字段记录并单独核验。

Abstract: A generated chest radiograph can be useful as declared augmentation, but the same image becomes unsupported evidence when submitted as the record of a real examination. We study this problem by separating three questions: whether an image passes an automated quality check, whether an origin classifier flags it as generated, and whether a trusted record supports the claimed acquisition. In our experiments, Qwen selects up to 20 images from pools of 1,000 existing Cheff-identified or SynthCheX candidates. A receiver evaluates quality with MedGemma and predicted origin with a classifier on frozen DINOv3 features. Quality-based selection raises quality passes from 12 to 19 of 20 for Cheff-identified images and from 8 to 17 for SynthCheX. We find no evidence that selection improves passage through both checks: no selected Cheff-identified image passes both, while uniform, quality, and quality-constrained surrogate selection produce 2, 1, and 2 SynthCheX passes. On unprocessed test images, the origin classifier flags 4,677 of 5,000 SynthCheX images, but it also misclassify 110 of 2,297 NIH (real) images and 568 of 957 genuine images from another collection as fake. Our results further show that JPEG compression reduces SynthCheX-versus-NIH AUROC from 0.987 to 0.841. Even without processing, 152 SynthCheX images pass both pixel checks. These checks can filter submissions, but they cannot verify an acquisition claim. We strongly advocate that source and intended use must be recorded and checked separately for admission of chest radiographs in medical datasets.

</details>


### [37] [SICAF: Time-Varying Focus Bottleneck for Self-Supervised Event-Based Optical Flow with Spiking Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29158)
*Shuangming Yang,Qing He,Shangqi Guo,Badong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SICAF 自监督脉冲神经网络框架，通过“紧凑压缩、时变聚焦”的约束优化策略实现事件相机光流估计，在 MVSEC 上 AEE 提升 8.14%，白盒对抗鲁棒性提升 13.49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件光流依赖自监督学习弥补标签缺失，SNN 与事件异步特性天然契合；但事件流的时间不规则性阻碍紧凑时空特征提取，且缺少显式标签易导致伪相关，使潜在表示冗余度上升。

Method: 将学习过程建模为带时间调制的约束优化，鼓励自监督 SNN 提取紧凑潜在表示并抑制过度压缩（避免丢失时间显著性）；设计带可学习时间注意力的估计器，实现跨时间步的特征选择性与上下文依赖建模。

Result: 在多个数据集上取得自监督 SNN 的最优性能：MVSEC 数据集 AEE 提升 8.14%，白盒对抗攻击下鲁棒性提升 13.49%。

Conclusion: 信息压缩与时变聚焦的联合约束可有效缓解事件流时空冗余与伪相关问题，为自监督脉冲光流估计提供紧凑且鲁棒的学习范式。

Abstract: Event-based optical flow estimation achieves high temporal resolution and dynamic range by exploiting asynchronous events, while self-supervised learning provides an effective solution to the absence of ground-truth labels. Spiking neural networks (SNNs), as brain-inspired models, process information through asynchronous spikes, naturally aligning with optical flow events. However, the temporal irregularity across event streams hinders the compact extraction of spatiotemporal features, while the absence of explicit labels leads to spurious correlations, thereby increasing redundancy within latent representations. In this paper, we propose a novel 'Compactly Compress, Time-varying Focus' training strategy. We present SICAF (Spikes Information Compression And Focus) framework that formulates the learning process as a constrained optimization with temporal modulation. It encourages self-supervised SNNs to extract compact latent representations while preventing over-compression that would discard temporal saliency. To better enable feature selectivity across time steps, we design an estimator with learnable temporal attention, achieving context awareness with cross-time dependencies. Experimental results on several datasets demonstrate that SICAF achieves state-of-the-art performance in self-supervised SNNs, with a 8.14% improvement in AEE on the MVSEC dataset and a 13.49% increase in robustness under white-box adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [38] [RiO-DETRv2: Geometry-Aware Uncertainty Modeling Meets Real-Time Oriented Object Detection Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29159)
*Zhangchi Hu,Zilong He,Yifan Zhao,Xiangyu Yu,Jie Chen,Peixi Wu,Yansong Peng,Hebei Li,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RiO-DETRv2几何感知不确定性建模框架，通过三项设计改进实时端到端旋转目标检测的定位精度，几乎不增加计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RiO-DETR是首个实时端到端Transformer旋转目标检测器，但进一步提升定位性能需要更强的边界框表示。将分布回归从水平检测迁移到旋转检测时，会出现几何失配的边界建模与拓扑不一致的朝向细化两大问题。

Method: 1) Object-Aligned Boundary Distribution Refinement：在目标对齐坐标系中将框边界表示为概率分布，并跨解码器层渐进细化，解决几何失配；2) Circular-to-Local Angular Residual Refinement：从全局周期搜索过渡到局部精细估计，保证朝向拓扑一致性；3) Unified OBB Quality-Aligned Learning：将分类、边界与角度细化同完整OBB定位质量对齐。

Result: RiO-DETRv2-l/x在DOTA上达到53.7/54.1 mAP50-95，较RiO-DETR-l/x分别提升1.2/1.5个点，计算开销几乎不变。

Conclusion: 所提设计在不牺牲效率的前提下提升定位精度，实现速度与精度更好的平衡，并可公开复现。

Abstract: RiO-DETR is the first real-time end-to-end transformer for oriented object detection, yet further localization gains require a more expressive box representation. Although distributional regression has succeeded in horizontal DETRs, extending it to oriented detection introduces geometry-misaligned boundary modeling and topology-inconsistent orientation refinement. We present RiO-DETRv2, a geometry-aware uncertainty modeling framework for real-time oriented object detection. To resolve the geometry mismatch, Object-Aligned Boundary Distribution Refinement represents box boundaries as probability distributions in an object-aligned coordinate system and progressively refines them across decoder layers. For better orientation topology consistency, we introduce Circular-to-Local Angular Residual Refinement that transitions from global periodic search to local fine-grained estimation. Unified OBB Quality-Aligned Learning further aligns classification, boundary and angular refinement with complete-OBB localization quality. Together, these designs improve localization without compromising efficiency, achieving a better balance between speed and accuracy. Specifically, RiO-DETRv2-l/x achieves 53.7/54.1 mAP50-95 on DOTA, outperforming RiO-DETR-l/x by 1.2/1.5 points with nearly unchanged computational cost. Code is available at https://github.com/RicePasteM/RiO-DETRv2.

</details>


### [39] [LocalAgent: Collaborative Agentic Verification for Fine-Grained Instance-Level Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29174)
*Jiachen Guo,Xinshan Zhu,Boyun Wang,Yanyan Liang,Jun Wan,Feng Lin,Ajian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LocalAgent，一个由通用 MLLM 与轻量专家验证器协作的智能体框架，用于细粒度实例级一致性验证，并以辅助信号封顶的强化微调策略学习"何时需要验证"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 在通用视觉理解上进展迅速，但在实例级细粒度一致性验证（判断两图是否为同一物理对象，需应对视角、光照、背景变化）上仍表现不佳。该任务既需全局语义感知，又需对微小可疑区域的精确局部证据检查——语义相似的物体可能仅在小范围内存在差异。单一通用 MLLM 难以兼顾二者。

Method: 协作式智能体框架：MLLM 先进行全局推理并定位可疑局部区域，再委托给局部证据验证器（LEV）工具包做细粒度匹配。LEV 含两模块：局部外观一致性（LAC）在光照与颜色变化下估计外观相似度；局部几何对应（LGC）在视角变化下估计空间对应关系。LEV 返回结构化标量证据，由 MLLM 整合并做出最终判定。训练采用辅助信号封顶的强化微调（auxiliary-capped RFT）：以答案正确性为主导，工具使用与格式等辅助信号权重受限，使策略学会何时有必要调用验证器。

Result: 相较最强 CoT-RFT 基线，平均准确率提升 4.9%；仅在 12.13% 的样本上调用验证器，同时保留底层 MLLM 的通用多模态能力。代码已开源（https://github.com/Firefly-zoo/LocalAgent-main）。

Conclusion: 将全局语义推理与专用局部验证工具解耦并协同，配合"正确性优先、辅助信号封顶"的 RFT 策略，可在显著降低工具调用成本的前提下提升细粒度实例级一致性验证性能，且不损害 MLLM 的通用能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress in general visual understanding, yet they still struggle with fine-grained instance-level consistency verification, where the goal is to determine whether two images depict the exact same physical object under varying viewpoints, illumination, and backgrounds. This task requires both global semantic perception and precise examination of subtle local evidence, since semantically similar objects may differ only in small suspicious regions. To address this challenge, we propose LocalAgent, a collaborative agentic framework that integrates the complementary strengths of a generalist MLLM and lightweight specialist verifiers. The MLLM first reasons globally to identify suspicious local regions, which are then delegated to a Local Evidence Verifier (LEV) toolkit for fine-grained appearance and geometric matching. LEV comprises Local Appearance Consistency (LAC), which estimates appearance similarity under illumination and color changes, and Local Geometric Correspondence (LGC), which estimates spatial correspondence under viewpoint changes. LEV returns structured scalar evidence that the MLLM integrates for the final decision. We further introduce an auxiliary-capped reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) strategy, in which answer correctness dominates auxiliary tool-use and format signals, enabling the policy to learn when verification is necessary. Experiments show that LocalAgent improves mean accuracy by 4.9% over the strongest CoT-RFT baseline while invoking a verifier on only 12.13% of samples and preserving the general multimodal capabilities of the underlying MLLM. The open-source repository is available at https://github.com/Firefly-zoo/LocalAgent-main.

</details>


### [40] [ROCOCO: Robust Outline Completion of Occluded Curvilinear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29177)
*Ken Zheng,Jaimyn Drake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出学习式生成模型 ROCOCO，用 flow matching 在无遮挡掩码先验条件下补全被环境障碍物遮挡的曲线特征，性能优于现有 inpainting 基线且参数量更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线特征（如道路、河流、线状目标）常被环境障碍物遮挡，需要补全被遮挡区域的曲线；现有 inpainting 方法依赖已知遮挡掩码这一特权信息，实际场景难以获得，故需摆脱对该先验的依赖。

Method: 1）采用基于 flow matching 的生成式补全器，实现遮挡区域的曲线特征 inpainting；2）额外训练 UNet 掩码预测模型，仅凭被遮挡观测即可识别需补全区域，从而消除对遮挡掩码先验的依赖；3）在程序化生成测试用例上验证各组件，在大型公共曲线对象数据集上验证完整流程，并与现成 inpainting 基线对比。

Result: 无遮挡掩码时，ROCOCO 大幅领先所有基线；提供掩码时与掩码监督式 inpainting 相当：全图误差最优，遮挡区域误差仅次于 LaMa；补全器仅 7.7M 参数，远小于 LaMa 的 51M 和 Stable Diffusion 的 860M。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不依赖遮挡掩码先验的情况下实现高质量曲线特征补全，兼顾精度与参数效率，验证了掩码预测+flow matching 补全流水线的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a learning-based generative model for completing curvilinear features under occlusion by environmental obstacles. To this end, we adopt a flow matching-based approach that supports the inpainting of occluded regions with the appropriate curvilinear features. To eliminate the reliance on privileged information about the occlusion a priori, we also train a UNet-based mask prediction model, which can identify regions for inpainting given only the occluded observation. The components of our approach are validated via experiments using procedurally-generated test cases, and the complete pipeline is validated using large public datasets for curvilinear objects. Against off-the-shelf inpainting baselines, ROCOCO is the strongest method by a wide margin when no occlusion mask is given, and is competitive with mask-supervised inpainting when one is, achieving best on full-image error and second only to LaMa on the occluded region, while using a completer of $7.7$M parameters against LaMa's $51$M and Stable Diffusion's $860$M.

</details>


### [41] [A Unified Evaluation Framework of Physical Adversarial Robustness in the Edge VLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29196)
*Eric Liang,Alan Luo,Kaiwen Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了紧凑型grounding VLMs在物理对抗场景下的鲁棒性，提出统一指标cASR以公平比较VLM与检测器，发现VLM可与检测器一样脆弱，且失效模式主要由输出格式而非架构家族决定，位移与替换类失效对下游系统更危险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM正被部署于边缘端安全关键系统，但其物理对抗鲁棒性缺乏系统研究；同时VLM的生成式输出与检测器的结构化输出存在本质差异，导致难以进行公平评估。因此需要构建跨模型家族的公平比较框架。

Method: 以"佩戴在人体上的对抗补丁是否使模型漏检"为统一任务，在所有代表性模型上测试；提出条件攻击成功率cASR（仅在匹配灰色遮挡物下模型仍能定位的图像上统计），并将失败划分为漏检（无框）、替换（框落在补丁上）与位移（框落在两者之外）三类。

Result: 紧凑型grounding VLM的物理对抗脆弱性与目标检测器相当；失效模式主要与模型输出格式相关而非架构家族；三类失效中仅漏检可被下游系统察觉，替换与位移输出的是置信且格式良好的框，潜在危害更大；在分布偏移下，原本以漏检为主的VLM会更多转向位移/替换失效。

Conclusion: grounding VLM可能继承检测器的脆弱性，并有可能呈现更危险的失效模式，因此物理对抗评估需纳入失效模式分类而非仅统计攻击成功率。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable end-to-end visual grounding via language prompts and generative decoding, demonstrating largely improved zero-shot and open-vocabulary detection capabilities. As a result, VLMs are rapidly being deployed at the edge in safety-critical real-world systems. However, the physical adversarial robustness of edge VLMs has not been systematically studied, and the fundamental differences in output with modern object detectors create methodological challenges for fair evaluation. To compare the two families directly, we evaluate every selected representative model on one outcome: whether an adversarial patch worn on a person evades detection of the model. We report this by introducing a unified metric, conditional attack success rate (cASR), counted only on images the model still localizes under a matched gray occluder, and partition every failure into omission (no box), substitution (a box on the patch), and displacement (a box on neither). We benchmark the physical adversarial vulnerability of compact grounding VLMs against a range of object detectors, and find that the VLMs can be as vulnerable as the detectors. We disclose that failure mode is largely associated with the output format of a model rather than its architecture family. Of the three, only omission is visible to a downstream system; displacement and substitution return confident, well-formed boxes, making them potentially more dangerous failures. Further, we find that, under distribution shift, VLMs that primarily failed by omission instead shift to failing more by displacement/substitution. Thus, a grounding VLM can inherit the detector's vulnerability while showing the possibility to adopt more dangerous failure modes. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/ericliang1/physical-adversarial-robustness.

</details>


### [42] [NeuralSRender: G-Buffer Guided Video Diffusion for Sim-to-Real Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29200)
*Yipeng Lu,Youqi Liao,Zhenyu Wang,Yuning Peng,Zhen Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuralSRender：基于预训练视频扩散DiT的仿真到真实视频翻译框架，用G-buffer几何先验注入+两阶段训练，仅1.45%参数开销，提升合成视频真实感并显著改善下游感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知缺乏高质量标注数据；仿真器可大规模生成数据，但合成视频真实感不足，导致真实场景感知性能下降。核心挑战在于提升真实感的同时保持与源场景的几何一致性。

Method: 基于预训练视频扩散Transformer（DiT）；用轻量projector注入来自G-buffers的几何与材质先验；采用LoRA进行参数高效适配。两阶段训练：第一阶段在带精确G-buffer的合成视频上学习G-buffer条件；第二阶段用真实视频及估计G-buffer适配真实外观，此时冻结projector与第一阶LoRA，仅训练额外LoRA以减少估计噪声干扰。构建GenReal大规模驾驶视频数据集（合成+真实视频及对应G-buffer）。

Result: 真实感提升且几何一致性具竞争力；仅1.45%参数开销，可在24GB显存消费级GPU训练。用翻译视频训练感知模型：nuImages检测AP50由35.1升至40.6，Cityscapes分割mIoU由32.4升至40.8。

Conclusion: NeuralSRender以极低参数代价实现几何一致的仿真到真实视频翻译，有效缓解合成数据真实感瓶颈，并显著提升下游检测与分割性能；配套发布GenReal数据集及代码、权重。

Abstract: The scarcity of high-quality annotated visual data remains a critical bottleneck for autonomous-driving perception. Simulators provide training data at scale, but the limited realism of synthetic videos compromises perception performance in real-world applications. To address this limitation, we propose NeuralSRender, a sim-to-real video translation framework that enhances the photorealism of synthetic videos while maintaining geometric consistency with the source scenes. Built on a pretrained video diffusion transformer (DiT), NeuralSRender uses a lightweight projector to inject geometric and material priors from G-buffers, and LoRA enables parameter-efficient adaptation. The model is trained in two stages: it first learns G-buffer conditioning from synthetic videos with accurate G-buffers, then adapts to real-world appearance using real videos with estimated G-buffers. During the second stage, an additional LoRA module is trained while the projector and first-stage LoRA remain frozen to reduce interference from estimation noise. NeuralSRender introduces only 1.45\% parameter overhead and can be trained on consumer-grade GPUs with 24 GB of memory. To support training and evaluation, we introduce GenReal, a large-scale driving video dataset comprising synthetic and real-world videos with corresponding G-buffers. Experiments demonstrate improved photorealism with competitive geometric consistency. Training perception models on our translated videos instead of raw simulator renders improves detection $\mathrm{AP}_{50}$ on nuImages from 35.1 to 40.6 and segmentation mIoU on Cityscapes from 32.4 to 40.8. Our code, data, and model weights are available at: https://github.com/WHU-USI3DV/NeuralSRender.

</details>


### [43] [DetM-Tracker: Deterministic and Memory-Efficient VIS for Autonomous Industrial Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29212)
*You-Rui Lin,Jen-Chieh Wu,Tzu-Ti Wei,Jen-Jee Chen,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DetM-Tracker 是一个面向工业边缘部署的轻量化视频实例分割（VIS）框架，通过紧凑标量记忆、动态实例通道与熵门控跳跃连接三项组件消除架构冗余，实现约20倍推理加速与6倍显存占用降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主工业巡检要求严格确定性的实时推理，且受限于紧张的内存与算力。现有VIS模型扩展性差：稠密时序记忆随空间分辨率与序列长度增长；固定容量解码器无法利用工业场景的稀疏性，在资源受限边缘平台形成"内存墙"。同时，目标在受限"微观"视野下外观高度相似，增加了区分难度。

Method: 提出三项协同组件：(1) 紧凑标量记忆（Compact Scalar Memory），将高维实例特征压缩为四个标量描述子，把时序记忆复杂度从 O(T·N·R) 降至 O(T·N̂)，并将帧级表示压缩为活跃实例库；(2) 动态实例通道（Dynamic Instance Channels），依据实际可见目标数量自适应分配查询容量；(3) 熵门控跳跃连接（Entropy-Gated Skip Connection），将计算预算集中于高不确定性空间区域，在单次前向中细化目标边界，无需辅助细化头。

Result: 与最先进的稠密特征基线相比，推理延迟降低约20倍，显存（VRAM）消耗降低约6倍。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了现代VIS模型与工业边缘部署实际需求之间的差距，为资源受限平台上的实时自主工业巡检提供了可行的高效VIS方案。

Abstract: Autonomous industrial inspection requires strictly deterministic real-time inference under tight memory and computational constraints. However, existing Video Instance Segmentation (VIS) models suffer from prohibitive scaling: dense temporal memory grows with spatial resolution and sequence length, while fixed-capacity decoders fail to exploit the inherent sparsity of industrial scenes, creating a practical memory wall on resource-constrained edge platforms. We present DetM-Tracker, a streamlined VIS framework for autonomous industrial inspection, where target objects often exhibit highly similar appearance under constrained "micro" fields of view. DetM-Tracker eliminates architectural redundancy through three synergistic components: (1) Compact Scalar Memory, which condenses high-dimensional instance features into four-scalar descriptors, reducing temporal memory complexity from $O(T \cdot N \cdot R)$ to $O(T \cdot \hat{N})$. This mechanism effectively compresses frame-level representations into a bank of active instances, where $R$ denotes resolution, $N$ and $\hat{N}$ represent global and window-specific instance counts, respectively and $T$ the temporal window size; and (2) Dynamic Instance Channels, which adaptively allocate query capacity according to the actual number of visible objects; and (3) Entropy-Gated Skip Connection, which concentrates computational budget on high-uncertainty spatial regions to refine object boundaries in a single forward pass without auxiliary refinement heads. Our approach effectively bridges the gap between modern VIS models and the practical demands of industrial edge deployment, reducing inference latency by approximately 20$\times$ and VRAM consumption by 6$\times$ compared to state-of-the-art dense-feature baselines.

</details>


### [44] [Cache Locally, Recompute Selectively for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29229)
*Junhyeok Lee,Sangyoon Lee,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FrameBlend，一种无需训练的即插即用方法，通过查询时对选定视频token进行选择性KV重计算来解决流式视频理解中预填充上下文与查询上下文不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解要求视频大模型低延迟地处理连续视频流。现有方法在空闲时预填充帧KV缓存以降低查询时开销，但为限制无界视频流的单帧计算量而受限上下文，产生根本性上下文错配：受限上下文可能遗漏对视频token正确语境化所需的信息，同时又包含与最终查询无关的信息（预填充时查询未知）。

Method: FrameBlend在查询时用与查询相关的上下文对选定的视频token重新预填充，并用重算得到的KV替换其缓存KV。该重计算使得空闲预填充阶段可采用更短的注意力窗口，从而显著降低预填充成本并提升精度，且无需任何额外训练，可即插即用。

Result: 在三个SVU框架和五个流式与离线基准上，精度提升最高达3.9个百分点。在RVS-Ego上，每帧预填充FLOPs降低超过20%；对基于检索的框架，总处理时间减少14–59%（已包含查询时重计算成本）。

Conclusion: 通过在空闲时与查询时之间重新分配语境化的职责，这一简单改动即可改善流式视频理解的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding (SVU) requires video-LLMs to process incoming video continuously while answering user queries with low latency. Recent methods reduce query-time cost by prefilling frames into KV caches during idle time and reusing the cached KVs at query time. To bound per-frame computation over unbounded video streams, these methods restrict the context available during prefill. This creates a fundamental context mismatch: the restricted context may omit information needed to properly contextualize video tokens, yet, still contain information irrelevant to the eventual query, which is unknown during prefill. We propose FrameBlend, a simple yet effective plug-and-play method to address this mismatch through selective KV recomputation, without requiring any additional training. At query time, FrameBlend re-prefills selected video tokens using query-relevant context and replaces their cached KVs with recomputed ones. Crucially, this recomputation enables shorter attention windows during idle-time prefill, substantially reducing prefill cost while improving accuracy. Experiments across three SVU frameworks and five streaming and offline benchmarks demonstrate accuracy gains of up to 3.9 pp. On RVS-Ego, FrameBlend reduces per-frame prefill FLOPs by over 20% and total processing time by 14-59% for retrieval-based frameworks, including the cost of query-time recomputation. These results demonstrate that a simple change in how contextualization is distributed between idle time and query time can improve the accuracy-efficiency trade-off for SVU.

</details>


### [45] [DisasterRoute: Evidence-Guided Dual-System Routing for Visual Disaster Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29231)
*Tan Zhang,Xing Deng,Lu Zhang,Xiaofeng Zhu,Ping Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向灾害场景低空无人机影像VQA的双系统框架：Disaster-S1以单次前向的选项判别实现快速直接作答，DisasterRoute结合置信度与灾害证据自适应路由至DisasterVL进行选择性复核，在DisasterBench上以低延迟取得更高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机低空影像VQA需在边缘算力受限下快速准确作答以支撑灾害响应；简单问题可直接决策，难题需逐步推理，但现有工作侧重视觉理解与生成式推理能力提升，缺乏对高效直接决策及其与生成式推理自适应协同的探索。

Method: 基于适配灾害领域的视觉语言模型构建Disaster-S1，以选项判别为训练目标，在一次多模态前向中输出答案选项的概率分布以实现快速决策；提出双系统路由框架DisasterRoute，融合预测置信度与灾害证据，判定是接受初始答案还是调用灾害推理模型DisasterVL复核，实现按需计算分配。

Result: 在DisasterBench上，Disaster-S1以低延迟取得更优准确率；DisasterRoute通过选择性复核进一步提升准确率，且延迟显著低于全量推理。

Conclusion: 直接判别式决策与选择性生成式复核的按需协同，可在边缘资源约束下兼顾灾害VQA的效率与准确性。

Abstract: Visual question answering from low-altitude UAV imagery must deliver timely and accurate judgments to support disaster response under limited edge computing resources. Routine questions may be answered through direct decisions, whereas more challenging questions may benefit from step-by-step reasoning. However, existing research primarily focuses on improving models' visual understanding and generative reasoning capabilities, with limited exploration of efficient direct decision-making and its adaptive coordination with generative reasoning. We develop Disaster-S1 based on a vision-language model adapted to the disaster domain. Trained for option discrimination, it outputs a probability distribution over answer options in a single multimodal forward pass for fast decision-making. We further propose DisasterRoute, a dual-system routing framework that combines prediction confidence with disaster evidence to decide whether to accept the initial answer or invoke the disaster reasoning model DisasterVL for review. By allocating inference computation on demand, the framework retains the efficiency of direct decision-making and improves overall question-answering accuracy through selective review. Experiments on DisasterBench show that Disaster-S1 achieves superior accuracy with low latency, while DisasterRoute further improves accuracy through selective review at substantially lower latency than full reasoning.

</details>


### [46] [Think-Capture: Active 3D Geometry Acquisition for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29232)
*Junjie Wang,Zhihao Yuan,Yunhan Tang,Sheng Xu,Jiaqi Mao,Xiaoyu Ye,Yueru Luo,Xu Yan,Chun-Mei Feng,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Think-Capture，一种查询条件化的按需几何获取框架：先定位任务关键物体，再通过3D工具链与符号化3D状态抽象结合分层GRPO训练VLM，使其学会在需要时使用几何证据、必要时回退视觉线索，显著提升3D空间推理并保持扰动鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在2D识别上表现优异，但在稀疏视角下难以维持一致的3D关系，在遮挡与视角变化下容易产生空间幻觉。已有方法通常对所有帧进行密集几何融合，计算与上下文开销大，且导致模型盲目依赖不完美的几何估计器。

Method: 1）查询条件化框架：先识别任务关键物体，再按需获取其几何信息；2）3D工具包将零样本掩码提升至统一坐标系；3）符号化3D状态抽象（Symbolic 3D State Abstraction）将物体与相机状态压缩为结构化文本；4）分层组相对策略优化（Hierarchical GRPO）在“几何有帮助”与“几何误导”两类样本上训练VLM，学习何时采用工具证据、何时回退视觉线索。

Result: Think-Capture-4B在MindCube-Tiny达54.4%（较骨干+21.5），MMSI-Bench位置子集39.1%（+7.9），ViewSpatial 42.8%（+5.2）；在坐标与掩码扰动下保持鲁棒；完整系统可容纳于单张24GB GPU，并作为异步高层推理器部署于Agibot G1。

Conclusion: 按需、查询条件化的几何获取配合符号化状态抽象与分层强化学习，可在有限算力下有效缓解VLM的3D空间幻觉，并具备扰动鲁棒性与真实机器人平台可部署性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at 2D recognition but often fail to maintain consistent 3D relations across sparse viewpoints, leading to spatial hallucinations under occlusion and perspective changes. Existing methods commonly fuse all frames with dense geometry, incurring substantial computation and context overhead while encouraging blind reliance on imperfect estimators. We present Think-Capture, a query-conditioned framework that first identifies task-critical objects and then acquires their geometry on demand. A 3D toolkit lifts zero-shot masks into a common coordinate frame, and a Symbolic 3D State Abstraction compresses object and camera states into structured text. Hierarchical Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) trains the VLM on cases in which geometry either helps or misleads, teaching it when to use tool evidence and when to fall back on visual cues. Think-Capture-4B reaches 54.4% on MindCube-Tiny (+21.5 points over its backbone), 39.1% on the MMSI-Bench positional subset (+7.9), and 42.8% on ViewSpatial (+5.2). It also remains robust under coordinate and mask perturbations. The full stack fits one 24 GB GPU and serves as an asynchronous high-level reasoner on an Agibot G1. Our code is available at https://github.com/819346675/Think-Capture.

</details>


### [47] [Encoded but Unused: Bounding the Sources of VLM Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29241)
*Anat Veikherman,Mor Ventura,Nitay Calderon,Lihi Zelnik-Manor,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将VLM错误分解为"视觉表征失败"与"视觉利用失败"，提出基于轻量探针的利用失败率估计方法，并给出考虑探针不完美的理论下界。作者提出VELens（基于排序、追踪目标物体及其真值属性的跨层探针），在6,000+图像上用LLaVA-1.6-7B与Qwen2.5-VL-7B取得ROC-AUC 0.95/0.91，估计利用失败分别占错误的84.4%与70.0%（保守下界77.5%/61.0%），结论是"视觉利用"而非"视觉编码"是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多笼统归因VLM错误为"视觉能力不足"，缺乏对错误来源的可量化拆分。作者希望区分两类失败：视觉证据未被编码（表征失败）与证据已编码但未被成功使用（利用失败），从而定位真正的改进方向。

Method: 1）建立错误分解框架，将错误归为visual-representation failure与visual-utilization failure；2）用轻量线性探针估计利用失败率，并推导在探针存在假阳/假阴情况下的真实失败率理论界；3）提出VELens：一种基于rank的探针，同时在VLM各层追踪目标物体及其ground-truth属性（颜色、材质、形状）的视觉证据；4）在6,000余张图像上以LLaVA-1.6-7B与Qwen2.5-VL-7B做评测，并在低资源与未见属性设定下与替代探针比较。

Result: VELens在LLaVA-1.6-7B与Qwen2.5-VL-7B上分别达到ROC-AUC 0.95与0.91，在低资源及未见属性设定下均优于替代探针。用于错误诊断时，估计视觉利用失败占LLaVA错误的84.4%（保守下界77.5%）、占Qwen错误的70.0%（保守下界61.0%）。

Conclusion: 视觉利用而非视觉表征是VLM的主导瓶颈；提升的关键机会在于设计更好的机制来定位、路由并使用模型已经编码的视觉信息，而非仅提升视觉编码能力。VELens提供了可量化、可跨层追踪的诊断工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate why vision-language models (VLMs) produce errors by decomposing them into two sources: visual-representation failures, in which the visual evidence required for the correct answer is not encoded, and visual-utilization failures, in which this evidence is encoded but not successfully used. We propose to estimate the visual-utilization failure rate using lightweight probes and derive theoretical bounds on the true rate that account for probe imperfections. We introduce VELens (Visual Evidence Lens), a rank-based probe that traces visual evidence for both a target object and its ground-truth attribute, such as color, material, or shape, across VLM layers. We evaluate VELens on over 6,000 images using LLaVA-1.6-7B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B. Our method achieves ROC-AUC scores of 0.95 and 0.91, respectively, and outperforms alternative probes in both low-resource and unseen-attribute settings. Using it to diagnose errors, we estimate that visual-utilization failures account for 84.4% of LLaVA errors and 70.0% of Qwen errors, with conservative lower bounds of 77.5% and 61.0%, respectively. Overall, our study identifies visual utilization as the dominant bottleneck in VLMs and suggests that a central opportunity for improvement lies in developing better mechanisms to locate, route, and use the visual information they already encode.

</details>


### [48] [Failure Modes of Vision-Language Models for Human Activity Understanding in Thermal HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29244)
*Han Hu,He Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估七个可本地部署的VLM在人体中心热红外数据上的人物活动理解能力，揭示其在细粒度动作识别、时间线重建与弃答上的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热像机可在黑暗与隐私敏感场景中感知人体活动，且比RGB泄露更少身份与环境信息；随着VLM被引入具身系统做场景理解与高层规划，需明确其在热成像人机交互中的失效模式，而该问题此前研究不足。

Method: 在两个以人为中心的热红外数据集上评估七个可本地部署的VLM（含面向物理AI与机器人的模型）；诊断任务包括细粒度动作识别、活动时间线重建与对无依据答案的弃答能力；以配对的RGB录制作为性能参照，并受控改变帧采样、热红外输入呈现方式与推理设置，检验错误是否可缓解。

Result: 模型能识别粗粒度活动类别，但在咳嗽、打喷嚏、打哈欠等细粒度动作上表现不佳；生成的事件时间线不准确，并对涉及物体的动作产生幻觉；简单的热红外输入预处理与额外推理仅带来有限且依赖模型的增益；即便有明确指令，各模型弃答行为差异显著。

Conclusion: 现有VLM在热红外人类活动理解上存在系统性失效模式，可靠性不足以支撑实际机器人应用；需针对性改进细粒度动作感知、时间建模与弃答校准。

Abstract: Thermal cameras enable robots to perceive human activities in darkness while revealing less personally identifiable appearance information and fewer details of the surrounding environment than RGB cameras. As vision-language models (VLMs), originally developed for RGB imagery, are increasingly integrated into embodied systems for scene understanding and high-level planning, thermal sensing offers a promising way to extend activity perception to low-light and privacy-sensitive settings. However, the failure modes of VLMs in thermal human activity understanding for human-robot interaction (HRI) remain underexplored. We fill this gap by evaluating seven locally deployable VLMs, including models developed for physical AI and robotics, on two person-centric thermal datasets. Our diagnostic tasks examine fine-grained action recognition, activity timeline reconstruction, and the ability to withhold unsupported answers. Paired RGB recordings serve as a performance reference, while controlled variations in frame sampling, thermal input presentation, and reasoning are used to assess whether the observed errors can be mitigated. Our results show that models can recognize broad activity categories but struggle with fine-grained actions such as coughing, sneezing, and yawning. They also produce inaccurate event timelines and hallucinate actions involving objects. Simple thermal input preprocessing and additional reasoning offer only limited, model-dependent gains. Abstention behavior also varies across models even under explicit instructions, limiting their reliability for practical robotic use.

</details>


### [49] [ER-Chord: Spatially Consistent One-Step Image Editing via Equal-Energy Reliability Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29248)
*Peize Li,Hanzhen Zhao,Xingguo Xu,Yilong Fan,Zhen Tao,Bo Yin,Zhifei Liu,Qingdong He,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、免反演的 ER-Chord 框架，用相邻噪声级编辑场一致性作为局部可靠性信号，通过等能量优化在保持编辑方向与总能量不变的前提下重分配控制强度，实现空间一致的单步文本引导图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步生成模型可实现快速文本引导编辑，但会意外改动应保持不变的区域；现有时间聚合方法虽在噪声级间稳定编辑场，却使空间分配对局部可靠性变化不敏感，时间稳定本身不足以保证空间一致。

Method: 以相邻噪声级编辑场之间的一致性作为局部可靠性信号；将编辑强度分配建模为等能量优化问题，按局部可靠性重分配控制能量，同时保持原始编辑方向与总控制能量不变；该问题具唯一全局解，可利用已有场评估高效求解，无需额外去噪器调用；整体为免训练、免反演框架。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 与 TEdBench 两个基准上优于最先进的单步编辑基线，源图像保真度更佳，并在多种生成骨干网络上取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 时间稳定性需辅以可靠性驱动的空间重分配才能保证编辑一致性；等能量优化为单步编辑提供了高效、可解析的空间控制强度分配方案。

Abstract: One-step generative models enable fast text-guided image editing, yet they often introduce unintended changes to regions that should remain unchanged. Temporal aggregation stabilizes editing fields across noise levels, but leaves their spatial allocation insensitive to local variations in reliability. Consequently, temporal stability alone does not ensure spatially consistent editing. To address this lim- itation, we propose ER-Chord, a training-free and inversion-free framework for reliability-guided spatial redistribution. Our key insight is that agreement between editing fields at neighboring noise levels provides a local reliability signal for allocating editing strength. We formulate this allocation as an equal-energy op- timization that redistributes control energy according to local reliability while preserving the original editing directions and total control energy. This formu- lation admits a unique global solution that can be efficiently computed using existing field evaluations, requiring no additional denoiser calls. Experiments on two benchmarks, PIE-Bench and TEdBench, demonstrate improvements over state-of-the-art one-step editing baselines, achieving superior source preservation alongside consistent gains across multiple generative backbones.The project page is at https://github.com/Peizeli1/ER-Chord.

</details>


### [50] [CRAB: Cuboid-based Representation for As-built BIM from Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29257)
*Haoran Xiong,Jing Wang,Shanshan Pan,Zihao Yan,Minglun Gong,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRAB框架，将墙体、门窗、梁柱编码为具备IFC兼容语义与参数的建筑长方体，结合ProposalNet与关系感知Flow Matching的AssemblyNet实现从点云到可编辑BIM的组件级重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从点云重建as-built BIM需要语义化、体量化、参数化且可编辑的建筑构件，而现有scan-to-BIM流程依赖手工规则、启发式优化或人工修正，学习型方法难以恢复墙厚、开口几何及构件级BIM结构。

Method: CRAB由两部分构成：ProposalNet预测粗略的构件长方体；AssemblyNet是基于关系感知Flow Matching的精化模型，用于精化几何、预测结构关系，并支持自动结构修正与人工引导编辑。为缓解长方体级监督不足，扩展Structured3D加入构件级长方体标注并构建基于长方体的程序化场景。

Result: 在构件完整度、几何精度与泛化性上优于基于平面图与平面的方法；内墙厚度估计平均误差2.80 cm，79.50%的匹配墙体误差在3 cm以内。

Conclusion: CRAB通过长方体参数化表示与关系感知生成模型，有效衔接点云与可编辑BIM，实现组件级、可编辑的高质量BIM重建。

Abstract: As-built BIM reconstruction from point clouds demands semantic, volumetric, parametric, and editable building components, beyond 2D layouts or planar abstractions. Yet existing scan-to-BIM pipelines often rely on handcrafted rules, heuristic optimization, or manual correction, while learning-based methods fall short of recovering wall thickness, opening geometry, and component-level BIM structure. We introduce CRAB, a BIM-ready cuboid reconstruction framework that encodes walls, doors, windows, beams, and columns as compact architectural cuboids with IFC-compatible semantics and parameters. CRAB bridges point clouds and editable BIM by combining ProposalNet, which predicts coarse component cuboids, with AssemblyNet, a relation-aware Flow Matching model that refines geometry, predicts structural relationships, and enables automatic structural correction and human-guided editing. To address limited cuboid-level supervision, we extend Structured3D with component-wise cuboid annotations and construct cuboid-based procedural scenes. Experiments show CRAB surpasses floor plan and plane-based alternatives in component completeness, geometric accuracy, and generalization. For interior wall thickness estimation, CRAB achieves a mean error of 2.80 cm, with 79.50% of matched walls within 3 cm. The project page is available at https://vcc.tech/research/2026/CRAB.

</details>


### [51] [Clinical radar heart sounds with echocardiographic phenotypes of ventricular dysfunction and valvular disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29267)
*Jinye Li,Qingchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个将雷达心音与超声心动图报告配对的公开数据集：162名患者共537条雷达心音记录，覆盖LVEF<50%、节段性室壁运动异常、中重度瓣膜反流三种表型，并提供原始/处理后信号与质量分段，患者级五折评价对降低LVEF的AUROC达0.770±0.138。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达心音可无接触捕捉心脏机械活动，超声心动图可刻画心室与瓣膜功能，二者配对有望将非接触机械信号与结构性心脏病表型关联；但此类配对数据在公开资源中极为稀缺，限制了相关研究。

Method: 构建162例患者的537条雷达心音与每人一份超声心动图报告配对数据集，覆盖三种重叠表型（LVEF<50%、节段性室壁运动异常、至少中度瓣膜反流，分别38、43、39例）；提供原始复数信号、处理后雷达心音及时间质量分段以支持信号—表型分析；进行技术验证（信号时长保留率、波形重建可重复性）；采用患者级五折交叉验证评估表型分类性能。

Result: 技术验证保留95.93%的信号时长，波形重建具可重复性；患者级五折评价中，降低LVEF检测的平均外部测试AUROC为0.770±0.138，表明雷达心音中蕴含与心室收缩功能障碍相关的机械信息。

Conclusion: 该资源为连接非接触心脏机械信号与超声心动图结构及功能表型的研究提供了数据基础，可用于探索雷达心音作为结构性心脏病筛查与表征的潜在无接触手段。

Abstract: Radar heart sounds capture cardiac mechanical activity without contact, while echocardiography characterizes ventricular and valvular function. Paired measurements connect these signals to structural heart disease, yet such data remain sparsely represented in public resources. Here we present 537 radar heart-sound recordings paired with one echocardiographic report for each of 162 patients. The cohort covers three overlapping phenotypes: left ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF) below 50%, regional wall-motion abnormality and at least moderate valvular regurgitation, present in 38, 43 and 39 patients, respectively. Original complex signals, processed radar heart sounds and temporal quality segmentation support signal-to-phenotype analysis. Technical validation retains 95.93% of signal duration and confirms reproducible waveform reconstruction. Patient-level five-fold evaluation achieves a mean outer-test AUROC of $0.770\pm0.138$ for reduced LVEF, identifying mechanical information associated with ventricular systolic dysfunction. This resource supports research linking non-contact cardiac mechanical signals to echocardiographic structural and functional phenotypes.

</details>


### [52] [Certified Abstention for Provably-Unidentifiable Index-Phase Recovery in Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29272)
*Dinh Tuan Nguyen,Trong Nhan Nguyen,Vu Nguyen Minh Hung,Tien Minh Khoi Nguyen,Jaeyeop Choi,Sudip Mondal,Jae Sung Ahn,Junghwan Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出在参考扰动采样成像中，位置保真与相位保真逻辑独立，任何仅依赖位置信息的估计器无法保证相位保真；给出紧的分辨率阈值区分可恢复与可证不可辨识区域，并提出带认证的恢复框架：对可辨识区域精确恢复、对不可辨识区域弃权。部署扫描系统中观测到符合理论的方向双峰偏移，认证方法在多模态下弃权而非伪造结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像配准是扫描、相机与显微成像测量的基础，但"样本到位置映射何时可恢复、何时任何报位估计器必然伪造位置"这一根本问题鲜被提出；现有经典与学习型复原方法在不可辨识区域仍强行输出结果，产生伪造结构。

Method: 在参考扰动采样成像框架下形式化位置保真与相位保真的独立性，证明二者不可由仅基于位置信息的策略同时保证；推导可恢复性与可证不可辨识性之间的紧分辨率阈值；构建认证式恢复框架：对可辨识区域精确恢复，对不可辨识区域弃权并提供风险界定。在部署扫描系统上实测偏移，并与经典及学习型复原基线对比；将弃权机制迁移到扫描、卷帘快门与冷冻电镜成像。

Result: 部署扫描系统测得与理论一致的方向双峰配准偏移；相较基线，无认证的偏移估计器在不可辨识区伪造结构，认证方法则弃权；认证-弃权机制可跨扫描、卷帘快门、cryo-EM 迁移，在标记区域弃权而非伪造，但弃权完整度与保留区风险界为模态特异（极端 SNR 下 cryo-EM 认证仅部分弃权）。

Conclusion: 配准可靠性存在结构性—统计性边界：可辨识性是可认证的结构性质，而非仅由统计估计精度决定；认证加弃权为跨模态配准提供了不伪造结构的可靠范式，但风险界与弃权强度随模态而异。

Abstract: Image registration underpins measurement across scanning, camera, and microscopy imaging, yet a fundamental question is rarely posed: when is the sample-to-position map recoverable at all, and when does any estimator that reports a position necessarily fabricate one? We show that in reference-perturbed sampled imaging, where samples are localized through a perturbed reference, position fidelity (where samples land) and phase fidelity (which reference index becomes each block's origin) are logically independent: no policy acting on position information alone can guarantee phase fidelity. We derive a tight resolution threshold separating recoverable from provably-unidentifiable registration, and a recovery framework that certifies identifiable regions--recovering them exactly--while abstaining on provably-unidentifiable regions rather than hallucinating structure. On a deployed scanning system we measure a direction-bimodal registration offset consistent with the theory. Against classical and learned restoration baselines, certificate-free offset estimators fabricate structure on the unidentifiable regime, whereas the certified method abstains rather than fabricating. Finally, the certified-abstention mechanism carries across scanning, rolling-shutter, and cryo-electron-microscopy imaging--declining rather than fabricating on the regime it flags--though how completely it abstains and how tightly it bounds kept-region risk stay modality-specific (at extreme SNR the cryo-EM certificate abstains only partially): a structural-versus-statistical boundary in registration reliability.

</details>


### [53] [Computer Vision and Knowledge-Grounded Language Models for Intelligent Beekeeper Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29283)
*Asaduz Zaman,Mohaimenul Azam Khan Raiaan,Malika Nisal Ratnayake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个边缘可部署的蜂巢框图像智能检测框架，将蜂巢框照片转化为可解释、可行动的检查报告：目标检测→结构化事实模式→受约束的事实性文本→基于事实触发的知识库给出解读与建议；基于开源FAIRHiveFrames-1K构建，并发布FAIRHiveText-1K数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜜蜂（管理蜂群与野生蜂）支撑约三分之一的全球食品产出，但蜂群面临气候变化带来的极端天气、寄生虫与疾病模式改变等压力。及时评估蜂群状态（蜂群强度、子脾面积、蜜粉储存、巢脾使用率）对适应性管理至关重要，而人工巢框检查速度慢、成本高且依赖稀缺的专业经验。

Method: 构建端到端的边缘可部署流水线：(1) 目标检测器识别具有生物学意义的巢房类别；(2) 将检测结果蒸馏为结构化事实模式（fact schema）；(3) 由事实转换为受约束的事实性文本；(4) 文本触发接地知识库，输出解读与建议；知识库条目须能由事实模式中的证据触发。基于开源FAIRHiveFrames-1K数据集，优先选用可复现、边缘可部署的模型而非大型通用前沿模型；并额外发布FAIRHiveText-1K数据集（将FAIRHiveFrames-1K转为可互换的事实与文本解读），同时提出构建接地知识库的路径。

Result: 实现了从巢框照片到可解释、可行动检查报告的完整流水线；发布FAIRHiveText-1K数据集以支持后续基于大语言模型的研究；给出了知识库构建路径。

Conclusion: 以边缘可部署、可复现的模块化设计替代人工检查，可为蜂群适应性管理提供可解释、可操作的状态报告，并通过公开数据集推动该方向的可复现研究与LLM应用。

Abstract: Managed and wild bees sustain roughly a third of the global food products, yet colonies face mounting stress from climate-change including extreme weather patterns, altered parasite and disease patterns. Timely assessment of colony condition such as understanding colony strength, brood area, honey and pollen storage, comb usage, is essential to adaptive management. However, manual hive frame inspection is slow, costly, and quality depends on scarce expertise. We present an artificial targeted intelligent system framework focusing on edge deployable pipeline that turns hive frame photographs into interpretable and action-oriented inspection report. The pipeline includes an object detector to detect biologically-meaningful comb-cell classes; detections are distilled into structured fact schema; facts are converted into constrained factual text; the text triggers a grounded knowledge-base for interpretations and recommendations. Knowledge-base entries are required to be triggerable by evidence expressed in the fact schema. We built our system based on open source FAIRHiveFrames-1K dataset and favour reproducible, edge-deployable models over large general purpose frontier models. We additionally release FAIRHiveText-1K dataset that turns FAIRHiveFrames-1K dataset into interchangeable facts and text interpretation of the facts, making it easy for further LLM-based research into this area. We also propose a pathway to develop the grounded knowledge-base that will be helpful in providing interpretable and actionable reports from the facts.

</details>


### [54] [Latent space configuration for neural network finetuning, fast ranking, and embedding dimensionality reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29295)
*Nikita Gabdullin,Anton Raskovalov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对神经网络嵌入库的top-K近邻检索，利用潜空间配置(LSC)方法使ArcFace预训练的IResNet50嵌入具备特定几何性质，从而通过O(1)复杂度的小候选子集搜索加速检索，在GPU上获得最高3倍、CPU上相对基线最高40%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模神经网络嵌入库中为查询寻找最相似的K个元素是计算机视觉与AI工业应用的核心任务，但穷举搜索代价随库规模线性增长，亟需在不显著损失检索精度的前提下加速top-K检索。

Method: 提出面向神经网络结构化潜空间(Latent Space)的检索算法，通过O(1)复杂度构造小型候选库子集以加速top-K项检索；采用潜空间配置(Latent Space Configuration, LSC)方法对ArcFace预训练的IResNet50模型进行微调，使嵌入呈现期望的几何性质；并在原始与降维后的潜空间维度上评估算法。

Result: 相比基线穷举搜索，微调模型嵌入上应用所提算法实现GPU最高3倍加速、CPU上相对基线GPU速度最高40%加速；潜空间维度降低可进一步加速检索，甚至改善rank-N指标。

Conclusion: 通过LSC调控嵌入几何并结合O(1)候选子集筛选，可在保持甚至提升rank-N检索质量的同时显著加速top-K相似检索，验证了结构化潜空间在高效近邻搜索中的有效性。

Abstract: Finding K most similar elements for a query in a large gallery of neural network (NN) embeddings is essential in various areas of computer science and industrial applications of artificial intelligence. This paper proposes search algorithms for structured latent spaces (LS) of NNs which allow to speed up top K item retrieval owing to O(1) complexity small candidate gallery subset search. ArcFace-pretrained IResNet50 models are finetuned using Latent Space Configuration (LSC) methodology to obtain new distribution of embeddings with desired geometric properties. Applying the proposed algorithms to embeddings obtained from finetuned models leads to several times speed-up over baseline exhaustive search. Specifically, we report up to 3 times speed-up on GPU and 40% speed-up on CPU compared to the baseline method GPU speed. These results are obtained for the original and reduced numbers of LS dimensions. The results show that LS dimensionality reduction allows to further accelerate the search and even improve rank-N metrics.

</details>


### [55] [OpenMAP-HIE: Pathology Informed Score-based Diffusion Model for Unsupervised Neonatal Hypoxic-Ischemic Encephalopathy Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29322)
*Pengyu Kan,Craig Jones,Kenichi Oishi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OpenMAP-HIE，一种病理先验引导的分数扩散模型，无需体素级病灶标注即可在ADC图像上无监督定位新生儿缺氧缺血性脑病（HIE）病灶，Dice达0.283±0.244，结合Z-map细化后升至0.602±0.269。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIE病灶在ADC上的准确定位具有重要临床意义，但专家标注稀缺且病灶表现变异大，体素级监督难以获得。

Method: 以去噪扩散模型学习健康新生儿ADC分布，并用基于ControlNet的条件分支保留患者个体解剖结构；推理时直接在独立采样的扩散噪声水平上比较条件与无条件噪声预测之差，获得异常证据，不依赖最终伪健康重建或顺序逆向扩散链；依据急性HIE的ADC降低特征仅保留符合病理方向的差异，并用几何平均跨噪声级聚合，辅以空间均值滤波抑制孤立响应；可选地用群体来源ADC Z-map细化。

Result: 在BONBID-HIE数据集上，仅用ADC的方法取得Dice 0.283±0.244、平均精度0.505、Hausdorff距离25.45±15.63 mm、平均表面距离9.40±8.49 mm、归一化表面距离0.355±0.236，在所评估的无监督方法中重叠度与表面距离表现最佳，Dice与有监督ADC单模态模型相当；可选Z-map细化将Dice提升至0.602±0.269。

Conclusion: 病理先验引导的分数差异可作为HIE病灶定位的有效信号，但小尺寸、低对比度病灶的检测仍是待解决的挑战。

Abstract: Accurate localization of neonatal hypoxic--ischemic encephalopathy (HIE) lesions on apparent diffusion coefficient (ADC) images is clinically important but is limited by scarce expert annotations and substantial variation in lesion appearance. We propose OpenMAP-HIE, a pathology-informed score-based diffusion model for unsupervised HIE lesion localization without voxel-wise lesion supervision. A denoising diffusion model learns the healthy neonatal ADC distribution, while a ControlNet-based conditional branch preserves patient-specific anatomy. At inference, anomaly evidence is derived directly from differences between conditional and unconditioned noise predictions at independently evaluated diffusion noise levels, avoiding dependence on a final pseudo-healthy reconstruction or sequential reverse-diffusion chain. Based on the characteristic ADC reduction associated with acute HIE, only discrepancies consistent with the expected pathological direction are retained and aggregated across noise levels using a geometric mean. Spatial mean filtering further suppresses isolated responses. The framework was evaluated on the BONBID-HIE dataset. The ADC-only method achieved a Dice score of $0.283\pm0.244$, average precision of $0.505$, Hausdorff distance of $25.45\pm15.63$ mm, mean average surface distance of $9.40\pm8.49$ mm, and normalized surface distance of $0.355\pm0.236$, providing the best overlap and surface-distance performance among the evaluated unsupervised methods and Dice performance comparable to supervised ADC-only models. Optional population-derived ADC $Z$-map refinement further increased Dice to $0.602\pm0.269$. These findings support pathology-informed score discrepancies as a useful signal for neonatal HIE lesion localization, while highlighting remaining challenges in detecting small and low-contrast lesions.

</details>


### [56] [Reading the Mask: Descriptor-Grounded BI-RADS Rationale Generation for Breast MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29358)
*Hrishikesh Singh,Aarti Sharma,Divyanshu Kumar,Millie Pant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文从DCE-MRI分割掩膜中派生BI-RADS结构化描述子，并用大语言模型（LLM）生成可核验的自然语言推理，构建了3012条可复现的BI-RADS依据语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型能准确勾画DCE-MRI上的肿瘤，但分割掩膜本身不提供放射科医生评估病灶可疑性时所依据的推理，限制了解释性与临床集成。

Method: 以BI-RADS指南为结构化框架，设计含8个节点的BI-RADS决策树，节点由8项影像组学定量测量、病灶大小及成分计数决定描述子；再用LLM将决策树记录转写为自然语言（约束仅表达所派生记录）；在MAMA-MIA数据集1506例的专家掩膜与自动掩膜上分别应用。

Result: 生成3012条BI-RADS依据的推理语料；忠实性得分99.98%，反事实准确率99.3%；专家与自动掩膜描述子一致性随掩膜质量由Good到Missed从0.832降至0.549。

Conclusion: 掩膜可支撑可复现、可核验的BI-RADS描述子与推理生成，但性能依赖掩膜质量；生成推理定位为供放射科医生审核与签署的草稿，而非自主临床报告。

Abstract: Deep learning based models accurately delineate tumours on dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI (DCE-MRI), but segmentation masks alone do not provide the rationales or reasoning used by radiologists to assess the lesion suspicion. This limits the explainability as well the clinical integration. We investigate whether clinically relevant descriptors can instead be derived from the segmentation masks and their dynamic contrast-enhancement series. We have used Breast Imaging Reporting and Data System (BI-RADS) guidelines as a structured framework. Using these guidelines we designed a BI-RADS decision tree with eight quantitative measurements using radiomics, together with the lesion size and component count, that determine the descriptors at eight nodes. Using these descriptors we generate natural-language rationale using a large language model. The model was constrained to express only the derived records from the decision tree. After applying this framework to the expert and automatic masks of each of the 1,506 cases in the MAMA-MIA dataset, we generated a reproducible corpus of 3,012 BI-RADS grounded rationales. We obtained a faithfulness score of 99.98% and a counterfactual accuracy of 99.3%, indicating that the generated rationales faithfully verbalises the measured descriptors and correctly reflects changes in the underlying record. Agreement between the descriptors from expert and automatic masks falls from 0.832 to 0.549 as mask quality falls from Good to Missed. The generated rationales are intended as draft representations for radiologist review and final sign-off rather than autonomous clinical reports.

</details>


### [57] [Seeing through Denoising: Frequency-guided One-step Denoising for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29361)
*Xuelin Qian,Zixuan Wang,Chang Xu,Dingwen Zhang,Junwei Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生成式框架DiffIRSTD，将红外小目标检测重构为背景重建问题，利用扩散模型恢复无目标背景并以残差突出目标，配合单步去噪与频域感知损失，作为即插即用模块在三个数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测面临目标尺寸极小、信噪比低、背景杂波强等困难。现有深度学习方法多采用判别式范式，直接将图像映射为目标掩码，忽视了生成式建模的潜力。

Method: 将小目标视为类噪声信号，用扩散模型从受扰动输入中重建干净的无目标背景，以重建背景与原图之差作为候选目标区域的显式线索；引入单步去噪策略降低扩散采样开销；设计频域引导感知损失以保留背景高频细节并关注目标区域重建质量；整体为即插即用框架，不增加检测器计算复杂度。

Result: 在IRSTD-1K、NUAA-SIRST、NUDT-SIRST三个数据集上的大量实验表明方法优越，三个基准均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 将检测转化为背景重建的生成式范式可行且有效，残差线索与现有IRSTD模型互补，可在不增加推理复杂度的前提下提升检测性能。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) is a challenging task due to the extremely small size of targets, low signal-to-noise ratios, and heavy background clutter in infrared imagery. While the progress of deep learning has advanced this field, existing methods primarily adopt discriminative paradigms that directly map images to target masks, overlooking the potential of generative modeling. In this paper, we propose DiffIRSTD, a novel generative framework that reformulates infrared small target detection as a background reconstruction problem. Instead of explicitly predicting target regions, we reinterpret small targets as noise-like signals and leverage a diffusion-based model to recover a clean, target-free background from a perturbed input image. The residual between the reconstructed background and the original image is then exploited to highlight candidate target regions, providing explicit cues for subsequent detection. To address the computational cost typically associated with diffusion sampling, we introduce a one-step denoising strategy that enables efficient background reconstruction while maintaining practical inference speed. Furthermore, we present a frequency-guided perception loss to preserve high-frequency background details and focus more on the reconstruction quality of target regions. More importantly, our DiffIRSTD operates as a plug-and-play framework, compatible with existing IRSTD models without increasing the computational complexity of detectors. Extensive experiments on IRSTD-1K, NUAA-SIRST, and NUDT-SIRST datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method, achieving state-of-the-art performance across three benchmarks.

</details>


### [58] [AOV-GS: Active Open-Vocabulary Semantic Mapping with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29374)
*Rodion Shkokov,Tatiana Zemskova,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AOV-GS: 用冻结的 SAM/CLIP 基础模型引导主动探索，并以收集到的 CLIP 嵌入离线拟合开放词汇语言场，实现兼顾几何导航与开放词汇语义查询的主动 3D Gaussian 建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人本环境中的机器人需要同时支持几何导航与开放词汇语义查询的地图；痛点在于语义增强不依赖人工标注分割掩码或闭集类别，且现有几何驱动的主动建图把语义当作轨迹的被动副产物，而主动语义建图依赖有监督分割。

Method: 1) 粗探索阶段用冻结 SAM 做实例分割、计算 CLIP 掩码嵌入新颖度，对几何上可行的候选视角重排序，使探索偏向语义信息丰富区域；2) 全流程由冻结的 SAM 与 CLIP 提供实例掩码与语言对齐嵌入；3) 离线将轻量场景特定语言场直接拟合到收集的 CLIP 嵌入（而非人工掩码），从而在新视角上支持开放词汇文本查询分割。

Result: 同一 SAM-CLIP 感知栈的受控对比下，语言场平均 mIoU 相比纯几何主动规划在 Replica 上提升 1.3 个百分点（八场景 34.6%），Matterport3D 上提升 1.8 个百分点（五场景 23.1%）；配合 CorrCLIP 在 Replica 达 35.6%，比匹配的 Geometry-based + CorrCLIP 基线高 2.0 点。两数据集上新视角 PSNR 与几何基线相当，Replica 上深度 L1 更优。

Conclusion: 以冻结基础模型的语义新颖度作为主动探索信号，可把语义从被动副产物变为主动优化目标，且无需标注掩码即可获得开放词汇查询能力，同时不牺牲几何重建质量，验证了语义驱动主动建图的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Robots deployed in human-centered environments need maps that support both geometric navigation and open-vocabulary semantic queries beyond a fixed label set. A key challenge is enriching such maps with semantic information without relying on manually annotated segmentation masks or closed-set semantic categories. Existing geometry-driven active mapping maximizes coverage but treats semantics as a passive by-product of the collected trajectory, whereas active semantic mapping typically depends on supervised semantic segmentation. We present AOV-GS, an active 3D Gaussian mapping system that guides exploration using frozen foundation models. During coarse exploration, AOV-GS re-ranks geometrically promising candidate views according to CLIP mask-embedding novelty computed from SAM instance segmentation, biasing exploration toward semantically informative regions. Frozen SAM and CLIP provide instance masks and language-aligned embeddings throughout mapping, while a lightweight scene-specific language field is fitted offline directly to the collected CLIP embeddings rather than manually annotated masks, enabling open-vocabulary text-query segmentation on novel views. In a controlled comparison using the same SAM-CLIP perception stack, AOV-GS improves average language-field mIoU over geometry-only active planning by 1.3 percentage points on Replica (34.6% on eight scenes) and by 1.8 points on Matterport3D (23.1% on five scenes). With CorrCLIP, AOV-GS reaches 35.6% on Replica, 2.0 points above the matched Geometry-based + CorrCLIP baseline. Novel-view PSNR remains comparable to the geometry-only baseline on both datasets while depth L1 improves on Replica. Code is available at https://github.com/OptimusFaber/AOV-GS.

</details>


### [59] [AdvFidelity: Unifying Preference Alignment and Few-Step Image Generation within Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29380)
*Sicong Kang,Fangao Zeng,Yuhan Li,Hao Zhou,Mengfei Xu,Wenxiang Shang,Wei Li,Bingbing Ni,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdvFidelity 通过在线对抗奖励将伪影敏感的保真度监督融入单阶段少步强化学习，无需 DMD 等显式蒸馏即可同时实现人类偏好对齐与高保真少步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际图像生成需同时满足人类偏好对齐与少步效率，但 RL 与蒸馏结合通常多阶段且可能侵蚀 RL 收益。作者观察到少步 RL 即使使用专门奖励也会逐步提升结构一致性，却残留低层伪影，而这些奖励难以惩罚此类伪影。

Method: 提出 AdvFidelity：训练判别器区分高质量参考（正样本）与由当前生成器产生的、内容匹配的重建样本（负样本）；负样本由对中等加噪的参考进行重建得到，既保留风格与场景布局又暴露低层退化，使判别器关注伪影而非内容差异；负样本在线生成并交替更新，使保真监督适配生成器演化中的失败模式；参考可来自目标域真实数据或全步模型采样；基于 LeapAlign，在推理所用少步采样轨迹上对两种奖励联合反向传播。

Result: 在两个模型骨干、三种任务设定下，AdvFidelity 仅用 4 或 8 步且不使用 CFG，即在对齐、任务性能与整体生成质量上匹配全步 LeapAlign，并与其他全步 RL 方法保持强竞争力。

Conclusion: 将保真度监督以对抗奖励形式并入 RL，可在单一 RL 阶段内兼顾奖励对齐与高保真少步生成，替代多阶段 RL+蒸馏流程。

Abstract: Practical image generation requires both human preference alignment and few-step efficiency, yet combining reinforcement learning (RL) with distillation is often multi-stage and may erode RL gains We observe that few-step RL progressively improves structural coherence, even with specialized rewards, but leaves low-level artifacts that these rewards may fail to penalize. To address this gap, we propose AdvFidelity, which integrates artifact-sensitive fidelity supervision into RL through an online adversarial reward. This enables joint reward alignment and high-fidelity few-step generation within a single RL stage, without distribution matching distillation (DMD) or other explicit distillation objectives. We train a discriminator to rank high-quality references (positives) above content-matched reconstructions (negatives) produced by the current generator. Reconstructing moderately noised references largely preserves style and scene layout while exposing low-level degradations. This encourages the discriminator to assess artifacts rather than content differences. With negatives reconstructed online, alternating updates adapt fidelity supervision to the generator's evolving failure modes. References can come from existing target-domain data or full-step model samples. Built on LeapAlign, AdvFidelity backpropagates gradients from both rewards through the few-step sampling trajectories used at inference. Across two model backbones and three task settings, AdvFidelity matches full-step LeapAlign in alignment, task performance, and overall generation quality and remains highly competitive with other evaluated full-step RL methods, using only 4 or 8 steps without CFG.

</details>


### [60] [FM-Occ: Feature-Diverse Upsampling and Memory-Aware Decoding for 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29388)
*Yujia Zhai,Guoming Huang,Zhuanghuai Wang,Bangquan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FM-Occ，包含3D稀疏亚体素重排(3S)与熵调控逐层记忆(ER-LM)，缓解体素上采样的特征同质化与解码层语义遗忘，在SemanticKITTI上以可忽略开销提升mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现两个问题：常规稀疏转置卷积上采样导致体素响应区分度低（特征同质化）；连续解码层会丢弃此前正确的语义预测（语义退化）。

Method: FM-Occ由两部分组成：(1) 3D Sparse Subvoxel Shuffle (3S)：在通道维构造候选子特征，联合变换与归一化后重排到更细的稀疏网格，显式建立特征-坐标对应，抑制特征同质化；(2) Entropy-Regulated Layer-wise Memory (ER-LM)：为每个query维护逐层记忆，经门控循环更新，并用预测熵调控向下一层传递的历史信息；训练时引入类加权熵损失L_cwe对匹配的语义query做标签引导正则。

Result: SemanticKITTI上，3S将归一化体素级RMS响应的空间变异系数由0.41%提升至4.03%；ER-LM降低语义退化并改善跨层纠错。FM-Occ整体mIoU由12.16%升至12.73%，内存开销可忽略；ER-LM在BEV-based DISC框架中也将mIoU由16.78%提升至17.17%。

Conclusion: 上采样与解码两方面的针对性设计可有效提升3D语义占据预测，且方法对体素基与BEV基架构均具通用性。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction estimates voxel-wise occupancy and semantic labels throughout a scene. Our experiments reveal that conventional sparse transposed-convolution upsampling yields poorly differentiated voxel responses and that successive decoder layers discard previously correct semantic predictions. To address these issues, we present Feature-Diverse Upsampling and Memory-Aware Decoding for 3D Occupancy Prediction (FM-Occ), comprising two components. First, 3D Sparse Subvoxel Shuffle (3S) mitigates feature homogenization by constructing candidate child features in the channel dimension, jointly transforming and normalizing them, and then rearranging them onto a finer sparse grid with explicit feature-coordinate correspondence. Second, Entropy-Regulated Layer-wise Memory (ER-LM) retains semantic information across decoder layers by maintaining a layer-wise memory for each query through gated recurrent updates and using prediction entropy to regulate the historical information propagated to the next layer. During training, a class-weighted entropy loss Lcwe provides label-guided regularization for matched semantic queries. On SemanticKITTI, 3S increases the spatial coefficient of variation of normalized voxel-wise root-mean-square (RMS) responses from 0.41% to 4.03%, while ER-LM reduces semantic degradation and improves error correction across decoder layers. The combined FM-Occ improves mIoU from 12.16% to 12.73% with negligible memory overhead. ER-LM further improves mIoU in the BEV-based DISC framework from 16.78% to 17.17%, supporting its applicability across 3D voxel-based and BEV-based architectures.

</details>


### [61] [Region-Aware Multi-View Inspection Using Deep Learning and Domain-Informed Decision Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29398)
*Srivishnu Ethirajulu Krishnaraj,Rishikesh R. Chopade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出区域感知双模型检测框架（实例分割座椅区域缺陷+图像级分类轴区域异常），并用固化工业SOP的确定性决策融合层将检测结果转为部件级判定，对380支喷油嘴针阀实现NOK召回率100%、误拒率由57.6%降至10.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一精密部件不同区域缺陷特征不同、验收标准不同，微米级表面缺陷的自动光学检测难以用单一模型/统一阈值完成；且直接聚合模型输出易造成大量过拒。

Method: 区域感知双模型框架：座椅区域用实例分割定位局部缺陷，轴区域用图像级分类判断全局异常；PLC同步成像系统在360°旋转中采集18张座椅图与18张轴图实现多视角周向覆盖；模型输出不直接作为拒收依据，而由形式化工业SOP的确定性决策融合层作出部件级判定。所有配置使用相同模型预测以保证可比性。

Result: 在覆盖代表性缺陷类型的380支车用喷油嘴针阀上评估：简单并集逻辑（任一视图检出即拒收）检出全部104件不合格品，但部件级误拒率57.6%；加入完整决策层后在NOK召回率保持100%的同时将误拒率降至10.9%，误正部件判定减少81.1%。

Conclusion: 形式化的制造验收准则可与高灵敏度视觉模型互补，把帧级检测转化为实用、可追溯的部件级决策；决策融合层是降低误拒的关键环节而非模型本身。

Abstract: Automated optical inspection of precision components with tiny surface defects remains challenging when different regions of the same component exhibit different defect characteristics and, therefore, different acceptance criteria. This paper presents a region-aware, dual-model inspection framework that combines instance segmentation of localized seat-region defects with image-level classification of global shaft-region anomalies. Rather than treating these two model detections as sufficient evidence for component rejection, a deterministic decision fusion layer that formalizes industrial Standard Operating Procedures is applied to the model outputs to make the final component level decision. A PLC-synchronized imaging system acquires 18 seat and 18 shaft images over a full 360-degree rotation to provide multi-view circumferential coverage. The framework was evaluated on 380 automotive fuel injector needles, covering representative operational defect types, using identical model predictions in all configurations. Direct model-output aggregation via simple union logic (where any single-view detection flags a rejection) identified all 104 non-conforming components but produced a component level false rejection rate of 57.6%. Inclusion of a complete decision layer maintained the observed non-conforming (NOK) recall of 100% while reducing the false rejection rate to 10.9%, corresponding to an 81.1% reduction in false-positive component decisions. The results demonstrate that formalized manufacturing acceptance criteria can complement sensitive visual models by translating frame-level detections into practical and traceable component-level decisions.

</details>


### [62] [VideoGen Model 1: Prompt Expansion Versus Formatting in Audiovisual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29407)
*David Grossman,Anton Koenig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在 VideoGen Model 1 的对照试点中，创意式提示词扩写相比原始/仅重排提示词提升了整体音视频质量评分，但提升主要来自提示词对齐与音频质量，视觉质量几乎不变，且不同评审模型给出的效应大小差异明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成视频质量是否可通过提示词工程（格式化与创意扩写）在固定生成器、随机种子与采样设置的前提下得到提升，并量化不同评审模型下效应的稳定性。

Method: 对 30 条提示词分别构造原始、重排格式、创意扩写三种版本，固定生成器（MiniMax H3 后训练版 VideoGen Model 1）、随机种子与采样参数生成片段；每条片段在五个维度上独立打分，取均值作为 1–10 的整体分；采用 VA-Judger 与 Gemini 3.8 Flash 两个评审模型，并给出 95% 区间。

Result: 相比仅格式化，整体分在 VA-Judger 下提升 0.63 分（95% 区间 [+0.11, +1.15]），Gemini 3.8 Flash 下提升 0.15 分（95% 区间 [-0.03, +0.35]），后者整体变化不确定；提示词对齐分分别提升 0.97 与 0.50；VA-Judger 显示音频质量更高，两评审下视觉质量变化均很小。

Conclusion: 创意扩写对整体质量有正向但评审模型依赖的效应，主要作用于提示词对齐与音频维度而非视觉维度；其中 Gemini 的区间跨零，结论尚不确定，需更大样本与多评审验证。

Abstract: Creative prompt expansion improved overall audiovisual quality scores over raw prompts in a controlled pilot of VideoGen Model 1, our post-trained version of MiniMax H3. We compared the raw, reformatted, and expanded versions of 30 prompts, holding the generator, seed, and sampling settings fixed. Each clip was rated separately on five dimensions. Their mean formed an overall score on a 1-10 scale. Compared with formatting alone, overall scores increased by 0.63 points (95% interval: [+0.11, +1.15]) under VA-Judger and 0.15 points (95% interval: [-0.03, +0.35]) under Gemini 3.8 Flash, whose overall change remained uncertain. Prompt-alignment scores increased by 0.97 points under VA-Judger and 0.50 under Gemini. VA-Judger also showed higher audio quality; visual-quality ratings changed little under either judge.

</details>


### [63] [LiveAct: Towards Real-Time Multi-Agent World Models with Heterogeneous Action Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29408)
*Xinyu Wang,Huafeng Shi,Yue Ma,Zijie Meng,Teng Xi,Yuzhuo Chen,Yucheng Wang,Ruicheng Zhang,Guoxin Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Xiu Li,Fangneng Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveAct 提出多智能体视频世界模型，通过异构动作接口（HAI）实现多智能体独立控制与异构动作语义适配，并迁移到分块因果生成器以支持高效流式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型多假设单受控智能体与任务专属动作接口，无法处理多智能体联合视觉预测及从二值游戏按键到连续机器人指令的异构控制，核心难点是动作流与视觉实体的独立绑定以及控制变量语义跨任务的适配。

Method: 提出 Heterogeneous Action Interface（HAI），含两部分：Agent-Aware Spatial Modulation 利用语言驱动的空间门控与智能体专属注意力，将每条动作流绑定到目标视觉智能体；Schema-Guided Action Adaptation 用语言描述每个控制变量并以同步数值调制其语义 token。再通过教师强制与 rollout 感知分布匹配将完整接口迁移至分块因果生成器。

Result: 在覆盖游戏与多机器人操作的六个多智能体数据源上评估视觉质量、动作跟随、智能体归属与延迟；在无智能体位置与身份裁剪条件下，游戏与机器人操作分别达到 0.898 与 0.942 的智能体归属准确率，因果生成器在 512×448 下达到 17.70 FPS。

Conclusion: LiveAct 将多智能体视频世界建模分解为独立控制与异构语义适配两个子问题，并通过 HAI 与因果生成器迁移统一解决，在保持智能体专属控制与动作语义的同时实现高效流式生成。

Abstract: Interactive video world models predict how an environment evolves under user actions, but most existing systems assume one controlled agent and one task-specific action interface. We study multi-agent video world modeling, where one model generates a joint visual future for multiple controlled agents while interpreting heterogeneous controls ranging from binary game buttons to continuous robot commands. This task presents two complementary challenges. First, every agent-indexed action stream must control its intended visual entity independently. Second, the model must adapt the meaning of each control variable across different tasks, where the same tensor dimension may denote an attack, steering command, or robot command. We introduce LiveAct, centered on a Heterogeneous Action Interface (HAI) whose two components mirror this decomposition. To support independent control, Agent-Aware Spatial Modulation uses language-driven spatial gates and agent-specific attention to bind each action stream to its intended visual agent. To accommodate heterogeneous controls, Schema-Guided Action Adaptation describes each control variable in language and modulates its semantic token with the synchronized numerical value. We further transfer the complete interface to a blockwise causal generator through teacher forcing and rollout-aware distribution matching, enabling efficient streaming generation while retaining agent-specific control and action semantics. We instantiate the formulation on six multi-agent sources spanning games and multi-robot manipulation, and evaluate visual quality, action following, agent attribution, and latency. Across games and robot manipulation, LiveAct reaches 0.898 and 0.942 agent-attribution accuracy without agent locations or identity crops, and the causal generator attains 17.70 FPS at $512\times 448$.

</details>


### [64] [LLMSeg: Training-Free Text-Prompted Image Segmentation with B-Splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29409)
*Abdullah Hamdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LLMSeg 是一个免训练流水线，直接将多模态大语言模型（MLLM）用作文本提示的分割器，无需分割头、掩码解码器或任务训练，在多个指代/推理分割基准上可与专用模型 SAM 3 媲美甚至超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可提示分割模型（如 SAM 3）虽具备强大的开放词汇能力，但依赖专用架构、大规模标注引擎和任务特定训练。作者提出反向问题：通用 MLLM 能否不借助额外分割头、掩码解码器或训练，直接完成图像分割，从而降低架构与训练成本，并随基座模型升级自动获益。

Method: LLMSeg 将分割分解为三个由 LLM 驱动的阶段：(1) detect——定位匹配自由文本查询的所有实例并描述其粗略形态；(2) segment——在检测框内用闭合周期 B 样条描迹实例轮廓，样条定义外边界及可选孔洞环；(3) refine——围绕残余误差生成焦点框并重新描迹受限样条，迭代至掩码稳定或达到轮次预算。输出为二进制掩码及可检查的中间几何。后端采用现成 Gemini-3.7-Flash，全程无分割专用训练。

Result: 在十个指代与推理分割划分（ReasonSeg、RefCOCO/+/g）上评估，ReasonSeg val 达到 71.5 cIoU，无需分割专用训练；同时评测开放词汇实例分割（LVIS、COCO、SA-Co）、语义分割与零样本检测（ODinW13、RF-100VL）。在相同样本上，LLMSeg 与专用 SAM 3 具有竞争力且常优于后者；后端模型更新可提升精度而无需重训流水线。

Conclusion: 通用 MLLM 本身具备可用的分割能力，通过"检测—样条描迹—迭代精修"的免训练结构化提示流程即可实现文本提示分割，为分割任务提供了一条不依赖专用架构与标注训练的替代路径，并具备随基座模型升级自然获益的可扩展性。

Abstract: Promptable segmentation models such as SAM 3 achieve remarkable open-vocabulary performance but require dedicated architectures, large-scale annotation engines, and task-specific training. We ask a different question: can a general-purpose multimodal large language model (MLLM) segment images directly, without an added segmentation head, mask decoder, or training? We present LLMSeg, a training-free pipeline that turns a vision-capable LLM into a text-prompted image segmenter. LLMSeg decomposes segmentation into three LLM-driven stages. First, detect localizes all instances matching a free-form text query and describes their coarse morphology. Next, segment traces each instance using closed periodic B-splines confined to its detected box. These splines define an outer boundary and optional hole loops. Finally, refine iteratively proposes focus boxes around residual errors and retraces confined splines. The process ends when the mask stabilizes or the pass budget is exhausted. The output is a binary mask with inspectable intermediate geometry. We evaluate LLMSeg across ten referring and reasoning segmentation splits (ReasonSeg and RefCOCO/+/g). We also evaluate open-vocabulary instance segmentation (LVIS, COCO, and SA-Co), semantic segmentation, and zero-shot detection (ODinW13 and RF-100VL). With an off-the-shelf Gemini-3.7-Flash backend, LLMSeg is competitive with and often surpasses the specialist SAM 3 model on identical items. It reaches 71.5 cIoU on ReasonSeg val without additional segmentation-specific training. Backend model updates can improve accuracy without retraining the pipeline. The code is available at https://abdullahamdi.com/llmseg .

</details>


### [65] [CCG Diffusion: Combinatory Categorial Grammar-Driven Compositional and Syntactically Disambiguated Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29415)
*Ryosuke Yamaki,Daichi Mochihashi,Nobutaka Shimada,Seitaro Shinagawa,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CCG Diffusion，一种基于组合范畴语法（CCG）的免训练文本到图像控制方法，通过prompt重写与注意力引导统一控制属性绑定、空间关系、数量以及介词短语（PP）附着歧义的消解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型不显式接收提示词的句法结构（修饰关系与短语附着），导致属性错绑、空间关系与数量描绘错误、句法歧义提示词难以按意图渲染。现有基于句法的方法主要依赖短语结构或依存语法，仅解决属性绑定，且需额外规则或树结构约束来提取修饰关系或指定句法解释，对空间关系、数量和句法歧义控制能力有限。

Method: 提出免训练的CCG Diffusion：利用CCG中每个词的范畴本身携带丰富句法信息（规定与何种成分组合、构成何种短语），修饰关系可直接从CCG解析中读取；PP附着歧义可通过固定介词的范畴直接消解。基于这两种CCG机制，统一采用prompt重写与注意力引导两种手段实施控制。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion与Sana两种架构不同的骨干上，CCG Diffusion在属性绑定与2D空间关系上取得对比方法中的最高分，并在PP歧义消解上显著优于所有对比方法。

Conclusion: CCG的范畴信息为修饰关系与句法歧义提供了直接、可读的表示，使免训练的prompt重写与注意力引导即可同时覆盖属性绑定、空间关系、数量与PP歧义等多类句法控制任务，且跨骨干模型有效。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation models do not explicitly receive the syntactic structure of the prompt, i.e., which word modifies which and where each phrase attaches. Consequently, they often struggle to bind attributes to the correct objects, to depict spatial relations and counts correctly, and to render syntactically ambiguous prompts in the intended interpretation. Existing syntax-based control methods mainly address attribute binding and have limited ability to control spatial relations and counts or to specify the interpretation of syntactically ambiguous prompts. They rely on phrase-structure or dependency grammars and require additional rules or constraints on the tree structure to extract modification relations between words or to specify a syntactic interpretation. We propose CCG Diffusion, a training-free method that controls image generation using Combinatory Categorial Grammar (CCG). In CCG, the category of each word carries rich syntactic information: it specifies what the word combines with and what phrase it forms. Modification relations between words can therefore be read directly from a CCG parse. Moreover, prepositional phrase (PP) attachment ambiguity, a representative syntactic ambiguity, can be resolved simply by fixing the preposition's category. CCG Diffusion applies the same two CCG-based mechanisms, prompt rewriting and attention guidance, both to controlling attribute binding, spatial relations, and counts and to resolving PP ambiguity. On two architecturally different backbones, Stable Diffusion and Sana, CCG Diffusion achieves the highest scores among the compared methods in attribute binding and 2D spatial relations and significantly outperforms all of them in PP ambiguity resolution.

</details>


### [66] [Look Beyond the Question: Preserve-Focus-Fuse for Whole-Slide Image Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29431)
*Xiangyu Li,Jincheng Li,Tianle Du,Yanheng Li,Haoxuan Che,Yuxin Sun,Long Bai,Liangliang Liu,Ran Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BeyonPath：Preserve-Focus-Fuse 框架，在保留全片形态学的同时按问题检索证据，实现WSI问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全片图像问题很少指明答案所需的全部形态学发现，问题引导的patch选择会遗漏支撑形态，而整片压缩会稀释局部证据。

Method: Preserve通过多问题答案监督学习问题无关的Morphology Bank；Focus从所有前景patch（含Bank外区域）检索问题特定证据；Fuse在证据位置通过Morphology-on-Visual-Evidence Fusion整合保存的形态，生成固定96 token视觉前缀。

Result: WSI-Bench上Avg-9达0.769，同一checkpoint下八项报告生成指标全部领先；SlideBench-BCNB零样本总体准确率0.639。

Conclusion: 消融表明形态保留与问题定向检索互补，支持跨问题监督及证据位置融合，需超越问题显式线索。

Abstract: A question about a whole-slide image rarely names every morphological finding needed to answer it. Question-guided patch selection can therefore overlook supporting morphology, while slide-level compression can dilute focal evidence. Inspired by how pathologists combine broad slide review, question-directed examination, and integration of their findings, we introduce BEYONDPATH, a Preserve-Focus-Fuse framework for whole-slide image question answering. Preserve learns a question-agnostic Morphology Bank through answer supervision across multiple questions about each training slide. Focus retrieves questionspecific evidence from all foreground patches, including regions outside the Bank. Fuse integrates the preserved morphology at the retrieved evidence positions through Morphology-on-Visual-Evidence Fusion, producing a fixed 96-token visual prefix. On WSI-Bench, BEYONDPATH achieves an Avg-9 of 0.769 and leads all eight reported report-generation metrics using the same checkpoint. It also achieves 0.639 zero-shot overall accuracy on SlideBench-BCNB. Controlled ablations show complementary benefits from morphology preservation and question-directed retrieval, and support cross-question supervision and fusion at the evidence positions. These results support looking beyond the question's explicit cues: preserving broader slide morphology while retaining the ability to seek evidence for each question.

</details>


### [67] [Sparsity Should Be ALLOTted by Output: Training-Free Allocation of Block-Sparse Attention for Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29441)
*Tianze Xia,Xiaoling Zhou,Ruya Lan,Yiqiao Liao,Chengru Song,Shiming Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的稀疏度分配方法ALLOT，通过输出误差估计各层-步单元贡献并按实测延迟预算联合优化密度，在等时或更短时间下优于所有对比分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频DiT中全3D注意力开销大，块稀疏注意力虽降本，但现有方法忽视稀疏度分配的优化潜力，或依赖启发式规则与局部指标来指导分配。

Method: 观察到不同层与去噪步的稀疏化对最终视频保真度影响差异显著，且最敏感的层-步对基本不随输入变化。将每个层-步对视为单元，通过(i)单元探测与(ii)轨迹校准估计其对最终视频误差的贡献，再在实测延迟预算下联合优化各单元密度；保留原有选择策略与算子内核，仅替换分配模块。

Result: 在等时或更短墙钟时间下，ALLOT配合XAttention优于所有对比分配策略，在HunyuanVideo上取得11.8 dB PSNR提升；在Wan2.1、Wan2.2与HunyuanVideo的四个已发表管线共九个设置中，全部优于或持平默认方案。

Conclusion: 输出敏感度驱动的层-步稀疏度分配可显著提升视频DiT稀疏注意力的保真度-效率权衡，且方法免训练、可即插即用于现有选择策略与内核。

Abstract: Full 3D attention is costly in video Diffusion Transformers. Block-sparse attention reduces this cost by computing only selected attention blocks. However, existing methods either overlook the potential to optimize sparsity allocation or rely on heuristic rules or local metrics to guide it. In this work, we observe that sparsifying attention in different layers and at different denoising steps affects final-video fidelity to substantially different degrees. Yet the identities of the most sensitive layer-step pairs remain nearly unchanged across inputs. Building on these findings, we propose Allocating Layer-step Latency by Output Tests (ALLOT), a training-free sparsity allocation method. Treating each layer-step pair as a cell, ALLOT estimates each cell's contribution to final-video error through (i) cell probing and (ii) trajectory calibration, then jointly optimizes cell densities under a measured-latency budget. It preserves existing selection strategies and kernels, replacing only the allocation component. At equal or shorter wall-clock time, ALLOT outperforms every compared allocation strategy with XAttention, with an 11.8 dB PSNR gain on HunyuanVideo. Extensive experiments across four additional published pipelines on Wan2.1, Wan2.2, and HunyuanVideo show that ALLOT outperforms or ties the default in all nine settings.

</details>
