### [68] [Beyond Shortcut Learning: Hierarchical Spatial Prototype Regularization for Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29442)
*Chao Shuai,Shaojing Fan,Kun Pan,Jinwu Hu,Weichen Lian,Ziqi Yang,Zhongjie Ba,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 作者指出朴素微调的视觉模型存在"取证表征坍塌"，并提出分层空间原型（HSP）正则化，通过结构化原型子空间建模伪造流形，提升深度伪造检测器的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型使高度逼真的人脸伪造成为现实，但深度伪造检测器向未见篡改流程的泛化仍是主要挑战。一个关键但研究不足的问题是：学习到的取证表征本身可能无法在不同篡改来源间保持稳定，易与数据集特定伪影纠缠。

Method: 提出分层空间原型（HSP）正则化，在空间特征上学习结构化原型子空间以显式建模伪造流形；并引入原型正交性与二阶分配平衡，分别保持潜在伪造因子的几何独立性、促进取证表征的高有效秩利用，从而减少对数据集特定捷径的依赖。

Result: 跨数据集与跨篡改设置下帧级AUC平均提升5.6%和5.3%；作为即插即用模块，将现有SOTA检测器平均视频级AUC提升至95.3%（+1.8%）。

Conclusion: HSP是一种易采用的正则化模块，可有效缓解取证表征坍塌、增强深度伪造检测器的泛化能力，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models have enabled highly realistic face forgeries, yet generalizing deepfake detectors to unseen manipulation pipelines remains a major challenge. One critical yet underexplored issue is that the learned forensic representation itself may fail to remain stable across different manipulation sources. In this paper, we provide new evidence that naively fine-tuned vision models suffer from severe \textit{forensic representation collapse}: their extracted cues are often entangled with unstable, dataset-specific artifacts, resulting in structurally fragile representations and poor cross-domain generalization. Leveraging this insight, we propose \textbf{H}ierarchical \textbf{S}patial \textbf{P}rototype (\textbf{HSP}) regularization, which explicitly models the forgery manifold by learning a structured prototype subspace over spatial features. We further introduce prototype orthogonality and second-order assignment balance, which jointly preserve the geometric independence of latent forgery factors and promote high-effective-rank utilization of the forensic representation, thereby reducing reliance on dataset-specific shortcuts. Empirical results demonstrate that our method substantially improves generalizability, yielding average frame-level AUC gains of 5.6\% and 5.3\% in the cross-dataset and cross-manipulation settings, respectively. Moreover, when used as a plug-in module, HSP boosts the average video-level AUC of existing state-of-the-art detectors to 95.3\% (+1.8\%). These results demonstrate the practical value of HSP as an easy-to-adopt regularization module for enhancing deepfake detectors.

</details>


### [69] [BlazeVGGT: 13x Faster VGGT at 2,000 Frames via Camera Token without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29454)
*Shujun Yao,Junhui Lin,Congying Han,Tiande Guo,Ziwen Liu,Bonan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BlazeVGGT 是一个免训练框架，通过结构感知的帧级 KV 稀疏化，显著加速 VGGT 类前馈几何模型的长序列推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT 类模型采用全对全跨视角注意力，计算量随输入视角数呈平方增长，高度重叠观测带来大量帧级冗余，使长序列推理昂贵。作者观察到 VGGT 相机 token 呈现"先拓扑、后几何"的演化：浅层token可靠捕获局部视角邻域，深层token逐步编码全局组织的视角间几何。

Method: 在每个全局注意力块中，利用相机token关系构建视角级相似度核，并通过贪婪行列式点过程（DPP）推理选取一组互互补的紧凑视角子集作为 key-value 上下文，同时保留全部输入帧作为 query（非对称注意力）。该方法无需参数更新，可直接作为即插即用模块集成到预训练 VGGT 中。

Result: 在2000帧时相较VGGT推理约提速13倍，同时Chamfer Distance由0.41降至0.32；在点云重建与相机位姿估计上取得良好的精度-效率权衡，并可与FastVGGT、LiteVGGT等加速器兼容，且可迁移至StreamVGGT和PAGE-4D等基于VGGT的模型。

Conclusion: 基于相机token演化的结构先验进行帧级KV稀疏化，可在免训练条件下大幅降低跨视角冗余计算，保持稠密逐帧预测，随序列长度增长优势愈发明显。

Abstract: Feed-forward visual geometry models, exemplified by VGGT, have demonstrated strong multi-view reconstruction capabilities through dense global attention. However, the resulting all-to-all cross-view computation scales quadratically with the number of input views, making long-sequence inference particularly expensive due to substantial frame-level redundancy among highly overlapping observations. We uncover a topology-first, geometry-later evolution in VGGT camera tokens: shallow-layer tokens reliably capture local view neighborhoods, whereas deeper-layer tokens progressively encode globally organized inter-view geometry. Motivated by this observation, we introduce BlazeVGGT, a training-free framework for structure-aware frame-level key--value sparsification. At each global-attention block, BlazeVGGT constructs a view-level similarity kernel from camera-token relations and employs greedy determinantal point process inference to select a compact set of mutually complementary views. The selected views constitute the key--value context, while all input frames are retained as queries. This asymmetric attention design preserves dense per-frame predictions while eliminating redundant cross-view computation, allowing our method to be directly integrated into pretrained VGGT as a plug-and-play module without parameter updates. Its advantages become increasingly pronounced with sequence length: at 2,000 frames, BlazeVGGT achieves approximately 13$\times$ faster inference than VGGT while reducing the Chamfer Distance from 0.41 to 0.32. Extensive experiments demonstrate a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off in point-cloud reconstruction and camera-pose estimation, compatibility with existing VGGT accelerators such as FastVGGT and LiteVGGT, and transferability to VGGT-based models including StreamVGGT and PAGE-4D. Codes are publicly available at https://github.com/MagiciannnY/BlazeVGGT.git.

</details>


### [70] [Extending Affordance Segmentation for Wearable Robots to Multi-Object Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29474)
*Simone Lugani,Matteo Montani,Paolo Gastaldo,Rodolfo Zunino,Edoardo Ragusa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉式可穿戴抓取部件识别管线在多物体场景下难以消歧的问题，提出引入激光指针的硬件微改与定制训练方案，并用混合现实程序化生成数据扩充场景，识别精度提升至多9%且计算成本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的便携式管线能支持上肢假肢装置的自动抓取选择与预塑形，但在多目标共存的实际场景中目标歧义问题突出，限制了其应用。

Method: 对常规硬件配置作小幅改动，加入激光指针作为目标消歧机制；据此对用于可抓取部件识别的深度卷积网络进行针对性重训练；采用混合现实策略复用已有高质量分割掩码，程序化生成逼真图像以扩展支持的场景范围。

Result: 实验表明，相比现有方案，所提管线将目标消歧准确率最高提升9%，同时保持计算成本不增加。

Conclusion: 激光指针硬件微改结合定制训练与混合现实数据生成，可在不提升计算开销的前提下有效扩展视觉抓取识别管线在多物体场景中的适用性与可靠性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a refinement to existing vision-based portable pipelines for the recognition of graspable object parts. Such pipelines can effectively support automatic grasp selection and preshaping in upper-limb prosthetic devices. However, realistic scenarios in which multiple objects are present still represent a challenge. The proposed method exploits a minor modification to the conventional hardware setup and a custom training approach to extend the applicability of vision-based pipelines. A laser pointer is introduced in the setup as an effective mechanism for object disambiguation. Accordingly, a suitable retraining of the deep convolutional neural networks supporting recognition of graspable parts is implemented. A mixed reality strategy allows the reuse of available high-quality segmentation masks to generate realistic images procedurally, extending the range of scenarios supported by the final device. Experimental results confirm that the proposed pipeline improves target disambiguation accuracy by up to 9% compared with existing solutions, while preserving the computational cost.

</details>


### [71] [Balancing Foundation Models and Trait-Specific Learning for Forehead-Crease Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29495)
*Geetanjali Sharma,Aryan Jain,Dev Nagaich,Aditya Nigam,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了额纹(forehead crease)作为互补生物特征在多模态大模型可解释零样本推理、域自适应学习和特征专门建模三条路线下的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸部分遮挡时常规面部线索失效导致生物识别性能下降，而额头区域始终可见且含具判别力的皱纹模式，可作为互补生物特征。

Method: 提出统一评估框架，涵盖：(1) FIRE，基于多模态大语言模型实现可解释的零样本验证；(2) 通过margin-based learning将预训练人脸识别模型适配到额头域（如Edge-Forehead）。

Result: 开集评测中，最佳零样本模型GPT-4o+FIRE的EER为18.22%；Edge-Forehead达到8.30% EER与54.01% TMR@0.1% FMR。

Conclusion: MLLM可提供可解释的零样本验证，但特征专门学习对稳健的额纹生物识别更为有效。

Abstract: Biometric recognition often degrades under partial facial vis- ibility, where conventional facial cues are occluded. The forehead region remains consistently visible and contains distinctive crease patterns that can serve as a complementary biometric trait. We present a unified eval- uation of forehead-crease recognition across explainable zero-shot rea- soning, domain-adaptive learning, and trait-specific modeling. FIRE en- ables interpretable zero-shot verification using multimodal large language models, while pretrained face-recognition models are adapted to the fore- head domain through margin-based learning. Under open-set evaluation, the best zero-shot model GPT-4o+FIRE achieves 18.22% EER, while Edge-Forehead achieves 8.30% EER and 54.01% TMR@0.1% FMR. The results show that although MLLMs offer interpretable zero-shot verifi- cation, trait-specific learning remains more effective for robust forehead- crease biometric recognition.

</details>


### [72] [AutoPhyX: Automatic Text-Condition Physics Property Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29517)
*Bei Huang,Yixin Chen,Hongbin Zha,Yuru Pei,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出文本条件驱动的AutoPhyX框架，单次前向预测空间变化的物理参数，并通过部件可控数据生成流程与跨模态调制机制解决参数歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真4D物理仿真需要准确的物理参数分配以匹配多样材料属性。现有方法存在瓶颈：要么依赖计算昂贵的逐场景优化，要么仅从纯视觉输入预测参数，后者存在固有歧义（如仅凭外观无法区分橡胶的软硬）。

Method: 1）构建部件可控的数据生成流水线：将复杂3D资产分解为语义独立部件，并配以物理上合理、多样的参数及对应文本描述，提供细粒度监督；2）AutoPhyX采用跨模态调制机制，用文本特征动态调制视觉特征以实现精确的物理 grounding；3）在体素场中建模物理属性预测，兼容网格、点云、Gaussian Splatting与NeRF等多种3D表示。

Result: 实验表明该方法可在单次前向中完成物理上合理、文本驱动的参数分配，精度高，并对野外（in-the-wild）物体具有稳健泛化能力。

Conclusion: AutoPhyX为物理密集型具身智能与机器人应用奠定基础，实现了高效、可泛化的空间变化物理属性预测。

Abstract: High-fidelity 4D physical simulation requires accurate physical parameter assignments that align with diverse material properties. Previous methods struggle with critical bottlenecks: they either rely on computationally intensive per-scene optimization or predict parameters from purely visual inputs. The latter suffers from inherent ambiguity, e.g., the inability to distinguish the stiffness of rubber from its appearance alone. To bridge this gap, we propose AutoPhyX, a text-conditioned framework for predicting spatially-varying physical properties. Since training robust feed-forward models requires fine-grained supervision, we first introduce a novel part-controllable data generation pipeline that decomposes complex 3D assets into semantically distinct components and pairs them with physically plausible, diverse parameters and corresponding text descriptions. Leveraging this dataset, AutoPhyX employs a cross-modal modulation mechanism where text features dynamically modulate visual features for precise physical grounding. By formulating property prediction within a voxel field, AutoPhyX ensures compatibility with diverse 3D representations, including meshes, point clouds, Gaussian Splatting, and NeRF. Experiments demonstrate that our method enables physically plausible, text-driven parameter assignment in a single forward pass. It achieves high accuracy and robust generalization to in-the-wild objects, paving the way for future physics-intensive embodied and robotic applications.

</details>


### [73] [Supervision-Adaptive Curriculum and Guarded Anti-Curriculum Learning for Dense Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29519)
*Chunming He,Dingming Zhang,Fengyang Xiao,Zhiwen Cao,Sina Farsiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaceShift 是一个"先稳定后挑战"（stabilize-then-challenge）的两阶段训练范式，将监督可靠性与时序更新价值解耦，用监督自适应课程选择加带防护的谱域后训练来提升密集预测精度，且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出仅以难度排序的课程学习不足以刻画密集预测中的监督可靠性：简单样本可能暴露捷径、困难样本可能极具信息量、而噪声或歧义样本可能误导训练。因此需要把"监督是否可信"与"当前时刻是否值得更新"这两个问题分开处理，并主张在模型稳定后再施加谱域挑战。

Method: 方法分为两个阶段。第一阶段（稳定）：从可用监督、增强一致性、模型分歧和预测确定性四方面估计可靠性；用时序统计指导样本加权，并用像素级不确定性权重降低早期不确定区域的影响。第二阶段（挑战）：在多个谱变换视图上对预测进行排序，排序依据包括监督一致性、鲁棒性、参考一致性与惩罚项；以冻结的第一阶段参考模型为基准构造成对偏好目标并配合锚点项来精炼预测。该框架兼容全监督、弱监督、半监督及模型自生成监督。

Result: 在上下文纠缠内容分割（CECS）、更广泛的密集预测任务，以及基础模型与跨模态适配实验中均取得精度提升；针对性诊断显示在测试设置下池化校准（pooled calibration）与损坏鲁棒性有所改善。方法保持部署架构不变，且不增加任何推理计算。

Conclusion: PaceShift 表明：在密集预测中应将监督可靠性与时序更新价值分离，并采用"先稳定、后挑战"的训练节奏，这一思路可带来稳定的精度与校准收益，同时对部署零额外开销，具备较好通用性。论文代码将公开。

Abstract: Curriculum learning orders samples by difficulty, but difficulty alone does not establish supervision reliability for dense prediction. Easy samples can expose shortcuts, hard samples can be informative, and noisy or ambiguous samples can mislead training. Building on our conference method CurriSeg, we present PaceShift, a stabilize-then-challenge paradigm combining supervision-adaptive curriculum selection with guarded spectral post-training. A learning-dynamics view motivates separating supervision reliability from temporal update value and applying spectral challenges after stabilization. The first phase estimates reliability from available supervision, augmentation consistency, model disagreement, and prediction certainty. Temporal statistics guide sample weighting, while pixel-uncertainty weights reduce the early influence of uncertain regions. The second phase ranks predictions from multiple spectral views using supervision agreement, robustness, reference consistency, and penalties. A pairwise preference objective measured against the frozen Phase-I reference, together with an anchor, refines the prediction. The framework supports full, weak, semi-supervised, and model-derived supervision. Experiments across context-entangled content segmentation (CECS), broader dense-prediction tasks, and foundation-model and cross-modal adaptations show accuracy gains. Targeted diagnostics indicate improved pooled calibration and corruption robustness in the evaluated settings. PaceShift preserves the deployed architecture and adds no inference computation. Code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [74] [Melange: A Synthetic Visual Bias Benchmark for Multi-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29537)
*Razieh Rezaei,Ehsaneddin Asgary,Masoud Jalili Sabet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态社会偏见基准 Melange（7类，基于BBQ扩展、经VQA自动筛选+人工校验），评测23个开源MLLM在三种匹配设定下表现：切换到视觉输入平均降低公平性0.291，差距取决于模型家族而非规模，且非视觉理解所致（属性识别90–95%仍不免偏），偏见作答时约3/4选择刻板印象答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM广泛部署但其偏见研究远少于纯文本LLM，存在现实社会风险；既有将偏见分析扩展到多模态的工作或仅覆盖少数偏见类别，或未确保被比较群体在图像中有明确视觉线索。

Method: 构建Melange合成视觉基准：以BigBench的BBQ数据集为基础，通过受控生成视觉场景扩展语境，使目标属性在视觉上清晰呈现；图像经两阶段验证（VQA自动筛查+人工标注者检查）。在text-only、image-only、image-with-context三种匹配设定下系统评测23个开源MLLM，隔离输入模态的公平性效应；辅以感知控制实验与极性感知的刻板印象对齐分析。

Result: 从文本转为视觉输入平均使公平性下降0.291，该差距随模型家族而非仅随规模变化；感知控制显示模型对图中属性识别准确率达90–95%，说明差距并非视觉理解失效所致，但模型仍未能弃答；模型给出有偏答案时，约四分之三选择人类刻板印象式回答。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM在视觉模态下存在显著且非源于感知能力的公平性缺口，需针对多模态偏见开展更系统的评估与缓解；Melange可作为覆盖面更广、验证更严格的诊断工具。

Abstract: Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have seen broad adoption, but their biases are still far less examined than those of standard LLMs, creating possible social harms when deployed in real-world settings. Prior efforts to extend bias analyses from LLMs into the multi-modal space either cover only a few bias categories or do not ensure that the groups being compared are clearly addressed by visual cues in the images. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Melange, a wide-ranging synthetic visual benchmark covering seven categories for assessing social biases in MLLMs. It is grounded in the widely used language-only BBQ dataset (Bias Benchmark for Question Answering) from BigBench, carefully extending its constructed contexts via controlled generation of visual scenarios so the target attributes appear visually and clearly. To maximize reliability, our generated images pass through two stages of validation: automated screening using visual question answering (VQA) and subsequent manual inspection by human annotators. By systematically evaluating 23 open-source MLLMs across three matched evaluation settings---text-only, image-only, and image-with-context---Melange isolates the fairness effect of the input modality. Switching from text to vision reduces fairness by 0.291 on average, a gap that varies by model family rather than by scale alone. A perception control confirms that this gap is not an artifact of visual comprehension: models identify the depicted attributes at 90--95\% accuracy yet still fail to abstain. When models do commit to a biased answer, polarity-aware stereotype alignment shows they choose the human-stereotypical response roughly three-quarters of the time.

</details>


### [75] [Hyperspherical Simplex Representations for Backward-Compatible Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29550)
*Simone Ricci,Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于softmax输出与logits的超球单纯形表示，实现独立训练模型间的向后兼容检索，无需兼容性专用训练、映射函数或结构修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型更新会改变内部特征表示，检索系统因此常需对整库(gallery)重索引，代价高昂；现有向后兼容方法依赖兼容性专用训练、学习式映射或结构改动，且往往显式依赖旧模型。

Method: 利用one-hot标签下softmax交叉熵训练诱导的单纯形结构作为跨模型更新的公共几何参照，构造超球单纯形表示；针对类别数增加导致单纯形扩张、旧类原型失配的问题，推导确定性线性变换恢复对齐；并在von Mises–Fisher类分布（更新间集中度递增）假设下，分析向后兼容形式化定义成立所需的两个几何条件。

Result: 对齐后的正则单纯形表示在期望意义下满足所识别的两个几何条件，为表示提供理论依据；在标准兼容性基准（含类别扩充、更强表达力架构、公共仓库独立训练模型）上取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 无需兼容性专用损失、学习式映射或架构修改，超球单纯形表示即可实现有效的向后兼容检索，并具备在明确假设下的理论支撑。

Abstract: Training modern AI models has become increasingly expensive, and updating deployed models can alter their internal feature representations, affecting applications built upon them. In retrieval systems, such updates often require complete gallery re-indexing, a computationally expensive process at scale. Existing backward-compatibility methods enable direct comparison of representations from updated and earlier models, avoiding re-indexing. However, they typically require compatibility-specific training, learned mapping functions, or architectural modifications, and often depend explicitly on the previous model. We propose hyperspherical simplex representations derived from softmax outputs and logits for backward-compatible retrieval across independently trained models. They leverage the simplex structure induced by softmax cross-entropy training with one-hot labels as a common geometric reference across model updates. Since this reference depends on the number of classes, adding new ones expands the simplex, misaligning existing class prototypes with their earlier counterparts. We therefore derive a deterministic linear transformation to restore their alignment. Our theoretical analysis of the formal backward-compatibility definition identifies two geometric conditions under which its inequalities hold in expectation, assuming von Mises--Fisher class distributions with increasing concentration across updates. Aligned regular-simplex representations satisfy these conditions, providing a theoretical basis for the proposed representations under the stated assumptions. Without using additional compatibility-specific losses, learned mappings, or architectural modifications, the proposed representations achieve competitive results on standard compatibility benchmarks, including expanded class sets, more expressive architectures, and independently trained models from public repositories.

</details>


### [76] [Generalized CycleGRPO for Fine-Grained Multimodal Understanding and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29552)
*Xin Zhang,Haochen Wang,Beibei Lin,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Robby T. Tan,Jason Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Generalized CycleGRPO，首个统一强化学习框架，将区域理解与定位在空间、时间、时空域中耦合，通过区域-文本循环一致性实现自评估，并发布 DOVE 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度多模态智能既要求模型理解指定区域，又要求从语言定位该区域，但现有方法用分离的目标与任务特定标注分别学习这两个方向，缺乏统一性且依赖参考描述或外部评判。

Method: 给定区域，MLLM 生成候选描述，再对每条描述做定位以重建输入区域；重建重叠度作为描述生成的可验证奖励，单条定位奖励改进逆向映射。以统一的区域空间形式，将循环从图像掩码与框扩展到视频区间与带时间戳的空间证据，无需参考描述或外部语言模型评判；视频场景下循环鼓励描述区分目标事件与相似重复事件。另提出 DOVE 对比基准评估时间区分性。

Result: 在 GroundingSuite 上 SAMTok 的总体 gIoU 从 57.5% 提升至 68.2%；GRES 平均 gIoU 从 71.2% 提升至 78.7%；Charades tIoU 达 51.9%、DOVE 时间证据覆盖率达 33.3%，显著优于 Qwen3-VL 初始化的 45.6% 与 14.8%。

Conclusion: 区域-文本循环一致性可作为多模态理解与定位的通用自评估原则，统一框架在空间、时间与时空任务上均取得一致提升。

Abstract: Fine-grained multimodal intelligence requires models to both understand a designated region and localize it from language. Existing methods learn these directions with separate objectives and task-specific annotations. We present Generalized CycleGRPO, the first unified reinforcement learning framework coupling region understanding and localization across spatial, temporal, and spatio-temporal domains. Given a region, a multimodal large language model generates candidate descriptions and then localizes each description to reconstruct the input region. Reconstruction overlap provides a verifiable reward for captioning, while individual localization rewards improve the inverse mapping. A common region-space formulation extends this cycle from image masks and boxes to video intervals and timestamped spatial evidence, without reference captions or an external language-model judge during reinforcement learning. In videos, the cycle encourages descriptions that distinguish a target event from similar recurrences. We introduce DOVE (Distinctive Occurrence-level Video-caption Evaluation), a human-verified contrastive benchmark for this temporal distinctiveness. CycleGRPO improves SAMTok's overall gIoU from 57.5% to 68.2% on GroundingSuite and average gIoU from 71.2% to 78.7% on GRES. For temporal tasks, it achieves 51.9% tIoU on Charades and 33.3% Temporal Evidence Coverage on DOVE, compared with 45.6% and 14.8% for the Qwen3-VL initialization. These results establish region-text cycle consistency as a general self-evaluation principle for multimodal understanding and localization. Models, code, and DOVE are available at https://devinxzhang.github.io/Generalized-CycleGRPO-Page/.

</details>


### [77] [Rethinking Underwater Image Enhancement: Learning through Text-Guided Degradation Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29569)
*Hongri Tian,Wenxue Li,Tian Ye,Sixiang Chen,Lujia Wang,Xianying Wang,Xinhu Zheng,Gang Hua,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的退化感知训练框架：文本条件扩散生成多样退化配对数据，配合渐进退化课程与透射引导的单步扩散模型，实现鲁棒的水下图像增强并在多个基准上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成对清晰-退化数据集规模有限或难以获取，且无法充分覆盖真实水下环境的多样退化，导致增强模型泛化受限，因此需要真实且可控的退化建模。

Method: 三部分：(1) 文本条件退化增强流水线，用文本引导扩散模型合成语义一致的多样退化样本，为后续课程学习提供配对数据；(2) 渐进退化课程，按多属性与难度分级组织训练以促进均衡学习；(3) 透射引导的单步扩散模型，利用渐进过程应对退化程度跨度大与恢复歧义问题。

Result: 在多个水下基准上的大量实验表明所提方法性能优越。

Conclusion: 通过可控退化合成与课程式训练结合单步扩散恢复，可有效提升水下图像增强的泛化性与鲁棒性；代码与模型将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Existing paired clean-degraded datasets have advanced learning-based underwater image enhancement, but they remain either limited in scale or difficult to acquire, and still fail to adequately cover the diverse degradations encountered in real-world underwater environments. Such scarce and insufficient data ultimately restrict the generalization of underwater image enhancement models, highlighting the need for realistic and controllable degradation modeling to better capture real underwater conditions and improve enhancement robustness. To this end, we propose a unified degradation-aware training framework for the underwater image enhancement task. A Text-Conditioned Degradation Augmentation Pipeline uses a text-guided diffusion model to synthesize diverse and semantically consistent degraded samples, providing paired data to support the subsequent curriculum learning strategy. The proposed Progressive Degradation Curriculum organizes training across multiple attributes and difficulty levels to promote balanced learning, and a Transmission-Guided One-Step Diffusion Model leverages this progressive process to better address the wide range of degradation severity and restoration ambiguity in underwater images. Extensive experiments on multiple underwater benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed method achieves superior performance. Our code and models will be released upon the publication of this work.

</details>


### [78] [Unlocking Spatial Grounding in Flow-Matching Models via SNR Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29581)
*Martin Thißen,Elke Hergenröther*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过调整训练时的信噪比(SNR)分布，将训练焦点转向高噪声阶段，可在不做任何架构改动的情况下显著提升流匹配模型的细粒度空间布局控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型虽在图像合成上成功，但细粒度空间控制仍困难，通常依赖辅助结构适配器或复杂的推理时引导。现代视觉-语言文本编码器已蕴含空间坐标表征能力，但生成模型未能有效利用。作者据此探究SNR分布是否是空间定位的关键影响因素。

Method: 构建几何基元合成基准，将结构机制与自然图像的纹理复杂性隔离；提出严格的颜色感知布局度量以量化空间保真度；系统分析流匹配模型在不同SNR分布下的空间定位表现，并识别高噪声区域为"关键学习阶段"；进一步考察分辨率、宽高比方差以及FLUX.1等模型的噪声调度影响。

Result: 中心化SNR分布训练布局遵循率仅25.9%；转向高噪声阶段后提升至80.8%，接近VAE重建上限83.6%；该偏移需求随分辨率与宽高比方差增大而增强，FLUX.1等模型的保守噪声调度不足以实现精确布局；在COCO上平均精度从1.0提升至57.3。

Conclusion: SNR分布是流匹配模型空间定位的决定性因素，仅通过分布调优即可解锁最先进的布局遵循能力，无需架构修改，并可迁移至真实图像。

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in image synthesis, achieving fine-grained spatial control remains a significant challenge, typically requiring auxiliary structural adapters or complex inference-time steering. While modern vision-language (VLM) text encoders possess the latent capacity to represent spatial coordinates, generative models often fail to leverage this information. We demonstrate that the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) distribution is a critical determinant of spatial grounding in flow-matching models. Using a novel synthetic benchmark of geometric primitives, we isolate structural mechanics from textural complexity inherent in natural imagery and introduce a rigorous color-aware layout metric to quantify spatial fidelity. We show that training on a centered SNR distribution results in poor layout adherence (25.9%). Instead, we identify high-noise regimes as a "critical learning phase" for spatial grounding. Shifting the training focus toward these stages increases layout adherence to 80.8%, effectively reaching the 83.6% reconstruction ceiling of the underlying variational autoencoder. We find that the necessity for this shift scales with resolution and aspect ratio variance, and that the conservative noise schedules employed by models like FLUX.1 are insufficient for precise layout adherence. Finally, we show that these findings translate to real-world imagery via the COCO dataset (1.0 vs. 57.3 Average Precision), where state-of-the-art layout adherence is unlocked through distribution tuning alone, without architectural modifications.

</details>


### [79] [CineEvolve: An Agentic Self-Evolution Framework for Cinematic Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29590)
*Jingzhe Zhang,Peixuan Zhang,Genying Li,Bing Ma,Shuchen Weng,Si Li,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CineEvolve——将导演(Director)与评论家(Critic)统一于单一视觉-语言策略的智能体自我进化框架，通过交替强化学习与 Evolutionary GRPO 实现电影级多镜头视频生成，并构建 40K 轨迹数据集 CineTraj。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影级多镜头视频生成需要叙事规划与迭代精修，但现有方法要么缺乏叙事推理能力，要么采用无策略更新的前馈式流程，难以像真实电影制作那样"指导—反馈"迭代改进。

Method: 1) 在一个视觉-语言策略中统一 Director（发现缺陷、提出修改）与 Critic（给出批评反馈）；2) 采用交替强化学习训练：Director 暴露问题以强化 Critic，更新后的 Critic 再引导 Director 做针对性修订；3) 提出 Evolutionary GRPO (E-GRPO)，借助历史锚定(historical anchoring)与修订保护(revision safeguarding)抑制优势膨胀，稳定协同进化；4) 构建 CineTraj 数据集，含 4 万条覆盖创作、批评、修订的电影化轨迹。

Result: 大量实验表明 CineEvolve 优于现有方法；代码将开源于给定 GitHub 地址。

Conclusion: 将导演与评论家统一在单一策略中并以交替强化学习协同进化，配合 E-GRPO 稳定训练，可有效提升电影级多镜头视频生成质量，叙事规划与迭代精修得以在统一框架内完成。

Abstract: Cinematic multi-shot video generation requires both narrative planning and iterative refinement. Existing methods either lack narrative reasoning or rely on feed-forward workflows without policy updates. In this paper, we propose CineEvolve, an agentic self-evolution framework for cinematic multi-shot video generation that unifies a Director and a Critic within a single vision-language policy. Emulating real-world filmmaking that couples iterative direction with critical feedback, we train CineEvolve via alternating reinforcement learning, where the Director surfaces flaws to sharpen the Critic, and the updated Critic guides the Director's targeted revisions. To stabilize co-evolution, we introduce Evolutionary GRPO (E-GRPO), leveraging historical anchoring and revision safeguarding to mitigate advantage inflation. Additionally, we contribute CineTraj, a 40K cinematic trajectory dataset spanning creation, critique, and revision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CineEvolve outperforms existing approaches. Our code will be available at https://github.com/JingZhe-8/CineEvolve.

</details>


### [80] [No Frames, Just Frequencies: Bandwidth-Efficient Deployment-Time Adaptation for Smart Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29607)
*Francesco Corti,Moritz Becker,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"只传频率、不传帧"的带宽高效源端辅助自适应框架：智能眼镜端计算紧凑频率描述子替代原始图像上传，主机端据此合成OOD数据并适配模型副本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署在智能眼镜上的深度网络需适应数据分布漂移，但受严格隐私与设备/带宽约束，无法直接上传相机帧做云端自适应。

Method: 眼镜端计算紧凑频率描述子并发送给持有模型副本的主机；主机将描述子映射为分布漂移算子，用于合成OOD数据并按预定协议适配模型副本。在ImageNet-C混合损坏上用ConvNeXtV2-Femto、DeiT-Tiny、TinyViT-5M评估，并覆盖Visual Wake Words人物分类与MS-COCO目标检测。

Result: 仅由描述子驱动的自适应恢复了监督oracle增益的93–95%（三种架构）、82%（Visual Wake Words）、94–98%（MS-COCO）；描述子分辨率从224降至28像素时性能平缓退化，实现213×–784×的带宽压缩。

Conclusion: 以频率描述子替代原始帧可在保障隐私与带宽的前提下实现接近oracle的自适应，且对分辨率下降具鲁棒性，适用于资源受限的边缘视觉设备。

Abstract: Deep neural networks deployed on smart glasses must adapt to changes in the distribution of incoming data while adhering to strict privacy and device constraints. We present No Frames, Just Frequencies, a bandwidth-efficient source-assisted adaptation framework that characterizes distribution shifts using compact frequency descriptors computed on-device. Rather than transmitting camera frames, the glasses compute and send frequency descriptors to a host device that contains a replica of the model. The host device maps the received frequency descriptor to a distribution shift operator that synthesizes out-of-distribution data and adapts a model replica using a pre-defined adaptation protocol. We evaluate the framework on mixtures of ImageNet-C corruptions using convolution-based (ConvNeXtV2-Femto), self-attention-based (DeiT-Tiny), and hybrid (TinyViT-5M) vision models, on person classification (Visual Wake Words), and on object detection (MS-COCO). Our results reveal that adaptation driven solely by the transmitted descriptors recovers $93-95\%$ of the supervised oracle adaptation gain on all three architectures, $82\%$ on Visual Wake Words and $94$--$98\%$ on MS-COCO. Notably, adaptation quality degrades gracefully as the descriptor resolution decreases from $224 $ to $28$ pixels, consistently across architectures, enabling bandwidth reductions from $213\times$ to $784\times$ across the frequency descriptors.

</details>


### [81] [Diff-IrisPAD: Modeling Bona Fide Iris Distributions for Unsupervised Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29611)
*Geetanjali Sharma,Harsh Yadav,Tanmay Deshpande,Shubham Ashwani,Aditya Nigam,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Diff-IrisPAD，基于Brownian Bridge Diffusion的无监督虹膜呈现攻击检测框架，仅用真实样本建模，通过重建不一致性检测攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虹膜PAD依赖带攻击数据的监督学习，泛化到未见攻击和新传感器能力有限。

Method: 基于Brownian Bridge Diffusion建模真实虹膜分布，不使用攻击样本；从退化输入重建干净虹膜纹理，并利用ViT打分与像素级度量评估重建不一致性来判别攻击。

Result: 在大规模多传感器数据集（12.5万+图像，NIR与VIS，8类攻击）上取得总体ACER 30.52，显著优于扩散基线，并对未见攻击表现出强泛化性。

Conclusion: 验证了基于扩散的真实分布建模对鲁棒、可扩展虹膜PAD的有效性。

Abstract: Iris recognition systems are vulnerable to presentation attacks (PAs), including textured contact lenses, print replays, and synthetic irises. Existing PAD methods rely on supervised learning with attack data, limiting generalization to unseen attacks and new sensors. We propose Diff-IrisPAD, an unsupervised framework based on Brownian Bridge Diffusion that models bona fide iris distributions without using attack samples. The method reconstructs clean iris textures from degraded inputs and detects attacks via reconstruction inconsistency using ViT scoring and pixel-level measures. Experiments on a large-scale multi-sensor dataset (125K+ images, NIR and VIS, 8 attack types) show that Diff-IrisPAD achieves an overall ACER of 30.52, significantly outperforming diffusion-based baselines while demonstrating strong generalization to unseen attacks. These results highlight the effectiveness of diffusion-based bona fide modeling for robust and scalable iris PAD.

</details>


### [82] [An interpretable deep learning method for early hepatocellular carcinoma diagnosis on multiphase CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29616)
*Emma Sarfati,Alexandre Bône,Pietro Gori,Sisi Yang,Marc-Michel Rohé,François Nicolas,Jeong-Min Lee,Jeong Hee Yoon,Maxime Ronot,Isabelle Bloch,Christophe Aubé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可解释人工智能方法dLI-RADS，结合神经网络预测与反映LI-RADS主要特征的手工定量影像测量，经逻辑回归集成，用于多期CT上肝细胞癌概率估计；内部集病灶级准确率80%对比LI-RADS的74%，达非劣效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝细胞癌早期诊断对保留根治性治疗机会至关重要；LI-RADS提供多期CT无创诊断的标准化框架，但其主要视觉影像特征评估一致性差，尤其在小病灶中难以稳定判读。

Method: 开发dLI-RADS：将神经网络预测与反映LI-RADS主要特征的手工定量影像测量相结合，通过逻辑回归集成，从手工分割病灶的多期CT图像估计HCC概率，并保持与LI-RADS影像概念的可解释对齐。多中心回顾性研究纳入具多期CT且至少一个肝脏病灶的患者，排除>40 mm病灶；HCC以组织学为参考标准，非HCC采用复合参考标准；内部验证集以10,000次重复bootstrap非劣效检验（5%界值）比较病灶级准确率，外部测试集比较敏感度。

Result: 内部评估集中dLI-RADS对LI-RADS病灶级准确率非劣效：80% vs 74%（p = 0.04）；外部测试集敏感度比较结果摘要未完整给出。

Conclusion: dLI-RADS在保持与LI-RADS概念对齐的可解释性前提下，可提升病灶级诊断准确率，有望作为LI-RADS的辅助工具改善小病灶的判读一致性。

Abstract: Early diagnosis of hepatocellular carcinoma is critical to preserve access to curative treatment options. The Liver Imaging Reporting and Data System (LI-RADS) provides a standardized framework for non-invasive diagnosis on multiphase computed tomography, based on major visual imaging features. However, these features may be difficult to assess consistently, particularly in small liver lesions. We developed dLI-RADS, an interpretable artificial intelligence method designed to estimate the probability of hepatocellular carcinoma from multiphase computed tomography images of manually segmented liver lesions, while remaining aligned with LI-RADS-related imaging concepts. dLI-RADS combines neural network-derived predictions with handcrafted quantitative imaging measurements reflecting LI-RADS major features and integrates these inputs using logistic regression. In this multicenter retrospective study, patients with multiphase computed tomography and at least one liver lesion were included, while lesions larger than 40 mm were excluded. Histology served as the reference standard for hepatocellular carcinoma, and a composite reference standard was used for non-hepatocellular carcinoma lesions. The lesion-level accuracy of dLI-RADS and LI-RADS were compared on the internal evaluation set using 10,000-repetition bootstrap non-inferiority tests, with 5% margins and significance levels. On the external test set, sensitivities were similarly compared. dLI-RADS was non-inferior to LI-RADS for lesion-level accuracy in the internal evaluation set, achieving 80% accuracy compared with 74% for LI-RADS (p = 0.04)...

</details>


### [83] [FogTTA: Online Test-Time Adaptation for Robust Transformer-based Object Detection in Foggy Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29618)
*Ali Abedi,Q. M. Jonathan Wu,Ning Zhang,Shiqi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FogTTA：面向雾天场景的在线测试时自适应框架，用教师-学生结构提升RF-DETR目标检测器的鲁棒性，无需源数据或重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶目标检测模型在训练数据与真实运行条件间存在域偏移，清晰天气训练的模型在雾天（能见度与对比度下降）性能显著退化，亟需无需源数据的在线自适应方案。

Method: 以RF-DETR为检测器，采用教师-学生架构进行在线测试时自适应：教师对弱增强目标输入生成伪标签，经非极大值抑制与置信度过滤精炼；学生对强增强样本以Varifocal损失学习以抑制伪标签噪声；教师通过指数滑动平均更新以保证稳定持续适应。

Result: 实验表明FogTTA优于先前基线，在保持实时性能的同时提升了检测精度与稳定性。

Conclusion: FogTTA验证了教师-学生式在线测试时自适应可在无源数据、无重训练条件下有效缓解雾天域偏移，兼顾精度与实时性。

Abstract: Object detection models for autonomous driving commonly experience substantial performance drops when deployed under adverse weather due to the domain shift between training data and real-world operating conditions. This degradation is especially evident when models trained on clear-weather images encounter foggy environments with reduced visibility and contrast. To address this challenge, we introduce FogTTA, an online test-time adaptation framework designed to improve the robustness of Transformer-based object detectors in fog. Using RF-DETR as the underlying object detector, FogTTA enables real-time adaptation to the streaming target domain without requiring source data or retraining. The framework follows a teacher-student design, where the deployed model serves as the teacher and generates pseudo labels from weakly augmented target inputs. These predictions are subsequently refined through non-maximum suppression and confidence filtering. The student model then learns from strongly augmented target sample using the Varifocal loss to mitigate pseudo-label noise. The teacher is updated via exponential moving averaging to ensure stable and continuous adaptation. Experiments show that FogTTA outperforms prior baselines, delivering improved detection accuracy and stability while maintaining real-time performance.

</details>


### [84] [OpenEarth-OVS44: A Multimodal Remote Sensing Dataset for Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29642)
*Yimin Wei,Hongruixuan Chen,Hao Zheng,Hao Liu,Jian Song,Yuting Zhu,Junshi Xia,Naoto Yokoya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OpenEarth-OVS44多模态基准，含290,587个专家核验掩码、2,832幅RGB图像与1,341对配准RGB-SAR，覆盖三国40城、统一44类、9大类目，设RGB、多云多模态、跨区域三条评测轨道，训练后未见类mIoU最高提升7.1、已见类提升27.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感开放词汇分割对跨区域、新类别的地物理解至关重要，但现有数据集类别覆盖有限、分类体系过粗，难以支撑城市功能区划、碳核算、灾后评估等所需的细粒度语义理解。

Method: 构建OpenEarth-OVS44基准：亚米级分辨率、法国/日本/美国40城、2,832幅RGB与1,341对严格配准RGB-SAR、44类统一分类（9大类）；设三条评测轨道——纯RGB细粒度基准、含多样合成云况的多模态耐候轨道、留出跨区域轨道；在代表性开放词汇分割模型上做广泛实验。

Result: 在该基准上训练可显著提升细粒度分割性能，相较最强免训练基线，未见类别最高提升7.1 mIoU，已见类别最高提升27.6 mIoU。

Conclusion: 该工作以细粒度、多模态、跨区域的统一基准填补遥感开放词汇分割评测空白，验证了训练数据对泛化与细粒度理解的增益；数据集与代码将公开。

Abstract: In remote sensing, open-vocabulary segmentation is particularly important for flexible and scalable land-cover understanding across diverse regions, as it improves generalization to novel categories without requiring exhaustive pixel-level annotations. Despite its great promise, progress in this area remains largely constrained by existing datasets with limited category coverage and overly coarse taxonomies, and therefore struggles to support fine-grained semantic understanding required in real-world applications such as urban functional zoning, carbon accounting, and post-disaster assessment. To address these challenges, we present OpenEarth-OVS44, a multimodal benchmark with 290,587 expert-verified segments from 2,832 RGB images and 1,341 strictly registered RGB-SAR pairs at sub-meter spatial resolution, covering 40 cities across France, Japan, and the United States under a unified 44-class taxonomy organized into 9 base categories. Three evaluation tracks are provided: an RGB-only fine-grained benchmark, a multimodal track with diverse synthetic cloudy conditions for weather-robust evaluation, and a held-out cross-region track. Extensive experiments on representative open-vocabulary segmentation models demonstrate that training on OpenEarth-OVS44 substantially improves fine-grained segmentation performance over the strongest training-free baseline, with improvements of up to 7.1 mIoU points on unseen categories and 27.6 mIoU points on seen categories. The dataset and code will be released.

</details>


### [85] [Joint Semantic-Channel Coding and Modulation for Generative Image Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29643)
*Hao Cao,Jingkai Ying,Yinhuan Huang,Zhijin Qin,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemQAM将图像源表征与QAM符号联合设计，用VQ索引映射调制符号并让信道损伤直接参与码本学习，实现生成式图像语义信道联合编码调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数字图像传输把信源编码、信道编码和调制分开优化，限制了源表征对信道损伤的自适应能力。

Method: 用矢量量化（VQ）表示潜在特征，将每个VQ索引映射为QAM符号，并更新被检测符号对应的码字，使信道损伤直接影响码本学习；同时以接收码字的条件期望近似接收端重建，抑制自回归编码中的误差传播；统一模型支持多SNR与多CBR，并提供SemQAM-C以兼容传统源编码。

Result: 相同LDPC与QAM设置下，SemQAM-C在LPIPS与DISTS上优于OneDC且复杂度显著更低；相比分离式对照，在LPIPS匹配时减少18.57%的信道使用，并优于所比较的JSCC方法。

Conclusion: 联合语义信道编码与调制能有效利用信道自适应表征，兼顾传统源编码能力，体现联合编码的增益。

Abstract: Digital image transmission typically optimizes source coding, channel coding, and modulation separately, limiting how source representations adapt to channel impairments. We propose SemQAM, a joint semantic-channel coding and modulation scheme for generative image transmission converting source to modulated symbols, such as standard QAM symbols. Channel impairments create two challenges: they can corrupt the latent representation at the receiver, and this corruption can compound through autoregressive coding, degrading reconstruction quality. First, SemQAM represents latent features with vector quantization (VQ), maps each VQ index to a QAM symbol, and updates the codeword corresponding to the detected symbol so that channel impairments directly affect codebook learning. Moreover, SemQAM uses the conditional expectation of received codewords to approximate the receiver reconstruction and reduce error propagation during autoregressive coding. SemQAM supports multiple SNRs and CBRs in a unified model, while also supporting conventional source coding through SemQAM-C. Experiments show that (i) under the same LDPC and QAM settings, SemQAM-C outperforms OneDC in LPIPS and DISTS with substantially lower complexity, showing that the unified model retains competitive capability for source coding. (ii) Compared with this separated counterpart, SemQAM reduces channel uses by 18.57% at matched LPIPS and outperforms the compared JSCC methods, demonstrating the benefit of joint coding.

</details>


### [86] [MS2CAST: A Video Anomaly Detection Framework via Multi-Scale Spatial Cross-Attention and Efficient Spatiotemporal Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29649)
*Ali Safaei,Mohammad Kazemi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MS2CAST：端到端半监督预测式视频异常检测，用MViTv2-S与ConvNeXt-T多尺度交叉注意力编码，并引入SwinLSTM或VMRNN两种隐空间时空模块替代光流，配合L1+GDL+MS-SSIM损失，在三大数据集上取得竞争性AUC且显存峰值低于1GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督VAD中异常样本稀缺且多样；现有方法依赖计算昂贵的光流网络建模运动，且CNN有效感受野高度局部化，限制空间表征能力。

Method: 端到端半监督预测式框架：(1) 多尺度交叉注意力编码器融合ViT（MViTv2-S）与CNN（ConvNeXt-T）的层级特征以互补感受野；(2) 在隐空间集成两种可互换时空模块——含Swin Transformer的SwinLSTM与含Vision Mamba块的VMRNN，免去光流估计；(3) 目标函数组合L1、梯度差分损失GDL与多尺度MS-SSIM，兼顾像素精度、锐度与结构完整性。

Result: 在UCSD Ped2、CUHK Avenue、ShanghaiTech上帧级ROC-AUC与顶尖方法相当，同时GPU显存峰值低于1GB。

Conclusion: 以跨架构多尺度融合与隐空间时空建模替代光流，可在极低显存开销下实现有竞争力的VAD性能，验证了轻量化高效方案的可行性。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) is a challenging task for surveillance systems due to the scarcity and diversity of anomalous events. While semi-supervised VAD approaches have advanced the field, many rely on computationally intensive optical flow networks to capture motion dynamics. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are also widely used for spatial encoding, but their effective receptive field is highly local. To address these limitations, we introduce MS2CAST, an end-to-end semi-supervised predictive VAD framework. For spatial encoding, we employ a multi-scale cross-attention encoder that fuses the hierarchical feature representations from a vision transformer (MViTv2-S) and a CNN (ConvNeXt-T) to exploit their distinct receptive fields. To model spatiotemporal dynamics without optical flow estimation, we integrate two interchangeable spatiotemporal modules in the latent space. One module (SwinLSTM) incorporates a Swin Transformer, while the other (VMRNN) integrates Vision Mamba blocks. Our objective function combines L1 loss, Gradient Difference Loss (GDL), and Multi-Scale Structural Similarity Index Measure (MS-SSIM) to ensure pixel-wise accuracy, sharpness, and structural integrity. Evaluations across the UCSD Ped2, CUHK Avenue, and ShanghaiTech datasets demonstrate frame-level Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) Area Under the Curve (AUC) scores that are competitive with top-tier techniques while maintaining a peak GPU memory usage of under 1 GB.

</details>


### [87] [Spatially Gated Demographic Steering for Debiasing Multi-Person Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29651)
*Venkatesh Thirugnana Sambandham,Torsten Schon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间门控引导（SGS），通过DiT注意力图提取逐人掩码并施加独立人群向量，实现多人场景中每人独立人口统计引导，兼顾图内多样性与聚合平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型复现社会刻板印象，而现有缓解方法对整个场景施加单一人口统计校正。在多人场景生成中导致权衡：全局引导虽改善聚合平衡，却使图内人物同质化（如Embedding Arithmetic使图内多样性低于未引导基线），文本/潜空间投影方法增益有限。

Method: SGS在关键去噪时间步从DiT内部注意力图提取逐人掩码，在后续步骤中于每个掩码内施加不同的人口统计向量；采用轻量分割器作用于冻结的DiT注意力特征，在早期时间步定位主体；无需微调、反演或布局指定。

Result: 在偏斜职业提示上，SGS在小幅影响图像质量、保持聚合平衡竞争力的同时，获得较高的图内种族与性别多样性；在架构不同的FLUX.1-Dev与SD3.5-Large上均得到验证。

Conclusion: SGS通过空间解耦的逐人引导，缓解多人场景中聚合平衡与图内多样性之间的权衡，且具有免微调、模型无关的通用性。

Abstract: Text-to-Image models reproduce societal stereotypes, and existing mitigation applies one demographic correction to the whole scene. In multi-person scene generation this creates a trade-off: global steering improves aggregate balance only by homogenizing the people within an image. Steering-based methods like Embedding Arithmetic drive the intra-image diversity below the unsteered baseline. Text- and latent-projection approaches shift the aggregate skew with only marginal gains in intra-image diversity. We present Spatially Gated Steering (SGS), which steers the demographics of each person independently by extracting per-person masks from the Diffusion Transformer's (DiT) internal attention maps at a critical denoising timestep and applying a distinct demographic vector within each mask for the remaining steps. SGS needs no fine-tuning, inversion or layout specifications, and uses a lightweight segmentor over frozen DiT attention features to localize subjects at early timesteps. On skewed occupational prompts, SGS reaches high intra-image racial and gender diversity while keeping aggregate balance competitive and image quality near baseline, a result we validate on two architecturally distinct diffusion transformers, FLUX.1-Dev and SD3.5-Large. Codebase and model weights are available at https://github.com/cvims/spatially-gated-steering

</details>


### [88] [Learning to Reason Like a Cytopathologist:Clinically Grounded Multiscale Evidential Learning for Cervical Cytology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29665)
*Ajeet Kumar Singh,Nehal Mishra,Anurag Tripathi,Santanu Chaudhury,Dorien Herremans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出多尺度证据学习框架，将宫颈细胞学沿有序病变连续谱建模，并以Dempster–Shafer证据理论融合三尺度证据，实现"可信决策"与"证据不足"的可解释分流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫颈细胞学的ASC-US等意义未明类别本身处于定义性的形态学灰色地带，诊断模糊性不可避免；临床决策支持不仅需要给出标签，还需区分"证据足以支持确定判断"与"需进一步评估"的样本，而常规分类模型无法显式表达这一区别。

Method: 采用有序疾病连续谱+临床依据的软锚点建模；以共享冻结的DINOv2骨干分别编码核形态、胞质特征、邻域细胞构筑三种空间上下文，避免早期融合掩盖尺度间分歧；将校准后的预测转换为Dempster–Shafer信度质量，并以可靠性折扣进行融合，使冲突与支持不足保持显式未承诺；融合信度状态驱动可解释路由，区分高置信分类与不可约证据不足。

Result: 在RIVA与CRIC Cervix两个公开常规细胞学数据集上一致泛化；不确定性与诊断难度强相关，错误预测的不确定性为正确预测的1.94–4.13倍。

Conclusion: 以证据理论显式表征模糊性可提升宫颈细胞学AI的可靠性与可解释性，实现面向临床分诊的"拒判"能力，而非强行决策。

Abstract: Cervical cytology presents a fundamentally ambiguous diagnostic landscape, where equivocal categories such as Atypical Squamous Cells of Undetermined Significance (ASC-US) occupy a morphological grey zone that is definitional. For clinical decision support, the goal extends beyond assigning a definitive label to every sample: cases must also be distinguished by whether the available evidence supports a confident decision or warrants further consideration, a distinction conventional classification models are not designed to explicitly represent. We propose a multiscale evidential framework that models cervical cytology along an ordinal disease continuum using clinically grounded soft anchors, representing progression from benign reactive changes to precancerous lesions and malignancy. Three biologically motivated spatial contexts--nuclear morphology, cytoplasmic characteristics, and neighbourhood cellular architecture--are independently encoded using a shared frozen DINOv2 backbone, preserving inter-scale disagreement that early fusion may obscure. Calibrated predictions are transformed into Dempster--Shafer belief masses and fused with reliability discounting, allowing conflicting evidence and insufficient support to remain explicitly uncommitted rather than forcing a decision. The fused belief state drives interpretable routing that separates confidently classified cases from those with irreducible evidential insufficiency, enabling clinically meaningful triage. Across two independent publicly available conventional cytology datasets, RIVA and CRIC Cervix, the framework generalises consistently, with uncertainty strongly associated with diagnostic difficulty: incorrect predictions carry 1.94x-4.13x higher uncertainty than correct ones.

</details>


### [89] [Rethinking Post-hoc Debiasing of Large Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29688)
*Divyaansh Mertia,Chirag Bhutra,Himanshu Shekhar,Shubham Bagwari,Pratik Mazumder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文指出主流 CLIP 后处理去偏方法依赖"偏置特征需被文本识别与表达"，在双共现偏置场景下会显著失效；作者发现冻结编码器几何结构中最接近的跨类训练对几乎总共享伪相关属性，据此提出无需偏置标注、无需 LLM 的线性映射后处理去偏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 等大预训练模型作为冻结特征提取器时，嵌入会继承训练数据中的伪相关，带来偏置。现有事后去偏研究多保持骨干不变，但作者发现这些方法隐含共同前提：偏置特征必须可被识别并用文本表达。该依赖并非无害——当存在多个共现偏置属性（如 UrbanCars 基准）时，近期方法甚至不如在冻结特征上训练的普通分类器，即使提供真值偏置身份也无法弥合差距。

Method: 实验揭示失效机制：所寻模式在冻结特征几何中直接可观测——被冻结编码器置于最近位置的跨类训练对几乎总共享同一伪相关属性，且与偏置类型无关、无需偏置标签。基于此提出新方法：识别这类"最近跨类对"，学习单一线性映射对特征进行变换，实现事后去偏。

Result: 在 UrbanCars 等多偏置基准上验证：依赖文本表达偏置的现有方法在多共现偏置下退化为不如 vanilla 分类器，供给真值偏置身份亦无法弥补；所提方法在所有数据集上取得具有竞争力的性能，且不使用偏置属性标注，也不依赖任何 LLM。

Conclusion: 事后去偏对"偏置需文本化表达"的依赖是可被规避的结构性瓶颈；冻结特征空间的几何近邻关系本身即编码了伪相关信号，仅凭单一线性映射即可高效去偏，为无需标注、无需语言模型的后处理去偏提供了简洁实用的范式。

Abstract: Large pretrained models such as CLIP are widely used as frozen feature extractors. However, their embeddings inherit spurious correlations from training data, introducing bias into the features. A growing body of research has sought to address this issue through post hoc debiasing, leaving the backbone untouched. We discover that despite differing mechanisms, these methods require the bias feature to be identified and expressed in text. This dependency is not innocuous. In this work, we experimentally demonstrate that this requirement incurs an unforeseen penalty in certain scenarios. For example, on the UrbanCars benchmark, having two co-occurring bias attributes, the recent methods underperform a vanilla classifier on biased frozenbackbone features, and even providing ground-truth bias identities does not close the gap. We show that the pattern that such methods seek is readily observable in the frozen geometry. Specifically, cross-class training pairs placed closest together by the frozen encoder almost always share a spurious attribute, regardless of the bias type, without requiring access to the bias labels. Based on this, we propose a novel approach to identify such pairs and learn a single linear map that transforms the features to perform post-hoc debiasing. The proposed approach achieves competitive performance across all datasets without access to bias attribute annotations or any LLMs.

</details>


### [90] [Efficient Generative Image Compression with Quantized Latents Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29691)
*Hao Cao,Yinhuan Huang,Wenqi Guo,Zhijin Qin,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GR-GIC，一种兼顾感知质量与快速推理的生成式图像压缩方法，通过引导细化（GR）提升低码率重建、通过重组的投影残差向量量化（P-RVQ）加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强感知质量的GIC依赖高延迟的预训练扩散模型，而近期轻量GIC虽降低延迟但感知质量较差。作者观察到：轻量GIC在低码率下，粗粒度确定性量化会把对应不同细节的邻近编码器潜变量坍缩到同一点，导致这些细节难以恢复。

Method: 提出GR-GIC：(1) Guided Refinement（GR），将熵上下文引导的潜变量细化与自适应扰动训练正则化相结合，在低码率下共同改善重建；(2) reorganized projected residual vector quantization（P-RVQ），把残差更新等价重组为低维查表，暴露更多并行性、减少顺序计算以实现快速推理。

Result: 在Kodak上，相比OneDC，GR-GIC在LPIPS、DISTS、PSNR、FID上分别取得23.19%、37.71%、22.30%、22.14%的BD-rate节省；高度优化实现在NVIDIA A100上编解码耗时4.60 ms和5.98 ms；重组P-RVQ在相同FP32设置下平均加速3.14倍；在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上延迟与轻量GIC方法相当。

Conclusion: GR-GIC在保持快速推理的同时实现有竞争力的重建性能，缓解了感知质量与低延迟之间的权衡，并适用于边缘设备部署。

Abstract: Generative image compression (GIC) delivers strong perceptual quality at low bitrates. However, GIC methods with strong perceptual quality often rely on pretrained diffusion models with high latency, while recent lightweight GIC methods reduce latency but deliver lower perceptual quality. We observe that, for lightweight GIC methods at low bitrates, coarse deterministic quantization can collapse nearby encoder latents corresponding to different fine details to the same point, making these details difficult to recover. Motivated by this, we propose GR-GIC, an efficient GIC that delivers competitive reconstruction performance with fast inference. For better performance, we introduce Guided Refinement (GR), which combines latent refinement guided by entropy context with adaptive perturbations for training regularization, jointly improving reconstruction at low bitrates. For fast inference, we introduce reorganized projected residual vector quantization (P-RVQ), which equivalently reorganizes residual updates into low-dimensional lookups to expose greater parallelism and reduce sequential computation. Experiments on Kodak show that for performance, compared with OneDC, GR-GIC achieves BD-rate savings of 23.19%, 37.71%, 22.30%, and 22.14% for LPIPS, DISTS, PSNR, and FID, respectively. For fast inference, the highly optimized implementation of GR-GIC encodes and decodes in 4.60 and 5.98 ms on an NVIDIA A100. Specifically, the reorganized P-RVQ implementation achieves a $3.14\times$ average speedup over the original implementation under the same FP32 setting. On an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX, GR-GIC achieves latency comparable to lightweight GIC methods.

</details>


### [91] [Forward-Only Learning for Shared-Kernel Wavelet Neural Networks without Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29697)
*Yongfei Guo,Xuan Sun,Ang Li,Zhao Zhang,Yuanhao Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于CMA-ES的全局前向训练框架，用于共享核小波网络，仅用6次训练集覆盖即超越Adam/BP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播依赖反向梯度与局部更新，深层网络中易出现梯度消失/爆炸；Forward-Forward虽回避反向传播，但基本局限于局部或逐层学习，缺乏全网整体的前向学习方案。

Method: 针对共享核小波神经网络，构建以CMA-ES为核心的全局前向学习框架；引入结构感知的参数分解与通道级信用分配，将搜索空间因子化为三个子空间进行优化。

Result: 在BSDS500上，测试MSE由4.1783降至4.0130，PSNR由42.621 dB提升至42.787 dB；训练仅需6次训练集覆盖，而BP需1500个epoch。消融显示结构分解与对齐信用分配比增大种群规模更关键。

Conclusion: 验证了紧凑重建网络全网前向优化的可行性，为资源受限成像及边缘/近传感器系统提供了一条无需反向传播的优化路径。

Abstract: Backpropagation remains the dominant training paradigm, using backward gradients and local updates that can suffer from vanishing or exploding gradients in deep networks. Although the Forward-Forward algorithm avoids conventional backpropagation, it remains mainly local or layer-wise, motivating global whole-network learning using forward evaluations only. For shared-kernel wavelet neural networks, we therefore propose a CMA-ES-based global forward-only learning framework. We further introduce structure-aware parameter decomposition and channel-specific credit assignment, factorizing the search into three subspaces. On BSDS500, our method reduces test MSE from 4.1783 to 4.0130 and improves PSNR from 42.621 to 42.787~dB over Adam/BP without backward propagation. Beyond these gains, training requires only 6 training-set coverages, compared with 1500 epochs for BP. Ablations show that structural decomposition and aligned credit assignment are more important than increasing population size, demonstrating the feasibility of whole-network forward-only learning for compact reconstruction networks. These results suggest a potential route toward forward-only optimization of compact reconstruction models for resource-constrained imaging and edge or near-sensor systems.

</details>


### [92] [Learning to Measure and Diagnose: A Clinical-Dictionary Radiomic Bottleneck Model for Thyroid Nodule Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29738)
*Guibin Zhang,Weier Luo,Lei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出临床字典放射组学瓶颈模型，用于甲状腺结节超声分类，在保持分类竞争力的同时提供可核查的定量证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声分类预测常缺乏与临床熟悉描述相联系的证据；放射组学测量虽可通过临床字典支撑解释，但需保证从超声图像中可靠估计，并在优化分类性能时保持测量保真度。

Method: 模型包含三部分：Family-Aware Radiomic Learning（FARL）在特征族间均衡监督下估计测量；Fidelity-Guided Bottleneck Modeling（FGBM）构建固定瓶颈，并在保真度引导下联合优化测量估计与分类；决策证据分解通过线性分类器追溯特征贡献，并按临床字典类别组织。

Result: 在 ThyroidXL 与 TN3K 数据集上取得有竞争力的分类性能；案例研究展示了良性与恶性结节的特征贡献。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法可实现可解释分类，并提供由临床知识引导、可核查的定量证据；代码已在线公开。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound supports the assessment of nodule malignancy, but classification predictions often lack evidence linked to familiar clinical descriptions. Radiomics provides explicitly defined measurements that can support such explanations through a clinical dictionary. However, using these measurements as interpretable evidence requires reliable estimation from ultrasound images and preservation of measurement fidelity when optimizing classification performance. We propose a clinical-dictionary radiomic bottleneck model for thyroid nodule classification. Within this model, Family-Aware Radiomic Learning (FARL) estimates measurements with balanced supervision across feature families. Fidelity-Guided Bottleneck Modeling (FGBM) constructs a fixed bottleneck, followed by joint optimization of measurement estimation and classification under fidelity guidance. Decision evidence decomposition then traces feature contributions through a linear classifier and organizes them by clinical dictionary categories. Experiments on ThyroidXL and TN3K show competitive classification performance, while case studies illustrate feature contributions for benign and malignant nodules. These findings indicate interpretable classification with inspectable quantitative evidence informed by clinical knowledge. Code is available online.

</details>


### [93] [APED-CXR: Adaptive Privileged Evidence Distillation for Chest X-Ray Grounding with Image-Only Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29740)
*Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Nishi Kanta Paul,Jamil Fayyad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出APED-CXR：用冻结的多模态教师模型向仅11.1M参数的紧凑学生模型蒸馏报告引导的空间证据，学生测试时只需图像即可同时预测疾病与病灶框。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型胸片模型若具备病灶定位能力更利于部署，但框标注稀缺，且报告感知的多模态模型推理代价高；因此需要在不依赖框标注、推理仅用图像的前提下获得定位能力。

Method: 以冻结的多模态教师（输入影像、报告与查询类别）对学生当前证据图所提出的区域进行评价，其支持度分数作为学生在该区域池化证据分数的训练目标；学生由共享证据图同时输出疾病预测与框。关键设计是训练中持续刷新学生提案供教师评价。

Result: 在PadChest训练、PadChest-GR留出集评估：刷新提案将框级定位F1（IoU>=0.5）从0.302提升至0.338（+0.036，95% CI [0.022,0.049]），诊断AUC保持；改用随机框、固定稠密框集、教师生成框或报告规则替代教师评价提案时增益消失，过期两次的提案仅恢复部分增益。NIH ChestX-ray14上框召回由0.423升至0.447。

Conclusion: 报告引导的空间证据可被有效蒸馏到小型单模态学生模型中，且增益来源于教师对学生当前提案的动态评价，而非额外框监督；方法在无需框标注、推理仅需图像的条件下实现了可行的定位性能。

Abstract: Compact chest X-ray (CXR) models that localize findings suit deployment, but box annotations are limited and report-aware multimodal models are expensive at inference. We introduce APED-CXR, which distills report-informed spatial evidence from a frozen multimodal teacher into a student with 11.1M parameters that predicts diseases and boxes from shared evidence maps and requires the image alone at test time. During training, the teacher uses the radiograph, report, and queried class to evaluate regions proposed from the student's current evidence maps, and its support scores serve as training targets for the student's pooled evidence scores within these regions. Trained on PadChest and evaluated on held-out PadChest-GR, refreshing the student's proposals for teacher evaluation improves box-level localization F1 from 0.302 with fixed warm-up proposals to 0.338 (IoU >= 0.5; gain 0.036, 95% CI [0.022, 0.049]), while maintaining diagnostic AUC. This gain depends on the teacher checking current proposals: with equally many random boxes, a fixed dense box set, teacher-generated boxes, or report rules instead of the teacher, F1 stays near the fixed-proposal level, and proposals two refreshes old recover only part of the gain. On NIH ChestX-ray14, a secondary test set, box recall also rises from 0.423 to 0.447.

</details>


### [94] [LEAF-Mamba: Feature Adaptation and Fusion for Whole Slide Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29746)
*Lubin Gan,Jing Zhang,Mingjun Ma,Linhao Qu,Dachun Kai,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LEAF-Mamba框架，联合进行潜在证据适配与融合，用于冻结特征的WSI分析：重建正则化适配器学习紧凑任务相关表示，并行Mamba与卷积流分别建模长程依赖与序列局部交互，再经输入相关门控融合后做slide级池化。在诊断分类与生存预测上均优于强MIL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI为千兆像素且标注有限，slide级学习困难；现有MIL依赖冻结预训练编码器的patch表示聚合，但仅靠任务监督未显式约束特征适配过程中的信息保持，且Mamba将局部处理与长程传播耦合，缺乏用于融合的独立表示。

Method: LEAF-Mamba：1) 重建正则化适配器，在重构输入特征的同时学习紧凑、任务特定的表示；2) 并行Mamba流与卷积流分别捕获长程依赖与序列局部交互；3) 输入相关门控调制二者联合表示，随后进行slide级池化。

Result: 在多种特征提取器下，诊断分类与生存预测的平均性能均优于强MIL基线；消融/组件分析支持适配与融合模块的贡献。

Conclusion: 联合设计特征变换与上下文聚合，可将预训练表示有效转化为slide级预测，凸显适配与融合协同设计的价值。

Abstract: Whole slide image (WSI) analysis enables computational diagnosis and prognosis, but gigapixel images and limited annotations make slide-level learning challenging. Multiple instance learning addresses this task by aggregating patch representations from frozen pretrained encoders. Recently, Mamba has enabled efficient modeling of long patch sequences. However, task supervision alone does not explicitly constrain information recovery during feature adaptation, while Mamba couples local processing with long-range propagation rather than retaining separate representations for fusion. Motivated by these challenges, we introduce LEAF-Mamba, a framework that jointly addresses latent evidence adaptation and fusion for frozen-feature WSI analysis. A reconstruction-regularized adapter learns compact, task-specific representations while reconstructing the input features. Parallel Mamba and convolutional streams then capture long-range dependencies and sequence-local interactions, with an input-dependent gate modulating their joint representation before slide-level pooling. Experiments across feature extractors demonstrate improved average performance over strong MIL baselines in both diagnostic classification and survival prediction. Component analyses further support the contributions of adaptation and fusion. These findings highlight the value of jointly designing feature transformation and contextual aggregation to translate pretrained representations into effective slide-level predictions.

</details>


### [95] [UniRAP: Towards Unified Part-level Physical Affordance Reasoning and Actionable Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29768)
*Linfei Li,Ruining Hu,Lin Zhang,Zhong Wang,Fengyi Zhang,Ying Shen,Binqiang Wang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniRAP统一模型，将部件级物理可供性与语言指令映射为可执行的几何表示，通过条件多模态生成、统一可供性解码器（UAD）和课程式迁移训练实现检测、部件可供性分割与4-DoF交互位姿联合预测，在三项任务上达SOTA并保持动态视频时空一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言感知在自然语言与视觉观察对齐上进展显著，但将高层语义落地为部件级物理交互仍具挑战；现有方法难以把语言指令直接映射为可操作的几何表示。

Method: 将问题建模为条件多模态生成任务，通过统一接口token机制把视觉输入、文本指令与可选提示整合进共享时空表示空间；设计统一可供性解码器UAD，利用中间分割特征联合执行目标检测、部件级可供性分割与4-DoF交互位姿估计；并提出课程式迁移训练策略，由通用视觉解析逐步过渡到交互感知，提升有限文本标注下的数据效率。

Result: 在指代表达分割、可供性接地与交互位姿估计任务上取得SOTA性能，并在动态视频场景中保持较强的时空一致性。

Conclusion: UniRAP可作为面向语言引导物理操作的有效统一感知框架。

Abstract: Vision-language perception has achieved impressive progress in aligning natural language with visual observations, yet grounding high-level semantics into part-level physical interaction remains challenging. To address this gap, we propose UniRAP, a unified model for inferring part-level physical affordances and mapping language instructions to actionable geometric representations. UniRAP formulates this problem as a conditional multimodal generation task, integrating visual inputs, textual instructions, and optional prompts into a shared spatiotemporal representation space through a unified interface token mechanism. To predict executable contact geometry, we introduce a Unified Affordance Decoder (UAD), which jointly performs object detection, part-level affordance segmentation, and 4-DoF interaction pose estimation by leveraging intermediate segmentation features. In addition, we propose a curriculum-based transfer training strategy that progressively adapts the model from general visual parsing to interaction-aware perception, improving data efficiency under limited textual annotations. Experiments show that UniRAP achieves state-of-the-art performance on referring expression segmentation, affordance grounding, and interaction pose estimation, while maintaining strong spatiotemporal consistency in dynamic video scenarios. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of UniRAP as a unified perception framework for language-guided physical manipulation.

</details>


### [96] [Key Findings for Small UAS for Post-Disaster Damage Assessment from the CURSE'26 Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29777)
*Thomas Manzini,Stephen Johnson,Priyankari Perali,David Merrick,Robin Murphy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLARKE 建筑与道路损毁评估AI分类器在CURSE 2026演练中用大量未见正射影像评估，建筑评估3.5分钟内完成、Micro F1达0.834且跨佛州/密苏里州泛化良好，道路评估因错位与环境敏感性表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLARKE 已实际部署于两场飓风（Debby 与 Helene），但缺乏在更大规模、未见过的影像集上的性能验证；CURSE'26 演练提供了对州/地方机构与非政府组织的23支sUAS队伍开展遥感影像采集培训，并借此机会系统评估模型。

Method: 将 CLARKE 分类器纳入 CURSE 2026 灾难性无人遥感演练，对多组此前未见的正射影像进行批量推理；测量建筑与道路损毁评估的运行时间，采用 Micro F1 评估精度，且未做任何空间错位的人工校正，并在佛罗里达与密苏里两州比对以检验地理迁移性。

Result: 建筑与道路评估合计平均运行 3.5 分钟；建筑损毁评估 Micro F1 = 0.834（无人工错位校正），在佛州与密苏里州表现一致，显示出良好的地理迁移性；道路损毁评估表现较差，原因是对路网与影像间的空间错位、以及城市场景的光照与环境条件敏感。

Conclusion: 演练还给出对sUAS社区有价值的五点发现：sUAS与机器学习应用已常态化并被接受；外业队伍可通过择时飞行提升分类精度；演练是良好的运行测试但用于评估学习模型的价值有限；需边缘计算以突破无线上传瓶颈；sUAS与AI用于灾后响应在飞行员与数据管理层面涉及伦理与法律影响。

Abstract: The CLARKE building and road damage assessment AI classifier was incorporated into the Catastrophic Unmanned Remote Sensing Exercise (CURSE) 2026, which trained 23 small uncrewed aerial systems (sUAS) teams from state and local agencies and non-governmental organizations in Florida and Missouri on how to collect imagery for an incident command Remote Sensing Cell. Although CLARKE has been operationally deployed to two hurricanes (Debby and Helene), CURSE'26 presented an opportunity to evaluate the machine learning model with a larger set of never-before-seen orthomosaics. The results show that building and road damage assessment together ran in 3.5 minutes on average, and that building damage assessment achieved a Micro F1 of 0.834 with no manual adjustments to correct spatial misalignment and delivered that performance in both Florida and Missouri, demonstrating good geographic transfer. It also showed that road damage assessment performed poorly due to sensitivity to misalignment between road maps and the images, and to lighting and environmental conditions in an urban setting. The analysis of CURSE'26 presents five other findings of value to the sUAS community: that the use of sUAS and machine learning is now operationally normalized and accepted, sUAS teams in the field can improve classification accuracy by timing their flights, exercises are good operational tests but their value for evaluating learning models is less clear, edge computing is needed to overcome the wireless upload barrier, and the application of sUAS and AI to disaster response has ethical and legal ramifications for pilots and data managers.

</details>


### [97] [JointLight: Human-Centric Video Relighting with Asynchronous RGB-Irradiance Joint Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29780)
*Henglei Lv,Bailin Deng,Xiaoqiang Liu,Yichen Wang,Haoxian Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Lin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出异步联合建模范式，通过联合去噪RGB与真值辐照度帧实现细粒度光照控制的视频重打光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人本场景光传输建模困难；缺乏多样、高质量、精确标注的视频重打光数据集；端到端方法隐式建模光传输导致可控性差；路径追踪辐照度线索含伪影，直接作为控制信号损害生成质量。

Method: 关键观察：噪声腐蚀移除高频伪影、保留低频光传输。据此设计两阶段训练：强制模型联合去噪RGB与真值辐照度帧，并采用分离的时间步调度；第二阶段引入域匹配策略弥合辐照度线索与真值辐照度图之间的域差异。推理时采用异步劫持生成管线实现灵活光照控制。数据方面用游戏引擎构建带配对辐照度图的多光照高质量合成视频数据集。

Result: 仅在合成数据集上训练即在真实场景中表现稳健泛化，在光照保真度与生成质量上均优于先前方法。代码与数据集将公开。

Conclusion: 利用扩散噪声对高频伪影的抑制特性，可将辐照度作为显式但鲁棒的控制蓝图，实现精确且灵活的视频重打光，并具备良好的真实世界泛化能力。

Abstract: Understanding and modeling light transport in human-centric scenes is a longstanding challenge. While recent progress in diffusion models has boosted research in relighting, precise illumination control still lags, primarily due to the scarcity of diverse, high-quality, precisely labeled video relighting datasets. Existing end-to-end relighting approaches model light transport implicitly through neural networks, which substantially complicates controllability. In contrast, some works resort to path-traced irradiance cues as an explicit blueprint for light transport. However, such constructed irradiance cues are prone to artifacts and hinder generative quality when directly used as control signals. With the key observation that noise corruption removes high-frequency artifacts and preserves low-frequency light transport, we introduce an asynchronous joint-modeling paradigm that facilitates fine-grained illumination control. During two-stage training, we force the model to jointly denoise RGB and ground-truth irradiance frames with separate timesteps scheduling. A domain matching strategy is applied to the second stage to bridge the domain gap between irradiance cues and ground-truth irradiance maps. At inference time, we introduce an asynchronous hijacked generation pipeline that facilitates flexible illumination control. For training data, we develop a multi-illumination high-quality synthetic video dataset with paired irradiance maps using a powerful game engine. Trained exclusively on this dataset, our model exhibits robust generalization to real- world scenarios and outperforms prior approaches in both lighting fidelity and generative quality. The code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [98] [Open-Vocabulary Models and Label Propagation for Uncertainty-aware Pseudo-Labeling of Object Detection Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29788)
*Jonathan Klees,Timo Heller,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出半监督框架，利用开放词汇检测器生成区域提议与对象表示，通过对象级相似图传播概率标签，为未标注对象分配软标签并量化偶然与认知不确定性，在1%标注成本下COCO达40.5% AP，接近全标注的40.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测依赖大规模人工标注；若需通过软标签刻画标注不确定性，则每个对象需多次标注，成本进一步上升。目标是在少量人工标注下自动生成高质量、带不确定性的软标签。

Method: 基于开放词汇目标检测器生成区域提议和语义化对象表示；据此构建对象级相似图并传播概率标签信息；对未标注对象分配软标签，同时量化偶然不确定性（aleatoric）与认知不确定性（epistemic）。

Result: 在四个带软标签标注的检测基准上验证：仅用少量人工标注对象即可获得高质量标注；半自动标注训练的检测器性能与全人工标注相当；COCO半监督目标检测取得SOTA下游性能，1%标注成本下40.5% AP，几乎匹配全人工数据训练的40.9%。

Conclusion: 开放词汇模型与图标签传播结合可实现高效、准确且不确定性感知的伪标签生成，显著降低软标签标注成本。

Abstract: Object detection models rely on large-scale annotated datasets that require substantial manual annotation effort. The labeling costs further increase when annotation uncertainty is captured through soft labels, as multiple annotations must be collected for each object. We address this challenge by introducing a semi-supervised framework for the uncertainty-aware annotation of object detection datasets. Our approach leverages open-vocabulary object detectors to generate region proposals and semantically meaningful object representations. Based on these representations, we construct an object-level similarity graph and propagate probabilistic label information. Our framework assigns soft labels to previously unlabeled objects, quantifying both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty. We evaluate our method on four object detection benchmarks with soft-label annotations and demonstrate that high-quality annotations can be obtained with only a small fraction of manually labeled objects. Furthermore, we show that detectors trained on semi-automatically annotated data achieve performance comparable to models trained on fully manually annotated data. We achieve state-of-the-art downstream detection performance for the task of semi-supervised object detection on the COCO dataset, with 40.5% AP virtually matching the performance of the model trained on fully manually constructed data (40.9%) at only 1% of labeling costs. These results highlight the potential of open-vocabulary models and graph-based label propagation for efficient, accurate and uncertainty-aware pseudo-labeling.

</details>


### [99] [Compositionality with Primitives: A Unified View from Euclidean to Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29811)
*Puru Vaish,Meelad Dashti,Christoph Brune,Pascal Mettes,Tatiana Tommasi,Nicola Strisciuglio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑组合零样本学习(CZSL)必须显式重组属性-对象对的假设，提出直接使用GDE分解出的原语表示即可识别未见组合，并系统分析嵌入几何与数据集结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作(GDE)认为视觉-语言模型嵌入空间含可分解语义结构，需通过向量相加重组原语来合成未见属性-对象组合；作者希望检验"组合泛化是否真的需要显式重组"这一前提。

Method: 在嵌入分布Frechet均值处的切空间中识别原语概念方向，得到GDE分解原语；设计新的组合打分函数，直接基于分解后的原语进行推理，而非显式合成组合向量；在CLIP、DINOv2、MERU、HyCoCLIP上比较欧氏、球面、双曲三种嵌入流形几何。

Result: 直接使用原语即可有效识别未见组合；组合结构在各模型上一致以原语表示形式出现；在MIT-States、VAW-CZSL、UT-Zappos50K、Clothing16K等CZSL基准上完成推理评估，并在Waterbirds与CelebA上验证组鲁棒性，同时揭示嵌入几何与数据集结构对组合推理的影响。

Conclusion: 组合泛化不必依赖显式的属性-对象重组，分解原语本身已足以支撑未见组合识别；嵌入流形几何与数据集结构是影响组合推理的关键因素，为CZSL提供更简洁的建模范式。

Abstract: Compositional zero-shot learning (CZSL) aims to recognize novel combinations of previously observed objects and attributes. Prior work showed that large-scale vision-language models encode decomposable semantic structures that can be recomposed to enable generalization. The Geodesically Decomposable Embeddings (GDE) framework formalizes this by identifying directions in the tangent space at the Frechet mean of the embedding distribution, which encode primitive visual concepts. Unseen attribute-object compositions can be synthesized through vector addition of these primitives. We revisit the assumption that compositional generalization requires explicit attribute-object pair re-composition. By means of new compositional scoring functions and extensive evaluations, we show that directly using the decomposed primitives suffices for recognizing unseen compositions. In that, we gain insights on how the geometry of the embedding manifold (Euclidean, spherical, or hyperbolic) and dataset structure affect compositional reasoning. Across models (CLIP, DINOv2, MERU, HyCoCLIP), we show that compositional structure consistently emerges as primitive representations, enabling inference on CZSL benchmarks (MIT-States, VAW-CZSL, UT-Zappos50K, Clothing16K) and group robustness on Waterbirds and CelebA. Code: https://github.com/nis-research/Compositionality-with-Primitives.

</details>


### [100] [GuidedSAM-Plume: An Expert-Guided Segmentation Framework for Volcanic Plumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29825)
*Taddeo D'Adamo,Simon Thivet,Catherine Pothier,Riccardo Simionato,Allan Fries,Jonathan Lemus,Costanza Bonadonna,Laure Tougne Rodet,Bertrand Kerautret*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出开源专家引导框架GuidedSAM-Plume，基于SAM2的零样本空间提示能力实现火山羽流与云的可交互分割，无需重训练即可在最小用户输入下显著优于全自动专用方案并保持火山学参数可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火山学中基于RGB图像的火山羽流与云几何表征受限于专用分割方案稀缺，且标注仍普遍依赖半手工方式，缺乏标准化与高效工具。

Method: 构建GuidedSAM-Plume开源框架，利用SAM2的零样本、基于空间提示的能力进行交互式分割；在挑战性数据集上通过专用分割指标与领域相关火山学描述符进行验证；并由专家火山学家在该框架下完成数据集重标注。

Result: 在最小用户输入下，分割性能显著优于全自动专用方案，同时保持所导出火山学参数的可靠性；重标注数据集形成更标准化的发布版本。

Conclusion: 无需重训练降低了火山学界的采用门槛，框架适用于复杂弥散状羽流形态，可推广至更广泛的环境科学领域；框架与数据集已开源发布。

Abstract: Quantitative analysis of environmental phenomena increasingly relies on image-based measurements. In volcanology, the geometric characterization of volcanic plumes and clouds from RGB images remains hindered by the scarcity of dedicated segmentation solutions and partially handcrafted annotations remain widely prevalent. We present GuidedSAM-Plume, an open-source expert-guided framework tailored for the interactive segmentation of volcanic plumes and clouds. It leverages the zero-shot, spatial prompt-based capabilities of SAM2. The framework was thoroughly validated on a challenging dataset through dedicated segmentation metrics and field-relevant volcanological descriptors. From minimal user inputs, it is able to significantly increase performance compared to fully automatic dedicated solutions while preserving the reliability of derived volcanological parameters. The absence of retraining requirements lowers adoption barriers within the volcanological community, and the framework's demonstrated suitability for complex, diffuse plume-shaped phenomena extends its applicability to broader environmental science domains. The re-annotated dataset, produced under the guidance of expert volcanologists within the proposed framework, ultimately yields a more standardized release. The framework and accompanying dataset are made available at https://github.com/tad-damo/GuidedSAM-Plume.

</details>


### [101] [Early versus Late RGB-Infrared Fusion for Counter-UAV Detection: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29828)
*Alberto De Zan,Denis Tavaris,Gian Luca Foresti,Ivan Scagnetto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文对比RGB-IR融合的早期（图像级）与晚期（决策级）两种策略用于无人机检测，实验表明早期融合原始性能更优，而晚期融合在传感器/帧失配场景下更具实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在恶劣环境与天气条件下提升无人机检测的准确率与精度，需研究可见光与红外多光谱融合的有效方式，并厘清两种融合层次的优劣。

Method: 实现并对比两种相反范式：图像级（早期）融合，将两光谱合成单幅图像后输入单一检测器；决策级（晚期）融合，融合两条检测流程的输出边界框以生成同时适用于两光谱的公共结果。在专用环境（明暗交替场景、天空/地面背景、5/25/60 m距离、不同高度、1X/2X/4X变焦）中广泛测试，并在对齐图像集上对两策略进行评估比较。

Result: 早期融合因训练初期即引入热红外信息而取得更优的原始检测性能；晚期融合则提供了可在传感器或帧失配情况下应用的创新检测流程。文中同时指出两者的优缺点。

Conclusion: 两种融合策略各有适用场景，应按实际运行优先级选择；研究为RGB-IR融合模态及无人机检测能力提升提供了实现考量与实用建议。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel strategy for UAV detection through visible (RGB) and infrared (IR) fusion, in order to improve accuracy and precision in harsh environments and weather conditions. In this research, two opposite approaches have been studied and implemented: the first concerns image-level (early) fusion, which merges two different spectra into a single image to which one detector is applied. On the other hand, the second approach, called decision-level (late) fusion, focuses on blending together the output bounding boxes of two different detection pipelines, aiming to create a common result that can be applied to both spectra simultaneously. Both strategies have been extensively tested in a dedicated environment, which included alternating low and high brightness scenes, different backgrounds (sky and ground), distances (5, 25 and 60 m), altitudes and zoom levels (1X, 2X and 4X). These tests allowed for the assessment of actual detection capabilities and system reliability in real-world applications. Furthermore, the two fusion strategies were evaluated and compared over a set of aligned images in order to highlight the better approach of the two. The results indicate that early fusion achieves better raw performance, since it incorporates the thermal information from the beginning of the training process. However, late fusion presents an innovative and useful detection workflow that can be applied in cases of sensor or frame misalignment. Both positive and negative aspects have also been pointed out and discussed, in order to give a more comprehensive overview. Finally, the paper outlines implementation considerations and practical recommendations based on operational priorities. Overall, the findings contribute to the research in RGB-IR fusion modalities, with the goal of enhancing UAV detection capabilities.

</details>


### [102] [Revealing Hidden Failure Modes in Chest X-ray Classification via Spectral Domain Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29830)
*Samuel Halimi,Loic Themyr,Arnaud Abreu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以数据为中心的频谱分析框架，通过将胸片投影到频域并提取紧凑的频谱能量分布画像，再对频谱画像做无监督聚类，发现模型失败并非随机分布，而是高度集中于特定频谱簇，从而无需真实失败标注即可预测模型可靠性并定位导致性能下降的数据切片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片异常检测的深度学习模型对细微分布漂移（采集技术、患者相关因素、预处理等）敏感，而传统误差分析依赖语义元数据或模型嵌入，容易掩盖导致性能退化的低层信号变化，难以揭示真正的失败模式。

Method: 数据为中心的自动化失败模式发现：将图像变换至频域，提取概括各频带信号能量分布的紧凑频谱画像；对这些频谱画像进行无监督聚类，并在簇层面关联模型失败情况，从而无需失败真值标注即可识别"盲点"与性能退化的数据切片。

Result: 模型失败并非随机分布，而是显著集中于特定频谱簇；频谱聚类能有效隔离模型"盲点"，可用于预测模型可靠性并发现导致性能下降的数据切片，且不依赖真实失败标注。

Conclusion: 频谱画像聚类为胸片异常检测提供了一种轻量、可解释且无需标注的失败模式发现手段，弥补了语义元数据与嵌入分析对低层信号变化的盲区，可作为数据质量诊断与可靠性评估工具。

Abstract: Deep learning models for chest X-ray anomaly detection remain vulnerable to subtle distributional shifts (e.g., acquisition technique, patient-related factors, and preprocessing). Traditional error analysis often relies on semantic metadata or model embeddings, which can mask low-level signal variations that degrade performance. In this work, we propose a data-centric framework for automated failure mode discovery using spectral analysis. We project images into the frequency domain and extract a compact profile summarizing the distribution of signal energy across frequency bands.
