By performing unsupervised clustering on these spectral profiles, we demonstrate that model failures are not randomly distributed, but are strongly concentrated within specific spectral clusters. This method effectively isolates "blind spots", enabling the prediction of model reliability and the discovery of performance-degrading data slices without requiring ground-truth failure annotations.

</details>


### [103] [How Do DiTs Denoise? An Aggregator-Predictor View of Attention and MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29854)
*Gyeonghun Kang,William Xingxu Chen,Xiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出DiT的"聚合器-预测器"视角：注意力实现输入自适应的上下文聚合，MLP实现逐patch预测，并用理论与实验支持这一功能分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）虽性能优异，但其如何表征图像内容、如何学习可泛化的复用规则缺乏组件级解释。作者希望为注意力与MLP赋予明确的功能角色，从而解释DiT的去噪与泛化机制。

Method: (1) 理论上建立GMRF先验下仅用部分上下文patch即可逼近全局贝叶斯去噪器，误差随上下文半径指数衰减；(2) 证明在聚合信息充分时，逐patch线性去噪器可逼近经验局部去噪器；(3) 在该框架下分析AdaLN调制注意力为输入-噪声相关的聚合器、MLP为学习到的key-value逐token预测；(4) 实验：在真实图像训练的DiT上测量噪声相关的去噪上下文与注意力质量分布；在CelebA64上以共享记忆patch预测器替换MLP；跨数据集组件移植（QK、VO、MLP）。

Result: 真实图像上训练的DiT表现出与解析GMRF设定相似的噪声相关去噪上下文，注意力质量呈现相应的展宽模式；共享记忆patch预测器可替代DiT MLP并取得有竞争力的去噪与生成性能；跨数据集移植显示QK负责空间加权、VO负责特征传输、MLP负责patch预测，兼容性模式与所提角色一致。

Conclusion: 研究给出了DiT去噪的组件级解释，将注意力与MLP分别对应为聚合器与预测器，为架构特定的微调与受控干预提供了依据。

Abstract: How do diffusion transformers (DiTs) represent image content and learn rules for reusing it to generalize? We develop an aggregator--predictor view of DiTs as input-adaptive local denoisers, in which context aggregation and patch prediction provide functional roles for interpreting attention and MLPs. We first establish that, for Gaussian Markov random field (GMRF) priors, the global Bayes denoiser can be approximated using only a subset of context patches, with error decaying exponentially in context radius. We further show that patchwise linear denoisers, often invoked to explain diffusion generalization, recover the empirical local denoiser with sufficiently informative aggregation. Within this view, AdaLN-modulated attention naturally parameterizes an input- and noise-dependent aggregator, while the MLP admits a learned key--value representation for tokenwise patch prediction. Our experiments support this view. DiTs trained on real images exhibit noise-dependent denoising contexts resembling those in the analytic GMRF setting, while attention mass exhibits a qualitatively corresponding broadening pattern. In a controlled CelebA64 study, shared-memory patch predictors used in place of DiT MLPs achieve competitive denoising and generative performance. Finally, cross-dataset component transplantation reveals compatibility patterns consistent with the proposed roles of QK in spatial weighting, VO in feature transport, and MLPs in patch prediction. Together, these results provide a component-level account of DiT denoising and motivate architecture-specific fine-tuning and controlled intervention.

</details>


### [104] [GeoMem-VLA: Internalizing 3D Scene Understanding into Temporal Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29866)
*Qinfan Zhang,Yifan Luo,Haoyuan Shi,Guanlin Liu,Han Dong,Haozhe Shan,Yong Dai,Xiangcong Ren,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMem-VLA 将显式 3D 几何监督内化为可学习的 GeoPlan 几何 token，并作为时序记忆持续更新，用于动作生成，无需在推理时调用 3D 教师模型或显式重建场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数 VLA 模型依赖 2D 观测表示，几何感知方法则需额外 3D 信息；且从单帧观测独立推断的几何往往被丢弃，未累积为持久任务表示。作者认为动作生成需要的是紧凑、任务相关且可连续更新的几何抽象，而非每步完整可渲染的 3D 重建。

Method: 提出 GeoMem-VLA 框架：用冻结的几何基础模型作为教师，以几何流匹配目标（针对当前场景点图与未来末端执行器轨迹）监督可学习的 GeoPlan token；这些 token 与观测表示的记忆集成，历史信息被检索、与当前观测自适应融合并按任务进程以事件帧形式巩固。推理时仅检索历史表示并与当前工作表示融合以预测动作，不调用 3D 教师、不做显式重建；该方法也不直接将 VLM 原生视觉嵌入与教师特征空间对齐。

Result: 经基准特定的下游适配后，LIBERO 平均成功率 98.9%，RoboTwin 干净/随机化设置分别为 91.10% / 91.24%；五个真实世界任务平均成功率 78.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将 3D 场景理解内化并加以记忆，是机器人控制的一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Geometric structure is essential for robotic manipulation and understanding the 3D world. Most VLA models rely on 2D representations of current observations, while geometry-aware approaches often require additional 3D information. However, geometry inferred independently from individual observations is often discarded rather than accumulated into a persistent task representation. Our key insight is that action generation needs a compact, task-relevant geometric abstraction that can be continuously updated, rather than a complete, renderable 3D reconstruction at every step. We introduce GeoMem-VLA, a VLA framework that internalizes explicit 3D supervision into dedicated geometric tokens and maintains them as temporal memory for action generation, without directly aligning the VLM's native visual embeddings with the teacher feature space. During training, a frozen geometry foundation model supervises learnable GeoPlan tokens through a geometric flow-matching objective over the current scene point map and future end-effector tracks. These tokens are integrated with the memory of observation representations, where historical information is retrieved, adaptively fused with current observations, and consolidated to retain event frames as the task progresses. At inference, the policy retrieves historical representations and fuses them with the current working representation to predict actions, without invoking the 3D teacher or explicitly reconstructing the scene. After benchmark-specific downstream adaptation, GeoMem-VLA achieves 98.9% average success on LIBERO and 91.10% / 91.24% on RoboTwin's clean/randomized settings. Across five real-world tasks, GeoMem-VLA achieves 78.0% average success. These results suggest that internalizing and remembering 3D scene understanding is a promising approach to robot control.

</details>


### [105] [Stochastic Nonlinearities Improve Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29875)
*Mohammad Sarhangzadeh,Amirreza M. Shebly,Doruk Oner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将随机激活切换重新定位为轻量级不确定性估计机制，无须集成训练即可从单一模型获得预测多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计方法常通过抑制中间特征、扰动输入或引入大量额外计算来获取预测多样性，代价高或信息损失大。

Method: 在前向传播中于多个非线性激活函数间随机采样，保持中间特征通路活跃，从而诱导预测多样性；单次训练模型即可估计不确定性，无需集成、任务特定不确定头或架构改造。

Result: 在分类、语义分割与分布外检测任务上，基于卷积与Transformer架构及多数据集评测，不确定性质量提升，同时保持有竞争力的主任务性能。

Conclusion: 随机非线性提供了一种有效且信息保持的预测变化来源，可作为视觉识别中的轻量级不确定性估计手段。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation is important across visual recognition tasks, providing information about the confidence and reliability of model predictions. Many uncertainty estimation methods obtain predictive diversity through mechanisms that can suppress intermediate features, perturb the input, or require substantial additional computation. Stochastic activation functions, where network units randomly select between multiple nonlinearities during a forward pass, have previously been used to improve optimization behavior, mitigate limitations of fixed ReLU activations, and increase output diversity. In this work, we reframe stochastic activation switching as a lightweight mechanism for uncertainty estimation. The proposed method samples between nonlinear activation functions, inducing predictive diversity while keeping intermediate feature pathways active. This enables uncertainty estimation from a single trained model without the training cost of ensembles, task-specific uncertainty heads, or architectural redesign. We evaluate the proposed approach across classification, semantic segmentation, and out-of-distribution detection using convolutional and transformer-based architectures on diverse datasets. Across these settings, stochastic activation switching improves uncertainty quality while preserving competitive primary-task performance, showing that stochastic nonlinearities provide an effective and information-preserving source of predictive variation for visual recognition.

</details>


### [106] [Expandable Modality Experts for Medical Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29881)
*DM BAPPY,Eunsu Kim,Hanjoo Lee,Sung Hak Lee,Sangjeong Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以可扩展“模态专家库”为核心的医学视觉-语言方法：Multiway Transformer 共享视觉计算、确定性路由的 FFN 专家与独立文本通路，支持无配对文本图像预训练与新模态增量扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像跨模态学习面临两大需求：可利用无配对文本的图像进行预训练；在吸纳新模态时保留已有表征而不发生灾难性遗忘。

Method: Multiway Transformer：共享视觉计算 + 确定性路由的前馈专家 + 独立文本通路；模态感知视觉 tokenizer 提供掩码预训练目标（7 模态、1940 万无配对图像行），再进行对比对齐（323 万图文对）；新增眼底与 OCT 专家从配对数据学习，原图像与文本嵌入路径冻结；ViT-Large 骨干。

Result: 9 模态 25 项基准中 24 项最优或并列最优（11 项零样本分类中 10 项、10 项线性探针全部、4 项检索全部）；零样本平均平衡准确率 0.674，线性探针 0.856，双向检索平均召回 20.70%；三随机种子序贯实验在匹配适配数据下，眼底与 OCT 检索分别提升 14.95 与 9.31 个百分点，前序眼底与病理任务无测得损失。

Conclusion: 该技术为医学视觉-语言表征的学习与扩展提供了模块化路径；保留性论证依赖既有计算路径与推理条件不变，并通过特征一致性检查在评估输入上验证。

Abstract: Learning across medical imaging modalities requires making use of images without paired text and accommodating new domains while retaining existing representations. We propose a medical vision-language technique built around an expandable bank of modality experts. Its Multiway Transformer combines shared visual computation with deterministically routed feed-forward experts and a separate text pathway. A modality-aware visual tokenizer supplies targets for masked pretraining on 19.4 million unpaired image rows across seven modalities; contrastive alignment then uses 3.23 million image-text pairs. Additional fundus and optical coherence tomography experts learn from paired data while the original image and text embedding paths remain frozen. With a ViT-Large backbone, the proposed technique achieves the best or tied-best result in 24 of 25 benchmark settings across nine modalities: 10 of 11 zero-shot classification benchmarks, all 10 linear probes, and all four retrieval benchmarks. Mean balanced accuracy is 0.674 for zero-shot classification and 0.856 for linear probing, and mean bidirectional retrieval recall is 20.70%. A separate three-seed sequential experiment with matched adaptation data yields fundus and OCT retrieval gains of 14.95 and 9.31 percentage points, with no measured loss on preceding fundus or pathology tasks. The preservation argument requires unchanged existing computation paths and inference conditions, and feature-identity checks verify retention on evaluated inputs. The proposed technique provides a modular approach to learning and expanding medical vision-language representations.

</details>


### [107] [ViBMR: A Large-Scale Multi-Sequence Brain MRI Dataset and Comprehensive Evaluation for Vietnamese Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29905)
*Dai Lam Le,Minh Khoi Nguyen,Son Dinh Nguyen,Amir Reza Jafari,Viet Ha,Nguyen Son,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi,Phi Le Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建ViBMR——首个大规模多序列脑MRI-越南语常规临床报告配对数据集（3,649名患者、11,875个序列，覆盖T1/T2/FLAIR/CE-T1），并建立脑MRI报告生成的综合评价体系与基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI报告自动生成可减轻放射科医师负担，但存在三重难点：脑MRI本质为三维体数据、各脉冲序列携带互补诊断信息、低资源临床语言标注数据极度稀缺。现有资源以英文报告、翻译文本或由任务型影像基准改造的数据集为主，无法代表常规低资源临床报告流程与异构多序列协议。

Method: 构建ViBMR数据集：3,649名患者、11,875个MRI序列，含T1、T2、FLAIR与增强T1，配对原生越南语常规临床报告。在此基础上搭建统一评测框架，覆盖代表性医学视觉-语言模型、多序列输入方式与特征聚合策略。

Result: 通过多模型、多序列输入与多种聚合策略的系统对比，得出关于数据集特性与模型行为的关键发现，并验证原生低资源临床数据集对可靠的越南语原生脑MRI报告生成的必要性。

Conclusion: ViBMR填补了多序列脑MRI与低资源语言原生临床报告配对的空白，为脑MRI报告生成提供了标准化评测基准与基线；数据集与基线模型将以非商业研究用途公开。

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation for multi-sequence brain MRI holds strong promise for alleviating radiologists' workload, yet it remains a demanding problem: brain MRI studies are inherently volumetric, each pulse sequence carries complementary diagnostic information, and annotated data are critically scarce for low-resource clinical languages. Existing brain MRI-report resources are still dominated by English reports, translated clinical text, or datasets adapted from task-specific imaging benchmarks, leaving routine low-resource clinical reporting and heterogeneous multi-sequence MRI protocols insufficiently represented. To bridge this gap, we introduce ViBMR, to the best of our knowledge, the first large-scale multi-sequence brain MRI dataset paired with routine Vietnamese clinical reports, comprising 3,649 patients and 11,875 MRI series across T1, T2, FLAIR, and contrast-enhanced T1 sequences. Building on ViBMR, we establish a comprehensive evaluation setup for brain MRI report generation, covering representative medical vision-language models, multi-sequence inputs, and feature aggregation strategies. The results provide key insights into dataset characteristics and model behavior, while demonstrating the necessity of native low-resource clinical datasets for reliable Vietnamese-origin brain MRI report generation. Our dataset and baseline models will be released for non-commercial research.

</details>


### [108] [Asking a Multimodal LLM Whether Someone Fell: Zero-Shot Screening of Low-Resolution Thermal Video in a Laboratory and an Occupied Home](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29913)
*Marcos Junca,Mauro Junca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将生产级多模态大模型 Gemini 3.8 Flash（temperature=0）作为事件触发式跌倒筛查器的决策模块，在公开 32x24 热成像数据集与 76 小时真实居家热成像录像上评估其跌倒判定性能，并与前代 Gemini 3.7 Flash 及两个在实验室数据上训练的常规检测器对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独居老人需要非侵扰式看护，热成像相机不采集人脸可保护隐私，而多模态大模型无需任务特定训练即可判断短视频，因此探索其作为跌倒筛查决策模块的实际部署表现（含误报率、提示词敏感性、分辨率鲁棒性等）。

Method: 按部署原型方式使用：每个短热成像片段一次请求，含音轨；在手工标注的 438 次摆拍跌倒与 563 个非跌倒窗口上测试，并在占用房间内 160x120 带声录制、超过 76 小时的日常录像中测试；记录保留/移除提示词中"离开画面不算跌倒"一句的影响、8x6 像素低分辨率下的表现、同一请求的重复一致性、模型解释的时间定位精度；与两个实验室训练的常规检测器在居家场景的误报率对比；释放帧级跌倒标注与全部模型回答。

Result: 留出受试者跌倒召回 69%（全体受试者 67%，排除床上跌倒），499 个非跌倒窗口零误报；居家 76 小时内 395 个片段零误报（95% 上界 1.2 次/天），仅捕获 9 次可见摆拍跌倒中的 4 次。移除该警示句后，留出受试者灵敏度由 69%升至 83%（3.8 Flash）、由 80% 升至 92%（3.7 Flash），居家可见跌倒从 4 次增至 9 次，日常活动仍无误报，但会把刻意躺倒判为跌倒。8x6 像素下灵敏度骤降至 34%；相同请求间不一致率 4%；判为跌倒时解释中的落地时刻与标注相差 1 秒以内的比例为 99%。两个常规检测器几乎检出全部留出实验室跌倒，但居家误报达 14.2 与 23.9 次/天。

Conclusion: 生产级多模态大模型在近乎零误报的居家条件下具备中等跌倒召回能力，提示词措辞对灵敏度影响显著，低分辨率下性能崩溃，且解释具备良好时间定位；相对常规检测器，其在真实居家场景的误报优势明显，但灵敏度仍需提升。

Abstract: Thermal cameras can watch over older people living alone without capturing faces, and multimodal large language models (LLMs) can judge a short video without task-specific training. We evaluate a production multimodal LLM, Gemini 3.8 Flash at temperature 0, as the decision stage of an event-triggered fall screener, used exactly as a deployed prototype uses it: one request per short thermal clip, with its sound track when there is one, and its predecessor Gemini 3.7 Flash on the same requests. On 438 staged falls and 563 non-fall windows that we located by hand in a public 32x24 thermal dataset, the deployed request called 69% of the held-out subjects' falls (67% over all subjects; bed falls excluded) and none of 499 non-fall windows, including people lying on the floor and getting up. Over 76 h of ordinary life in an occupied room, recorded at 160x120 with sound, it called none of 395 clips a fall (95% upper bound 1.2 false alarms per day) and caught 4 of the 9 staged falls the camera could see. One cautionary sentence in the prompt ("a person who goes out of view ... is not a fall") accounted for many of the misses: removing it raised sensitivity on the held-out subjects from 69% to 83% on 3.8 Flash and from 80% to 92% on Gemini 3.7 Flash, and in the home from 4 to 9 of the 9 visible falls, with no false call in ordinary living but at the price of calling deliberate lie-downs a fall. Sensitivity collapsed at 8x6 pixels (34%); identical requests disagreed on 4% of windows; and when the model called a fall, its explanation placed it within a second of the annotated landing 99% of the time. Two conventional detectors trained on the laboratory data caught nearly every held-out laboratory fall but raised 14.2 and 23.9 false alarms per day in the home. We release the frame-level fall annotations of the public dataset and every model answer on it.

</details>


### [109] [CausalAlign: Causal Alignment of Video Generation Models for Consistent Ego-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29915)
*Kai Zhu,Yintianrun Zhang,Shuwei Jiang,Ruolin Zuo,Yixin Chen,Duomin Wang,Miao Liu,Yin Li,Daquan Zhou,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CausalAlign：先用线性探针与因果干预诊断VGM中相机自运动表示，再通过delta-token空间对齐3D基础模型，显著提升几何一致性与具身任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式视频生成模型（VGM）视觉逼真，但长时序下相机自运动（ego-motion）易漂移，破坏几何一致性与轨迹稳定性；作者希望定位并强化模型内部对自运动的表征。

Method: (1) 诊断：用线性探针对VGM各层做探针与因果干预，刻画自运动精度如何被表示、使用与保持，发现去噪过程中呈"先升后降"模式；(2) 干预：在识别出的关键层，通过delta-token空间（编码相邻帧间变化，聚焦运动动态而非静态场景内容）将VGM表征对齐到3D基础模型。以Wan2.2在DL3DV与RealEstate10K上微调。

Result: 运动精度、几何一致性与时序平滑性大幅提升且保持视觉质量，几何一致性较基座模型提升超20%；微调后的VGM使world action model（WAM）在RoboCasa365移动操作任务上的成功率提升10个百分点。

Conclusion: 因果诊断指导的表征对齐能有效缓解扩散VGM的相机自运动漂移，并向下游具身模型迁移收益。

Abstract: Diffusion-based video generation models (VGMs) can produce visually plausible videos, yet their camera ego-motion often drifts over long horizons. Camera ego-motion determines how the viewpoint changes between frames and is essential for maintaining consistent geometry and trajectories. To address this problem, we propose CausalAlign, a causal alignment framework with two components. First, we diagnose how precise ego motion is represented, used, and retained within VGMs using linear probes and causal interventions, revealing a rise-and-fall pattern during denoising. Second, we strengthen ego-motion state in VGM by aligning the representations at the identified layers to a 3D foundation model through delta-token space. This space encodes the changes between consecutive frames, allowing the alignment to focus on ego-motion dynamics rather than static scene content. With Wan2.2 fine-tuned on DL3DV and RealEstate10K, CausalAlign can largely improve motion accuracy, geometric consistency, and temporal smoothness, while preserving visual quality. For example, it improves geometric consistency by more than 20% relative to the base model. Moreover, with the fine-tuned VGM, CausalAlign improves the success rate of world action models (WAMs) by 10 points on mobile manipulation tasks of RoboCasa365 benchmark. Overall, CausalAlign improves ego-motion consistency in diffusion-based VGMs and yields noticeable gains in embodied models.

</details>


### [110] [DewHand: Direct Metric World-Space Hand Motion Reconstruction from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29936)
*Naru Suzuki,Satoshi Ikehata,Takehiko Ohkawa,Yoichi Sato,Shuhei Kurita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DewHand：单阶段联合重建世界坐标系下的手部与相机运动，用VGGT-Ω场景几何特征+度量监督解决尺度歧义，用流匹配条件生成处理遮挡与出视野，提升世界空间精度与时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一视角视频恢复度量世界空间的手部运动对理解人机交互并迁移至具身智能体很重要，但存在三大难点：多阶段流水线会传播手部与相机独立估计的误差；单目观测导致物理尺度歧义；遮挡与出视野手部存在多种可能轨迹。

Method: 提出DewHand统一模型，在单一阶段流水线中于共享世界坐标系直接联合预测手部与相机运动，避免多阶段误差累积；融合VGGT-Ω的场景级几何特征与度量空间监督以消解尺度歧义，同时保留手部专用特征以维持细粒度关节结构；将重建建模为基于流匹配的条件运动生成，仅需两步采样。

Result: 在HOT3D与ARCTIC上，刚体对齐世界空间误差较StableHand分别从32.9降至28.4 mm、从76.2降至36.2 mm；HOT3D中三分之二片段相机尺度误差在10%以内（SLAM约四分之一）。零样本评测在HoloAssist、H2O、TACO上均取得最低WA-MPJPE。

Conclusion: 单阶段联合建模、几何基础模型特征与生成式流匹配范式相结合，可同时提升世界空间精度、时序一致性与跨设备泛化能力。

Abstract: Recovering metric world-space hand motion from egocentric video supports understanding human interactions and transferring them to embodied agents. Three challenges complicate this task: multi-stage pipelines propagate errors from independently estimated hand and camera motion; monocular observations leave physical scale ambiguous; and occlusion and out-of-view hands admit multiple plausible motion trajectories. We present DewHand, a unified model for direct joint reconstruction of world-space hand and camera motion. To avoid compounding errors from multi-stage estimation, DewHand jointly predicts hand and camera motion in a shared world coordinate system within a single-stage pipeline. To resolve metric-scale ambiguity, it combines scene-level geometric features from VGGT-$Ω$ with metric-space supervision, while hand-specific features preserve fine-grained articulation. To handle occlusion and out-of-view hands, we formulate reconstruction as conditional motion generation with flow matching. Under the same architecture, this generative formulation outperforms direct regression in both accuracy and temporal smoothness with only two sampling steps. Experiments on HOT3D and ARCTIC demonstrate improved world-space accuracy and temporal consistency over state-of-the-art methods. DewHand reduces rigid-aligned world-space error relative to StableHand from 32.9 to 28.4 mm on HOT3D and from 76.2 to 36.2 mm on ARCTIC. It also recovers camera scale within 10% on two thirds of HOT3D clips, versus about a quarter for SLAM. Moreover, in zero-shot evaluations on HoloAssist, H2O, and TACO, captured with different devices, DewHand achieves the lowest rigid-aligned world-space error (WA-MPJPE) on each dataset.

</details>


### [111] [Training-free Acceleration for Blind Compressed Image Restoration via Activation-Calibrated Singular Value Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29959)
*Suhang Yao,Xin Li,Xijun Wang,Yufei Wang,Yeying Jin,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的低秩加速方法 ASVD，通过对重复投影算子做激活校准的 SVD 分解，在盲压缩图像复原（CIR）模型中实现参数与计算量削减而不需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 Transformer 的盲压缩图像复原模型因密集堆叠的投影算子而部署代价高，亟需免训练的加速手段；同时均匀低秩目标与压缩图像的通道不均衡特征分布不匹配。

Method: 进行算子级实证分析：剖析计算集中位置、刻画压缩输入下的通道激活分布、分析代表性投影权重的奇异值衰减。据此将注意力、前馈、卷积与提示条件通路中的重复投影确定为低秩替换目标，并以 TSVD 作为免训练基线。进一步提出 ASVD：仅用少量压缩校准图像获取的轻量激活统计量，以通道激活幅度对分解加权，离线导出低秩因子，无需重训练与在线校准，保持原推理图不变。

Result: 在 PromptCIR 上取秩 64，ASVD 在四个标准基准、相同参数与 MAC 预算下 PSNR-B 一致优于 TSVD；参数减少 59%（34.75M→14.34M），256×256 输入计算量减少 16.1%（258.79→217.16 GMACs）。

Conclusion: 算子级实证分析可有效定位低秩替换目标，激活校准的 SVD（ASVD）为盲 CIR 提供了一种无需重训练、即插即用的加速方案，兼顾恢复质量与部署效率。

Abstract: Transformer-based models achieve strong performance on blind compressed image restoration (CIR) under unknown compression settings, but their densely stacked projection operators make deployment costly. We study training-free acceleration for such models through an empirical, operator-level analysis. Specifically, we profile where computation is concentrated, characterize channel-wise activation distributions under compressed inputs, and examine the singular-value decay of representative projection weights. These measurements identify the recurring projections in the attention, feed-forward, convolutional, and prompt-conditioning pathways as effective low-rank replacement targets that preserve the original inference graph. They further establish truncated SVD (TSVD) as a principled training-free baseline for blind CIR and reveal why a uniform low-rank objective is mismatched to the channel-imbalanced feature distributions of compressed images. Accordingly, we formulate Activation-Calibrated Singular Value Decomposition (ASVD) for this restoration setting, deriving low-rank factors entirely offline from pretrained weights and lightweight activation statistics gathered from only a few compressed calibration images. By weighting the decomposition with channel-wise activation magnitudes, our ASVD assigns larger penalties to errors on more active channels, requiring neither retraining nor online calibration and leaving the restoration pipeline intact. When instantiated on PromptCIR at rank 64, ASVD consistently improves PSNR-B over TSVD across four standard benchmarks under identical parameter and MAC budgets. At this operating point, the factorized model cuts parameters by 59% (34.75M to 14.34M) and computation by 16.1% (258.79 to 217.16 GMACs for a 256 x 256 input), enabling acceleration without retraining.

</details>


### [112] [Looking Ahead: Modeling Future Evolution in Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29989)
*Ruopeng Gao,Yukun Huang,Jiarui Hu,Shuai Wang,Wenbo Hu,Ying Shan,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Future Chunk Prediction (FCP) 辅助目标，与教师强制去噪联合优化，通过预测中间视觉上下文缺失的未来分块，迫使模型学习真实视觉演化，在更低训练算力下获得更优物理合理性，且不改变自回归推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型逐块生成视频，但在教师强制下，强时间冗余使模型可依赖局部视觉连续性作为当前分块去噪的捷径，从而削弱了对场景如何演化（visual evolution）的建模压力；仅生成合理延续并不代表真正捕捉了场景演化。

Method: Future Chunk Prediction（FCP）：在主教师强制去噪目标之外，联合优化一个辅助目标，要求模型在无法访问中间视觉上下文的情况下预测时间上间隔的未来分块。监督信号直接取自训练视频，无需额外预训练视觉编码器，推理流程保持标准自回归不变。

Result: 相比标准教师强制自回归训练，物理合理性（physical plausibility）更优，同时整体训练算力预算更低；可视化结果显示 FCP 促使表征空间更具结构性，对构建自回归世界模型具有潜在价值。

Conclusion: 未来分块监督是建模视觉演化的一种有效且高效的手段：在保持推理不变、不引入额外编码器的前提下，缓解教师强制的时间冗余捷径问题，并可能带来更结构化的表征，有利于自回归世界模型的发展。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models generate videos chunk by chunk, yet producing plausible continuations does not guarantee that they capture how scenes evolve. Under teacher forcing, strong temporal redundancy may allow local visual continuity to serve as a shortcut for current-chunk denoising, reducing the pressure to model visual evolution. We introduce Future Chunk Prediction (FCP), an auxiliary objective jointly optimized with teacher-forced denoising that predicts temporally separated chunks without access to the intervening visual context. Drawing supervision directly from training videos, FCP requires no additional pretrained visual encoder and leaves autoregressive inference unchanged. Our method achieves superior physical plausibility over standard teacher-forced autoregressive training with a lower overall training compute budget, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of future-chunk supervision for modeling visual evolution. Intriguingly, our visualizations suggest that FCP fosters a more structured representation space, which may be valuable for building autoregressive world models.

</details>


### [113] [Multiclass Boundary Refinement with Neural Cellular Automata for Remote Sensing Land Cover Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30007)
*Pascoassis Souza Santos Meira,Gilson Antonio Giraldi,Rafael Duarte Coelho dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出边界细化神经细胞自动机BRNCA，将二值rNCA推广到多类情形（Aitchison对数空间更新保持概率单纯形，并加入自由隐藏通道），对冻结分割器输出的类别概率逐像元迭代修正，平均BIoU提升2.36点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感影像语义分割的类别边界常偏离真实位置数像素，该位置误差集中于Boundary IoU所度量的细带区域，需要针对性修正边界而非整体重训分割器。

Method: 以冻结的预训练分割器输出的类别概率为初始状态，边界细化被建模为一个小型学习到的局部规则的迭代应用（BRNCA）；将二值rNCA推广到多类：可见通道通过对数空间（Aitchison）更新保持在概率单纯形上，并引入自由隐藏通道增强表达能力。

Result: 最优配置将平均BIoU相对基线分割器由46.63提升至48.99（+2.36点）；增益高度集中于不规则林地类（BIoU +8.5点）。

Conclusion: 轻量局部迭代规则可有效纠正多类分割的边界位置误差，且误差幅度与类别边界的分形维数相关，为按类别特性设计边界修正策略提供了线索。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of remote sensing imagery often places class boundaries a few pixels away from where they truly lie, and this position error is concentrated in the thin band measured by Boundary IoU (BIoU). We treat boundary refinement as the iterated application of a small learned local rule: the Boundary Refinement Neural Cellular Automaton (BRNCA), that starts from the class probabilities of a frozen, pre-trained segmenter and corrects them cell by cell. We generalise the binary rNCA to the multiclass case by keeping the visible channels on the probability simplex through an Aitchison (log-space) update and by adding free hidden channels. The best configuration improves the mean BIoU over the base segmenter by 2.36 points (from 46.63 to 48.99). The gain is concentrated on the irregular woodland class (+8.5 points BIoU); this suggests that position error is correlated with the fractal dimension of the class boundaries.

</details>


### [114] [ClunoGS: Compact Language 3D Gaussian Splatting via Unified Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30013)
*Jian Xu,Yi Li,Ying Huang,Siyi Wu,Bingzhe Li,Yuede Ji,Miao Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ClunoGS，一个面向 Language 3DGS 的紧凑压缩框架，将语义感知的高斯稀疏化与矢量量化统一为单一约束优化目标，在激进压缩下显著缓解语义表征退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Language 3DGS 通过为高斯基元关联语言特征实现开放词汇场景理解，但语义属性显著增加模型存储；现有 3DGS 压缩方法主要面向 RGB 渲染质量，若顺序执行稀疏化与量化，仅按渲染贡献剪枝会误删 RGB 贡献小但对语义预测关键的高斯，且其语义信息在被删后无法由后续量化恢复。

Method: 提出统一约束目标，联合建模渲染质量、语义准确率与紧凑度；在稀疏化阶段引入语义监督；采用交替优化，依次进行原始变量更新、稀疏化、量化与对偶变量更新。

Result: 在 LERF 与 ScanNet-10 基准的开放词汇定位与分割任务上，激进压缩下统一语义感知公式显著减轻了顺序压缩与语义无关稀疏化导致的语义表征退化；消融实验表明，联合协调稀疏化与量化相比独立应用能更好地保持语义精度。

Conclusion: 语义感知的稀疏化与量化应在统一框架内协同优化，而非串行处理，才能在高压缩率下兼顾渲染质量与开放词汇语义性能。

Abstract: Language 3D Gaussian Splatting enables open-vocabulary scene understanding by associating Gaussian primitives with language features. These semantic attributes increase model storage, while most existing 3DGS compression methods are designed mainly to preserve RGB rendering quality. One straightforward approach is to apply sparsification and quantization sequentially. However, pruning based only on rendering quality can remove Gaussians that have small RGB contributions but are important for semantic prediction. Once removed, their semantic information cannot be recovered by later quantization. We propose ClunoGS, a compact framework for Language 3DGS that combines semantics-aware Gaussian sparsification and vector quantization in a unified constrained objective. Semantic supervision is used during sparsification, and the objective jointly considers rendering quality, semantic accuracy, and compactness. We optimize the unified objective with alternating primal, sparsification, quantization, and dual updates. We evaluate ClunoGS on open-vocabulary localization and segmentation using the LERF and ScanNet-10 benchmarks. Under aggressive compression, our unified, semantics-aware formulation substantially alleviates the degradation of semantic representations caused by sequential compression and semantics-agnostic Gaussian sparsification. Ablations further show that jointly coordinating sparsification and quantization better preserves semantic accuracy than applying them independently.

</details>


### [115] [RealMe: Immersive Volumetric Video from Minimal Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30023)
*Chenxu Zhou,Zhiyuan Yu,Yudong Jin,Yinji Shentu,Yu Zhang,Jiaming Sun,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RealMe框架，借助高分辨率视频扩散模型将稀疏低分辨率多视角采集增强为2K、4D一致的视频，进而重建沉浸式体积视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集多视角重建能生成高质量体积视频，但采集成本高昂；已有生成式方法虽降低采集要求，却存在分辨率受限和4D不一致的问题。

Method: 构建统一框架：从采集视频生成辅助数据，与布局感知位置嵌入一同编码为潜变量；视频扩散模型将分解的时空注意力适配到多视角采集布局，跨视角与时间去噪并解码为增强视频以细化4D表示；并引入潜空间频率损失，缩小生成视频与真实采集的数据差距。

Result: 在公开DNA-Rendering基准和自建RealVV数据集上，RealMe可生成沉浸式体积视频，在视觉保真度与4D一致性上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将高分辨率视频扩散模型与多视角布局适配及频率域监督相结合，可在稀疏低分辨率采集条件下实现高质量、4D一致的体积视频重建。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of creating immersive volumetric videos from sparse and low-resolution capture resources. Conventional dense multi-view reconstruction methods can produce high-quality volumetric videos, but their reliance on costly capture setups makes them impractical in real-world scenarios. Recent approaches use generative models to relax capture requirements, but still suffer from limited resolution and 4D inconsistency. To overcome these constraints, we propose RealMe, a unified framework built around a high-resolution video diffusion model that enhances limited captures into high-resolution (2K), 4D-consistent videos for volumetric video reconstruction. Specifically, RealMe constructs auxiliary data from capture videos and encodes them into latents along with layout-aware positional embeddings. The video diffusion model adapts factorized spatio-temporal attention to the multi-view capture layout, denoises these latents across viewpoints and time, and decodes them into enhanced videos to refine the 4D representation. This adaptation helps the model capture temporal dynamics and multi-view relationships, leading to more 4D-consistent generation. To reduce the data gap between generated videos and real captures, we apply a latent frequency loss that provides frequency-domain supervision in latent space. Extensive experiments on the public DNA-Rendering benchmark and our self-captured RealVV dataset demonstrate that RealMe is able to create immersive volumetric videos and outperforms state-of-the-art methods in visual fidelity and 4D consistency.

</details>


### [116] [Plausible but Unimplantable: A Trustworthiness Audit of Shape Completion Foundation Models for Cranial Defect Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30035)
*Marek Wodzinski,Kamil Kwarciak,Daria Hemmerling,Mateusz Daniol*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对3个公开预训练形状补全模型（含2个基础模型）在239例颅骨缺损上做零样本评测，结果无一输出可植入；而任务专用体素模型除1例外全部可植入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅骨缺损植入物目前需专家在CAD中手工绘制，耗时且昂贵；作者希望验证新兴的形状补全基础模型能否以零样本方式替代这一手工环节。

Method: 将任务形式化为形状补全问题，审计3个公开预训练模型（2个为基础模型），在239例缺损（210例由公开颅骨数据集人工挖除生成、29例真实去骨瓣术后病例）上零样本应用；与"仅返回患者残余骨"这一基线对比，并检验预测材料能否在不与现存骨相交的前提下放置，即"可植入性"。

Result: 210例人工缺损中三个模型均未超越"什么都不做"基线；29例真实病例中仅一个模型以超过随机水平的表现胜出。但任何模型在任何配置下均无输出可植入（同时满足避开现存骨且补充多于基线的材料），而任务专用体素模型除1例外全部可植入。外观最像颅骨的输出并不更具可放置性；在该点密度下，输出表示本身无法读出植入物厚度。

Conclusion: 现可获得的形状补全基础模型不可信，按原样发布不可用于临床；厚度不可测是输出表示层面的固有属性而非单个模型问题；仍需大量研究才能构建可信的形状补全基础模型。

Abstract: A patient who has lost part of the skull vault needs an implant that fits the defect exactly, and such implants are still drawn by hand in CAD by specialists, which makes each case slow and expensive. The task can be considered as a shape completion problem, so pretrained completion models, and now the first shape completion foundation models, look like a way to remove that manual step. We audited three publicly released pretrained shape completion models, two of them foundation models, applied zero-shot to 239 cranial defects, some carved from public skull datasets and some real post-craniectomy cases. Each output was compared with simply returning the patient's own remaining bone, and tested for whether the predicted material could be placed without intersecting the bone that is still there. On the 210 carved defects none of the three improved on doing nothing, and on the 29 real cases only one did so beyond chance. Yet not one output, from any model in any configuration, was implantable: none managed both to stay clear of the bone the patient still has and to place more material than doing nothing, where a task-specific volumetric model, trained for this task and selected on these cases, was implantable on all but one. The output that most convincingly resembles a skull is no more placeable than the rest, and at these point densities implant thickness cannot be read off the emitted points at all, which is a property of the output representation rather than of any one model. On this evidence the shape completion foundation models we could obtain cannot be trusted and, used as released, are not usable in clinical practice. Substantial research towards trustworthy foundation models for shape completion is still needed.

</details>


### [117] [Attn-Forcing: Learning Full-Context Attention Proxies for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30036)
*Junyao Gao,Sibo Liu,Jiaxing Li,Yanan Sun,Hanxin Zhu,Weidong Zhang,Jun Zhang,Cairong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Attn-Forcing 通过可学习的注意力代理（attention proxies）在有界内存与计算下保留完整上下文的注意力贡献，并用 Attention Matching Loss 显式监督，从而提升流式视频生成质量并取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成中增大注意力上下文长度可显著提升质量，但内存与计算开销随上下文增长而不可承受。现有方法依靠注意力选择或压缩来捕获上下文信息，受限于有界表示能力，且缺乏注意力层面的显式监督，难以保留历史上下文对当前查询的贡献。

Method: 提出 Attn-Forcing 框架：引入注意力代理（attention proxies）持续近似历史上下文所诱发的注意力响应；将代理实现为可学习视觉潜变量，并用 Proxy Update Module 融合历史潜变量中的视觉上下文；同时提出 Attention Matching Loss，利用完整上下文的注意力监督显式约束学习到的代理的注意力行为。

Result: 大量定性与定量实验表明，Attn-Forcing 一致性地提升流式视频生成质量，并在现有基线方法上达到 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 以代理压缩历史注意力贡献并辅以注意力级显式监督，是在有界内存与计算约束下逼近完整上下文注意力的有效途径，可显著改善流式视频生成。

Abstract: Increasing the attention context length significantly improves streaming video generation quality, while incurring prohibitive memory and computational costs as the context grows. Existing methods attempt to capture this contextual information through attention selection or compression, but remain limited by bounded representations and lack explicit attention-level supervision to retain historical contributions to current queries. To address this, we introduce Attn-Forcing, a novel framework that preserves full-context attention contributions for streaming video generation under bounded memory and computation. We achieve this by introducing attention proxies that continuously approximate the attention responses induced by the historical context. Specifically, we implement the proxies as learnable visual latents and employ a Proxy Update Module to incorporate visual context from historical latents. Moreover, we propose an Attention Matching Loss to explicitly constrain the attention behavior of the learned proxies using full-context attention supervision. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments demonstrate that Attn-Forcing consistently improves streaming video generation quality and achieves state-of-the-art performance over existing baseline methods.

</details>


### [118] [ProactEgoMem: Benchmarking Proactive Memory in Streaming Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30089)
*Ruotong Liao,Meikel Kokowski,Xiaoxiao Sun,Yongqin Xian,Laura Bravo Sánchez,Shiye Su,Federico Tombari,Serena Yeung-Levy,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ProactEgoMem 基准，用于评估流式自我中心视频中多模态助手的"主动记忆"能力，包含信息捕获、状态跟踪、位置跟踪三类任务与两级主动性协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态助手依赖用户显式提示来解读视觉证据；可穿戴助手若要在长时程中减轻用户记忆负担，需主动获取并维护有用记忆，而这要求识别关键信息、实时理解场景并在后台发起记忆维护。现有基准缺乏对此类主动性的评估。

Method: 构建流式自我中心视频基准 ProactEgoMem：① 三类任务（Information Capture 保留瞬时密集视觉信息、State Tracking 跟踪物体状态变化、Location Tracking 记录物体移动）；② 两级主动性协议：Notification Level 要求单次及时通知满足用户意图，Intervention Level 要求通过 start-to-stop 工具生命周期主动维护记忆；③ 数据规模：Daily/Sci 两个并行赛道各 300/100 段视频片段，配 ProactEgoMem-QA 各 1000/500 个问题以评估主动获取记忆的质量。

Result: 在专有模型、开源模型、视频-语言模型及流式基线等各类模型上评测，即使最强模型也无法同时做到记忆的及时性与准确性。

Conclusion: ProactEgoMem 为主动式多模态辅助及可与用户共存的视频智能体提供了严格的评测基础，揭示了当前模型在主动记忆方面的显著不足。

Abstract: Today's multimodal assistants are typically prompted to interpret the visual evidence presented to them. To offload the cognitive burden of remembering across long horizons, wearable assistants should instead proactively acquire and maintain useful memory as the user's surroundings evolve. Doing so on the user's behalf requires identifying what matters, understanding the scene in real time, and initiating memory maintenance in the background while the user stays focused on their activities. We introduce ProactEgoMem, a benchmark that evaluates proactive memory in streaming egocentric video. Three tasks ground the evaluation in ambient environmental needs: Information Capture (IC) preserves transient yet dense visual information, State Tracking (ST) follows changing object states, and Location Tracking (LT) records object movements. The benchmark defines its protocol at two levels of proactiveness: the Notification Level asks for a single timely notification that satisfies the user's intent, while the deeper Intervention Level asks the agent to maintain memory on the user's behalf through an active start-to-stop tool lifecycle. Parallel ProactEgoMem-Daily and ProactEgoMem-Sci tracks contain 300/100 clips covering daily and scientific use cases, paired with 1000/500 questions in ProactEgoMem-QA, which measures the quality of the proactively acquired memory. Across proprietary, open-source, video-language, and streaming baselines, even the strongest models fail to capture memory both timely and accurately. ProactEgoMem provides a rigorous evaluation foundation for proactive multimodal assistance and for video agents that co-exist with their users.

</details>


### [119] [ALPS: Visual State Encoders for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30120)
*Yanqing Liu,Letian Zhang,Chengzhi Liu,Zijun Wang,Jieru Mei,Hongliang Fei,Yuyin Zhou,Cihang Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALPS：一种统一物理状态信息、语言对齐与像素重建的预训练视觉状态编码器，在单一patch token集合中兼具三类能力，并在LIBERO等下游任务上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的视觉基础表示应同时融合物理状态信息、原生语言对齐与像素重建，从而用同一表示支持预测、指令与可视检查；但现有编码器通常只擅长其中一到两项能力。

Method: 提出ALPS，仅用静态图像与文本从零预训练ViT-B/16，采用语言、重建与空间对应三类目标，再通过跨视角自蒸馏精修；与原生文本塔配对使用。

Result: 在十五个公开编码器的统一评测中，ALPS在ViT-B类模型中取得最低的综合LIBERO状态探针误差（媲美参数量大3.5倍的V-JEPA 2-L），同时保持较强语言对齐与准确像素重建；冻结token配合文本塔在LIBERO上达到85.5%的最高平均策略成功率，在PushT、Wall、Granular的世界模型规划中持平或超越领先编码器，并可把预测的未来token直接解码为像素以供检查。

Conclusion: 将视觉预训练确立为世界模型中理解、预测与动作的共享基础，验证了多目标统一表示的可行性与价值。

Abstract: World models turn visual representations into predictions and actions. We argue that their visual foundation should combine physical-state information, native language alignment, and pixel reconstruction, so that a single representation can support prediction, instruction, and inspection. However, existing encoders typically specialize in only one or two of these capabilities; we therefore introduce ALPS, a pretrained visual state encoder that unifies all three in a single set of patch tokens. Using only still images and text, we pretrain a ViT-B/16 from scratch with language, reconstruction, and spatial correspondence objectives, and then refine it via cross-view self-distillation. In a unified evaluation across fifteen public encoders, ALPS achieves the lowest aggregate LIBERO state-probe error among ViT-B models (matching the 3.5x larger V-JEPA 2-L) while maintaining strong language alignment and accurate pixel reconstruction. Downstream, frozen ALPS tokens reach the highest mean policy success on LIBERO (85.5%) when paired with our native text tower, match or exceed leading encoders on world-model planning across PushT, Wall, and Granular, and allow predicted future tokens to be decoded directly into pixels for visual inspection. We hope this work helps establish visual pretraining as a shared foundation for understanding, prediction, and action in world models.

</details>


### [120] [VoxProg: 3D Spatial Reasoning through Voxel Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30127)
*Zhichao Zhai,Menglei Zhao,Guikun Chen,Zihao Yan,Kanzhi Wu,Wenguan Wang,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoxProg 将 3D 物体放置任务形式化为“体素编程”：语言模型生成可执行程序，在场景表面的局部体素视图上构造几何引用，并通过执行反馈迭代修正程序，从而求解目标 SE(3) 位姿；同时提出 71 任务的 VoxProg-Bench 基准与配套评测套件，在多项基准上优于关系、关键点与网格类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型具备任务理解与程序生成能力，为 3D 空间推理提供基础；但在 3D 场景中遵循空间指令时，语言隐含的几何参考（如区域边界、贴合面）需要由程序显式构造。现有方法难以从指令中恢复这些隐式几何约束。

Method: 提出体素编程范式：LLM 将任务理解翻译为在局部体素视图上运行的可执行程序。体素视图以规则网格、显式坐标与邻接关系索引表面几何，使“证据程序”能从场景网格直接分离任务相关区域与边界，再由下游“位姿程序”对所得几何进行度量并计算目标 SE(3) 位姿。执行产生的视觉与数值反馈支持模型检查中间选择与候选放置，并据此迭代修改代码。评测方面构建 71 任务的 VoxProg-Bench，配套感知层面的意图对齐（Intent Alignment）与 3D 物理有效性（Physical Validity）评测套件，覆盖功能用途、几何契合与上下文布局三类开放式放置。

Result: 在 VoxProg-Bench 及外部基准上，VoxProg 一致优于基于关系、基于关键点和基于网格的基线方法。

Conclusion: 以体素为中间表征的程序化接口为 LLM 提供了显式、可度量、可回读的几何接地方式，是解决 3D 空间指令执行的有效途径；配套基准与双重指标为开放式物体放置提供了更贴近物理合理性的评测标准。

Abstract: Language models' abilities to interpret tasks and generate programs offer a promising basis for 3D spatial reasoning. Yet following spatial instructions in 3D scenes requires programs to construct geometric references that language leaves implicit. In this work, we introduce VoxProg, which formulates 3D object placement through voxel programming: a language model translates task interpretations into executable programs over local voxel views of scene surfaces. Because these views index surface geometry on regular grids with explicit coordinates and neighborhood relations, an evidence program can isolate task-relevant regions and boundaries directly from scene meshes, and a downstream pose program can measure the resulting geometry to compute a target $SE(3)$ pose. Visual and numerical feedback from execution lets the model inspect intermediate selections and candidate placements to iteratively revise its code. To evaluate open-ended placement across functional use, geometric fit, and contextual arrangements, we introduce the 71-task VoxProg-Bench benchmark along with an evaluation suite pairing perceptual Intent Alignment and 3D Physical Validity. Across VoxProg-Bench and external benchmarks, VoxProg consistently outperforms relation-based, keypoint-based, and mesh-based baselines.

</details>


### [121] [Rethinking Video Autoencoders for Interactive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30129)
*Adrien Ramanana Rahary,Yutong Bai,Nicolas Dufour,Patrick Pérez,David Picard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CADRE——开源因果扩散表征自编码器族，系统研究视频世界模型应在何处、如何以及以多大程度压缩，并给出四条实证结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在视频自编码器隐空间生成，压缩率决定每帧成本与动作生效延迟；但现有工作直接复用离线文生视频自编码器，既无智能体等待约束，又因缺少数据与代码无法在别的压缩率上重训，因此需要可控可比的压缩设计研究。

Method: 构建覆盖多种时空压缩率的CADRE族：成员仅在冻结特征上加一个小瓶颈，并共享经自蒸馏的少步扩散解码器；在操作与导航两个领域、各两个环境，用同一世界模型在每种自编码器上训练评估。

Result: CADRE占据大部分延迟-质量前沿，移动相机场景尤为明显；强压缩下扩散解码器比感知回归更锐利；机器人操作中时间压缩放在自编码器优于放在世界模型；操作与导航需要相反压缩——相机静止时时间压缩代价小，相机移动时空间压缩代价小；相机静止时、粗空间token对运动损伤远大于图像质量。

Conclusion: 世界模型的压缩应任务与环境自适应：按相机运动与操作/导航需求选择时空压缩维度，强压缩时用扩散解码，开源族为系统比较提供可控基线。

Abstract: Video world models predict future frames from past frames and actions. They serve as simulators in which agents train, plan or are evaluated, and that humans can control interactively. They generate in the latent space of a video autoencoder, whose compression sets their cost per frame and how long each action waits to take effect. Yet world models often reuse the autoencoders of text-to-video generators, built for offline generation with no agent waiting and, without their training data and code, not retrainable at another compression. We therefore ask how, where and how much a world model should compress. We build CADRE, an open family of causal diffusion representation autoencoders spanning multiple spatio-temporal compressions. Its members differ only in a small bottleneck on frozen features and share a diffusion decoder self-distilled to one or a few steps. We train the same world model on each autoencoder in two domains, manipulation and navigation, with two environments each. CADRE forms most of the latency-quality frontier, especially with a moving camera. Our study also leads to four findings. At strong compression, a diffusion decoder is sharper than perceptual regression. In robot manipulation, temporal compression is more effective in the autoencoder than in the world model. Manipulation and navigation call for opposite compressions: temporal compression costs little when the camera is still, and spatial compression when it moves. Surprisingly, with a still camera, coarser spatial tokens hurt motion far more than image quality. Code, models and a live demo are at https://kyutai.org/blog/2026-10-21-cadre

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Guess and Send: World-Model-Guided Semantic Packet Transmission for Robust Video Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29587)
*Yan Gong,Zheng Chu,Wei Koong Chai,Hari Mohan Pandey,Jiankang Zhang,Jingrui Hou,Miao Zhang,Yuanjian Li,Pei Xiao*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: WorldGT：以世界模型引导、面向接收端状态的语义分组传输框架，在带宽受限无线链路上按对接收端感知的贡献优先传输信息，显著提升低SNR下的视频感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带宽受限的无线链路下，应只传输接收端无法从其演进中的场景表示中恢复的信息；已有传输方案未按接收端感知贡献度组织数据，在低SNR和丢包条件下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 共享特征空间世界模型通过仿射运动补偿与学习到的残差位移预测接收端场景状态，发送端验证可复用预测；与ACK同步的接收端孪生体通过两次反事实评估估计任务影响分数，指导分组选择与LDPC编码信道上的非等误保护；采用紧凑3比特特征表示提升带宽约束下的空间覆盖，并以预测回退维持丢包期感知。

Result: 在KITTI与VisDrone、Rayleigh衰落与AWGN下低SNR增益显著：KITTI在4.13%参考带宽预算、0 dB Rayleigh SNR下达47.5% J&F，较H.264高11.3个百分点；消融显示世界模型预测在Rayleigh衰落中贡献最高8.7点；目标检测实验表明该方法可迁移至其他视觉感知任务。

Conclusion: 世界模型引导、接收端状态感知的传输是严重通信约束下实现鲁棒视频感知的有效途径。

Abstract: Reliable video perception over bandwidth-constrained wireless links requires transmitting information that the receiver cannot already recover from its evolving scene representation. This paper introduces WorldGT, a world-model-guided semantic packet transmission framework that prioritizes information according to its contribution to the receiver's perception. A shared feature-space world model predicts the receiver's scene state through affine motion compensation and learned residual displacement, while sender-side verification identifies reusable predictions. An acknowledgment-synchronized receiver twin estimates task-impact scores through two counterfactual evaluations, guiding packet selection and unequal error protection over LDPC-coded wireless channels. A compact 3-bit feature representation increases spatial coverage under stringent bandwidth constraints, while predictive fallback sustains perception during packet outages. Experiments on KITTI and VisDrone under Rayleigh fading and AWGN demonstrate substantial gains in low-SNR conditions. On KITTI at a 4.13% reference bandwidth budget, WorldGT achieves 47.5% J&F at 0 dB Rayleigh SNR, outperforming H.264 by 11.3 percentage points. Controlled ablations show that world-model prediction contributes up to 8.7 points under Rayleigh fading. Object-detection experiments further demonstrate the transferability of receiver-relative predictive transmission across visual perception tasks. These results establish world-model-guided, receiver-state-aware transmission as an effective approach to robust video perception under severe communication constraints.

</details>
